### [1] [DexSuite: An All-in-One Framework for Dexterous Manipulation Across Arms, Hands, and Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37799)
*Anas Houssaini,Hugo He,Junming Shi,Amin Abyaneh,Ricardo Chahine,Shuo Wen,Mohamad H. Danesh,Charles-Étienne Fréchette,Mariana Sosa Guzman,Mathias Desrochers,Rayan Houssaini,Adam Landa,Theophile Soulie,Charlotte Morissette,Annie Tims,Martino Russi,Jonathan Lussier,Gregory Dudek,Doina Precup,Julen Urain,Hsiu-Chin Lin,David Meger*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 DexSuite，一个构建于 Genesis 仿真器之上的全流程灵巧操作仿真框架，覆盖从机器人搭建、演示收集到策略评估的完整工作流，并提供 30 个任务、100+ 单臂机器人组合、标准化 API、遥操作与触觉仿真，以及 16 组任务-本体基准和 4700 条成功轨迹数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类级别的灵巧性是机器人领域的核心开放问题，但现有仿真框架很少覆盖灵巧操作的全流程：既缺少便捷的机器人配置能力，也缺少演示数据收集与策略评估的统一支持。

Method: 在 Genesis 仿真器基础上构建一体化框架 DexSuite：提供 30 个任务，将 10 种机械臂与 12 种末端执行器（从平行夹爪到灵巧手）组合成 100 余种单臂机器人数千种双臂配对；提供标准化环境 API、集成遥操作接口与仿真触觉感知；并定义 16 组覆盖不同接触复杂度和时间跨度的任务-本体基准。

Result: 发布包含 4700 条成功回合的仿真数据集，数据来源包括人类遥操作、脚本专家和学习策略；框架与数据集均开源，并提供项目主页与视频。

Conclusion: DexSuite 为灵巧操作研究提供了统一、可扩展的仿真基础设施与基准，作者开源并期望社区在此基础上继续构建。

Abstract: Human-level dexterity remains a central open problem in robotics, yet few simulation frameworks cover the full workflow of dexterous manipulation, from setting up a robot to collecting dexterous demonstrations and evaluating policies. We present DexSuite, an all-in-one simulation framework for dexterous manipulation built on the Genesis simulator. DexSuite provides 30 tasks and assembles its 10 arms and 12 end-effectors, from parallel-jaw grippers to dexterous hands, into more than 100 single-arm robots and thousands of bimanual pairs. It also ships a standardized environment API, integrated teleoperation, and simulated tactile sensing. Moreover, we define a benchmark of 16 task-embodiment pairs spanning diverse contact complexities and horizons, and release a dataset of 4,700 successful episodes in simulation from human teleoperation, scripted experts, and learned policies. We release DexSuite as open source and hope others will build on it. Videos and datasets are available at https://dexsuiteorg.github.io

</details>


### [2] [An Empirical Study of Wrist-Target Construction and Joint-Angle Limits in Humanoid Arm Retargeting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37801)
*MinJun Chang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文将"期望腕部位置的构造方式"与"姿态匹配偏好"视为两个独立的运动重定向决策：在Unitree H1-2上比较"重建目标"（由演示臂方向与机器人连杆长度构造）与"可达距离归一化目标"（保持方向、重新缩放距离），并用关节角限位触达率、跟踪误差和实际力矩来评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 运动重定向把人类演示动作映射为机器人关节运动，但很少区分"目标构造"与"姿态匹配"这两个环节各自的影响。作者关注：期望腕部位置如何构造，是否会单独导致计算出的臂关节角撞上允许限位，从而影响可执行性与力矩负荷。

Method: 在Unitree H1-2上，用演示的臂段方向与机器人连杆长度重建目标腕位（其肩–腕距离随演示臂段夹角变化，即使不施加姿态匹配偏好亦然）；对照方案为"可达距离归一化"目标：保留方向、重新缩放距离，使用同一批处理后的演示与同一关节角求解方法。评测包括前伸扫描（20次重复）、单一被试25段记录（含砌砖与举升任务，并对第二被试重复该比较）、以及硬件回放（16条方向匹配臂记录 vs. 16条归一化记录），统计关节角触限的计数，并比较触限帧的跟踪误差与执行力矩同该次运行其他帧的差异。

Result: 前伸扫描中，重建目标在20次重复里有10次使某关节角落在边界上，归一化目标为0次；25段记录中，对应计数在伸臂侧为8 vs. 6，在支撑臂侧为14 vs. 5。硬件回放中，16条方向匹配臂记录有12条出现关节被指令至边界，而归一化记录16条中为0；在符合条件的记录中，这些触限帧的跟踪误差与实际力矩均高于同一次运行其他帧。跨身伸取与臂–臂间隙则揭示了径向调整的局限。

Conclusion: 结果表明，目标构造与姿态匹配应被视为两个分开的重定向决策，并应与操作任务所需的腕位和间隙一并评估；仅靠方向匹配构造目标会显著提高关节触限风险与力矩负荷，而保持方向的可达距离归一化可大幅降低此类情形，但其径向调整在跨身伸取和臂间避碰方面仍有局限。

Abstract: Motion retargeting maps a demonstrated human movement onto a robot to compute its joint motion. This study examines how the construction of the desired wrist position affects whether computed arm joint angles reach their permitted limits on a Unitree H1-2. A reconstructed target is formed from demonstrated arm directions and robot segment lengths, so its shoulder-to-wrist distance depends on the demonstrated angle between the segments even when no posture-matching preference is applied. A reach-normalized target retains that direction while rescaling the distance, using the same processed demonstrations and the same joint-angle method. Across a forward-reach sweep the reconstructed target places a joint angle at a bound in 10 of 20 repetitions and the reach-normalized target in none. Across 25 recordings from one participant the corresponding counts are 8 and 6 on the reaching arm and 14 and 5 on the supporting arm, and a second participant repeats the bricklaying and lifting comparison. In hardware replay a joint is commanded to a bound in 12 of 16 direction-matching arm-records and in none of the 16 normalized records, and within the eligible records both tracking error and executed torque at those frames exceed their values elsewhere in the same run. Cross-body reach and arm-to-arm clearance identify limits of radial adjustment. The findings support treating target construction and posture matching as separate retargeting decisions, and evaluating both alongside the wrist positions and clearance that manipulation requires.

</details>


### [3] [TACOS: Terrain-Aware COntact Supervision for Visual Footstep Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37806)
*Jean-Michel Fortin,William Larrivée-Hardy,Gabriel Jeanson,Olivier Gamache,Daniele De Martini,François Pomerleau,Philippe Giguère*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出TACOS方法，利用腿部状态估计中因子图残差构造足步失稳评分，并将其投影到前置图像上以自监督方式训练稠密视觉预测器，在多项评估中显著优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类依靠视觉提前预判地面反应再决定落脚，自监督学习有望赋予足式机器人同样能力；但现有自监督信号多凭直觉选取，其对模型学习内容的影响很少被系统考察，因此需要一个有意义且一致、可量化比较的监督信号。

Method: 提出基于足式机器人状态估计中因子图残差的足步失稳评分，将其投影到对应前置图像区域，以监督稠密视觉预测器；给出两种变体：利用事后信息的硬评分，以及可即时获得的因果软评分。并在两套互不共享数据与标签的独立评测中与SOTA方案对比，另有室外实机与50米森林轨迹实验。

Result: 在1716个手工标注接触上，两种变体在失稳检测与严重程度排序上均优于所有对比方法，综合严重度排序比最强基线提升28%；在三条室外测试序列上训练出的视觉预测器精度最高，秩相关比最强替代方案提升58%，加入机载上下文信息后再提升25%；50米森林实机实验中，预测器能处理泥地、植被等非几何地形属性，同时绕开倒木等几何障碍。

Conclusion: 监督信号的设计本身是足式机器人自监督视觉地形感知的关键，TACOS提供的评分一致、有意义且可复现，可训练出兼顾非几何地形属性与几何障碍的视觉预测器，代码已开源。

Abstract: Humans navigate their environment based on their vision system, anticipating how the ground will respond before committing to a step. Self-supervised learning could provide legged robots the same ability, but the supervisory signal is typically chosen on intuition, and its effect on what the model learns is rarely examined. We present Terrain-Aware COntact Supervision (TACOS), which proposes a meaningful and consistent footstep instability score, based on the residual of a factor graph in legged state estimation. It projects the continuous score into preceding images to supervise a dense visual predictor. We propose two variants, a hard score that exploits hindsight and a causal soft score available immediately. Both metrics are compared against state-of-the-art alternatives in two independent evaluations that share no data or labels. On 1716 manually annotated contacts, both variants outperform every evaluated alternative at detecting instability and ranking its severity, improving pooled severity ranking by 28 % over the strongest baseline. On three outdoor test runs, our metric also trains the most accurate visual predictor, improving rank correlation by 58 % over the strongest alternative, and by a further 25 % when supplemented with onboard context. In a real-world experiment over a 50 m forest trajectory, the trained predictor handles non-geometric terrain properties such as mud and vegetation, while stepping around geometric obstacles like fallen branches. Code is available for reproducibility at https://github.com/norlab-ulaval/TACOS.

</details>


### [4] [Echo: Scaling Visual Reinforcement Learning with Off-policy Experience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37865)
*Gagan Khandate*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出单阶段离策略视觉强化学习框架 Echo，用"逆蒸馏"让状态辅助策略模仿视觉策略并低成本采集无渲染经验，配合共享特权评论家直接改进视觉策略，避免两阶段蒸馏的模仿鸿沟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPU 加速仿真下的视觉 RL 计算开销大（渲染与视觉策略推理昂贵），常用两阶段教师-学生蒸馏来训练视觉策略，但会引入模仿鸿沟。作者希望在不按比例增加视觉计算的前提下扩展经验采集。

Method: Echo 为单阶段 off-policy 框架：通过 inverse distillation，让一个基于状态的辅助策略模仿不断演化的视觉策略，并采集廉价的无渲染经验；这些经验用于 off-policy 价值学习，并通过共享的特权评论家直接改进视觉策略。实例化为 Echo + FastTD3，在 Genesis 仿真中评测三个视觉控制任务与两个灵巧手任务。

Result: 相比 SDPG 基线，在三个运动任务上达到参考回报的时间平均减少 60%，两个手部任务减少 56%；峰值平均回报分别提升 62% 与 12%；两个手部任务在相同仿真器转移预算下平均回报也更高。

Conclusion: 初步结果表明，扩展 off-policy 经验是实现高效直接视觉 RL 的有前景方向。该工作为初步研究，仍需更广泛验证。

Abstract: Scaling visual reinforcement learning (RL) with GPU-accelerated simulation is computationally expensive due to rendering and visual-policy inference costs. Consequently, two-stage teacher--student distillation is commonly used to train visual policies, but introduces an imitation gap. In this preliminary study, we introduce \echomethod{}, \textbf{a single-stage off-policy RL framework that scales experience collection without proportionally increasing visual computation}. Through \textbf{inverse distillation}, a state-based auxiliary policy imitates the evolving visual policy and collects inexpensive, unrendered experience. Echo leverages this experience for off-policy value learning, directly improving the visual policy through a shared privileged critic. We instantiate \echomethod{} with FastTD3 and evaluate it on three visual control tasks and two dexterous manipulation tasks in Genesis. Relative to \sdpgmethod{} under the evaluated configurations, Echo reduces time to reference returns by an average of \textbf{60\% across the three locomotion tasks} and \textbf{56\% across both hand tasks}, while improving peak mean returns by \textbf{62\% and 12\%}, respectively. Both hand tasks also achieve higher mean returns at matched simulator-transition budgets. These initial results suggest that scaling off-policy experience is a promising approach to efficient direct visual RL.

</details>


### [5] [When More Planning Hurts: Learned Planners for World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37891)
*Josef Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文在PushT基准上比较固定参数动作更新器与已有规划器，并考察多次更新对成功率的影响。结果显示，该更新器在16个场景中只解决8个，且未达预设的任务成本标准；增加更新次数反而使部分原本成功的任务失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望用参数量极小的固定动作更新器（1,505参数）替代或对比现有规划器，在相同配置挑选流程与任务成本标准下进行评估，并检验“更多更新步骤能否挽回失败任务”这一直观假设。

Method: 在PushT的16个场景（8个场景簇）上进行受控对比实验：一方面比较固定动作更新器与Selected Adam、adapted iCEM、released LePlanner的成功任务数和规划加编码总耗时；另一方面做匹配实验，每个上下文只回放前十条指令，随后分别给4、8、12次更新预算（此后统一为8次），并记录成功/失败变化以及所选取的潜世界模型轨迹成本。

Result: 固定动作更新器解决8/16个场景，总耗时59.079s，但未满足预先固定的任务成本标准；Selected Adam解决7个（218.440s），adapted iCEM解决2个（49.309s），released LePlanner解决16个（5.652s）。4/8/12次更新预算分别取得9、8、7次成功，额外更新未挽回任何失败任务，且所有成功/失败变化都发生在同一场景簇中，表现为所选潜世界模型轨迹成本下降但原本成功的任务转为失败。

Conclusion: 参数量极小且耗时中等的固定动作更新器在成功率上仅略优于Selected Adam，但未达到预设的任务成本标准；增加更新预算不仅没有带来收益，反而在特定场景簇中导致成功率下降，说明更新次数与轨迹成本之间存在与任务成功不对齐的问题。代码、权重、评测器、执行轨迹与成本核算均随论文公开。

Abstract: On PushT, our fixed action updater (1,505 parameters) solves 8 of 16 scenes reserved from our configuration selection. The comparison planners perform as follows: Selected Adam (7 tasks solved, total planning and encoding time 218.440s), adapted iCEM (2 tasks solved, total planning and encoding time 49.309s), released LePlanner (16 tasks solved, total planning and encoding time 5.652s). The total planning and encoding time for our action updater is 59.079s. Note that our action updater does not satisfy the task-cost criterion we fixed before evaluation. We also conduct another matched experiment where we only replay the first ten commands in each context and then allow the next solve 4, 8 or 12 updates (subsequent solves all get 8 updates). Among all 16 contexts (8 scene clusters), the 3 budgets get 9, 8 and 7 successes, respectively. Interestingly, the extra updates did not rescue any failing task in this experiment. All the changes in success/failure happen in one scene cluster, where we observe that the extra updates decrease the costs of the selected latent world-model trajectories for our updater, while the tasks that used to be successes become failures. The code, weights, evaluators, execution traces and cost accounting accompany the paper.

</details>


### [6] [What Actually Matters? A Paired Evaluation of Inference Settings for $π_{0.5}$ on BEHAVIOR-1K](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37917)
*Ruben Hammele,Samira Huber,Mohammad Nazeri,Amirreza Payandeh,Alexandyr T. Card,Aaron Nguyen,Anuj Pokhrel,Daeun Song,Xuesu Xiao,Sören Pirk*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文在 2025 BEHAVIOR Challenge 全部 100 个任务上，系统评估了已有 VLA 推理与数据采集策略的迁移性，发现这些策略在不同环境与任务间表现不一致，无法均匀泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 模型被视为通用家用机器人有前景的范式，但此前在小规模场景中验证有效的推理与数据采集策略，未必能推广到大规模、多样化的任务套件，这一空白尚缺乏系统性证据。

Method: 基于 2025 BEHAVIOR Challenge 冠军方案微调 π_{0.5} 模型，在全部 100 个任务上进行配对比较评估，逐一检验已有推理策略与数据采集策略的效果。

Result: 微调后的 π_{0.5} 模型在公开测试集上取得 0.39 的 Q-score；评估表明所提出的各类策略在新环境与新任务上并不能一致地迁移或生效。

Conclusion: 小规模设定下的经验难以直接外推至大规模任务套件，VLA 的推理与数据采集策略需针对任务多样性重新设计与验证。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models offer a promising paradigm for general-purpose household robotics. However, inference and data-collection strategies that have been deployed in smaller-scale settings do not necessarily generalize to large-scale, diverse task suites. To address this gap, we conduct a systematic, paired evaluation of previously proposed inference and data-collection strategies across all 100 tasks. Our evaluation uses a fine-tuned $π_{0.5}$ model based on the 2025 BEHAVIOR Challenge winner, which achieves a Q-score of 0.39 on the public test set. These results show that proposed strategies do not transfer uniformly to new environments and tasks. Project page, videos, and detailed analysis: https://rubenhammele.github.io/behavior2026

</details>


### [7] [3D Occupancy-Language Fields from mmWave Radar](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37929)
*Haowen Lai,Zhiwei Zheng,Mingmin Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TextRadar 从全景雷达观测构建场景级 3D 占据-语言场，支持用开放词汇自然语言查询重建场景，并通过雷达估计位姿在仅用雷达的情况下完成新场景建场。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 雷达能恢复场景几何并识别预定义类别，但缺乏可用于开放式语言查询的持久空间表示；此类查询需要将不同视角的观测关联到同一物理位置。

Method: 构建结合表面占据与尺度相关语言特征的场景级 3D 占据-语言场；用单帧雷达蒸馏模型从每次雷达观测预测对齐的距离与语言特征；利用雷达估计位姿配准这些预测，仅凭雷达重建该场。

Result: 在七栋建筑约 2 万次同步观测上，实现场景级自然语言查询与无提示语义可视化；相机到雷达的概念迁移分析显示，结构、材质、人体姿态的一致性高于颜色与印刷文字。

Conclusion: 雷达可独立支撑开放词汇的场景级语义建图与查询，但概念迁移在纹理类属性上仍受限。

Abstract: Radar systems can recover scene geometry and recognize predefined categories, but these outputs do not provide a persistent spatial representation for open-ended language queries. Such queries require linking observations from different viewpoints to the same physical locations. We present TextRadar, a framework that constructs a scene-level 3D occupancy-language field from panoramic radar observations. The field combines occupancy with scale-dependent language features at surface locations, allowing the reconstructed scene to be queried with new descriptions. A single-frame radar distillation model predicts aligned ranges and language features from each radar observation. Using radar-estimated poses, we register these predictions to reconstruct the field from radar alone in a new scene. Experiments on approximately 20k synchronized observations across seven buildings demonstrate scene-level natural-language querying and prompt-free semantic visualization. Analysis of camera-to-radar concept transferability shows stronger agreement for structures, materials, and human poses than for colors and printed text.

</details>


### [8] [FORGE: Feasible Online Reduced-Order Reference Generation and Whole-Body Execution for Agile Humanoid Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37974)
*Adrian B. Ghansah,Yujin An,Jeeseop Kim,Soon-Jo Chung*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出一个两阶段学习框架，将在线降阶模型（ROM）参考生成与全身跟踪控制耦合，实现通用且敏捷的人形机器人运动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法难以在单一统一框架下覆盖大范围指令空间和多种运动策略；通过在简化的ROM空间学习运动学-动力学一致的参考生成器，可在广指令空间内生成参考轨迹。

Method: 第一阶段：结合单刚体动力学与完整人形运动学，训练以指令为条件的全身参考生成器；第二阶段：在线滚动冻结的ROM策略生成参考，在全阶动力学模型上训练全身跟踪策略。

Result: 在Unitree G1人形机器人上验证：纵向速度-2.0至2.8 m/s，横向速度±1.0 m/s，角速度±3.0 rad/s，单腿跳跃，以及指令间连续切换，仅使用一个统一参考策略和一个全身跟踪策略。

Conclusion: 耦合ROM参考生成与全身跟踪的两阶段框架能够以单一统一策略实现大范围、多策略、平滑切换的人形机器人敏捷运动。

Abstract: This paper presents a two-stage learning framework for versatile and agile humanoid locomotion that couples online reduced-order model (ROM) reference generation with whole-body tracking control. In the first stage, we train a command-conditioned whole-body reference generator using single rigid body dynamics combined with full humanoid kinematics. In the second stage, the frozen ROM policy is rolled out online to generate references for training a whole-body tracking policy on the full-order dynamics model. Learning a kino-dynamically consistent reference generator in the simplified ROM space enables us to generate references over a broad command space and over multiple locomotion strategies using a single unified framework. We demonstrate the approach on a Unitree G1 humanoid robot, achieving longitudinal velocities from -2.0 m/s to 2.8 m/s, lateral velocities up to +-1.0 m/s, angular velocities up to +-3.0 rad/s, one-legged hopping, and continuous transitions across the commands, all using one unified reference policy and one whole-body tracking policy.

</details>


### [9] [Active Constraints for Simultaneous Guidance and Safety in Handheld Micromanipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37981)
*Roger Ho,Robert A. MacLachlan,Cameron N. Riviere*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种用于手持式微型操作机器人的基于优化的主动约束框架，可同时实现任务引导与安全保障，并在医学相关任务中验证了显著的安全性与精度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在显微操作（如显微外科）中，人手的误差运动限制了裸手操作精度，影响任务表现与安全性。手持式机器人（微型操作器）能以低成本、紧凑、直观的形式提供超越人手极限的运动精度。主动约束是有效的辅助方式，可依据任务需求限制机器人允许的运动，从而提供引导并保障安全。但现有手持系统的主动约束形式有限，主要缺陷是难以同时提供引导与安全保障；而基于优化的实现方式虽能解决该问题，却针对独立式机器人设计，忽略了手持机器人是相对操作者手部驱动且工作空间受限这一特点。

Method: 提出面向手持式微型操作的基于优化的主动约束框架，将任务引导与安全保障统一在同一优化问题中，并考虑手持机器人相对操作者手部驱动、工作空间受限的特性；将该公式化方法在手持机器人上实现，并在一个具有医学相关性的仿真/人工任务上进行演示与评估。

Result: 与基线方法相比，所提公式化方法将安全违规率降低两个数量级以上，同时弥补了仅实施安全保障所带来的约三倍精度损失。

Conclusion: 基于优化的主动约束框架能够在手持式微型操作中同时实现任务引导与安全保障，既大幅降低安全违规，又恢复精度，验证了其相较现有手持系统主动约束方法的优势。

Abstract: In micromanipulation, erroneous hand motion limits unaided manipulation accuracy, which can affect task performance and safety in critical applications like microsurgery. Handheld robots (micromanipulators) enable motion accuracy beyond human limits by providing robotic assistance in a low-cost, compact, intuitive form factor. Particularly, active constraints are an effective form of robotic assistance, which aids the operator by restricting the robot's permissible motion based on task-specific requirements or objectives, thereby providing task guidance and enforcing safety. However, existing formulations of active constraints for handheld systems are limited, most notably because of the inability to provide guidance and enforce safety simultaneously. Implementing active constraints with optimization-based methods can address these limitations, but existing implementations are designed for free-standing robots, and neglect that handheld robots actuate relative to the operator's hand with a limited workspace. In this work, we propose an optimization-based active constraint framework for handheld micromanipulation that enables simultaneous guidance and safety enforcement. The formulation was implemented in a handheld robot and demonstrated on a medically relevant artificial task. Results show that the proposed formulation reduced safety violation rate by more than two orders of magnitude relative to baselines, while recovering the threefold accuracy loss from enforcing safety alone.

</details>


### [10] [Self-Labeled Preference Transfer for Steerable Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38000)
*Kaiwen Hong,Shaoting Peng,Xiangyu Zeng,Pengcheng Wang,Boyuan Tian,Haonan Chen,Yifan Zhu,Yixiao Wang,Katherine Driggs-Campbell*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Self-Labeled Preference Transfer（SPT），通过在新任务演示上自标注偏好，实现无需人工偏好标注的跨任务偏好控制迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA策略可从带偏好标签的演示中学习执行偏好（如抓取位置、绕障方式），但为每个新任务标注偏好代价高昂；作者希望研究偏好控制能否迁移到有行为演示但缺少偏好标注的新任务。作者观察到：带偏好标注训练的策略在新任务上无需适配即可跟随偏好但任务成功率有限；而在无标注目标演示上微调虽提升成功率却削弱偏好控制。

Method: SPT在带标注的源演示上联合训练共享视觉-语言骨干，同时进行偏好条件动作预测与偏好识别；随后冻结识别器以保留其跨任务偏好识别能力，并用其对目标演示进行自标注，用于策略微调。

Result: 仿真中五类执行偏好下识别器准确率达96.4%；SPT偏好跟随84.8%、任务成功89.2%，而朴素微调为59.4%和88.0%。多偏好场景下SPT达93.8%偏好跟随，基线为42.5%。真实世界两个任务迁移中，偏好跟随由52.5%提升至92.5%。

Conclusion: SPT可在无额外人工偏好标注的情况下，实现跨任务、跨物体的偏好适配，同时保持任务成功率。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) policies can learn to follow execution preferences, such as where to grasp an object or how to move around an obstacle, from demonstrations annotated with preference labels. Providing these annotations for every new task, however, is costly. We study whether learned preference control can transfer to new tasks whose demonstrations contain the desired behaviors but lack preference annotations. We find that a policy trained with preference annotations can follow preferences on a new task before adaptation but achieves limited task success. Fine-tuning on target demonstrations without these annotations improves task success while weakening preference control. Motivated by this finding, we introduce Self-Labeled Preference Transfer (SPT). SPT jointly trains a shared vision-language backbone for preference-conditioned action prediction and preference recognition on annotated source demonstrations. It then freezes the recognizer to preserve its cross-task preference recognition capability and uses it to self-label target demonstrations for policy fine-tuning. Across five types of execution preferences in simulation, the recognizer achieves 96.4% accuracy, and SPT reaches 84.8% preference following and 89.2% task success, compared with 59.4% and 88.0% for naive finetuning. We further extend to multiple preferences, achieving 93.8% preference following compared with 42.5% of baselines. In real-world experiments, SPT increases preference following from 52.5% to 92.5% relative to naive finetuning across two task transfers. These results show that SPT can support preference adaptation across tasks and objects while maintaining success, without additional manual preference annotations.

</details>


### [11] [METATRON: A Promptable Whole-Body Execution Layer for Humanoids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38027)
*Chen Tessler,Yifeng Jiang,Alec Otto,Yi Shi,Umar Iqbal,Gal Chechik,Xue Bin Peng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Metatron：以可提示（promptable）的全身执行层取代任务专用训练，冻结的生成式基座策略接收“身体部位目标+速度+风格”组成的提示生成全身运动，上层用普通代码编写的有状态技能（skills）表达任务意图，并由编码智能体通过分析运动数据自动拟合并精炼技能规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人控制器虽普遍共享平衡与全身协调的需求，却往往仍需针对每个任务单独训练；作者希望构建一个任务无关的通用执行层，使新任务可以通过代码（技能程序）而非再训练来获得。

Method: 方法分两层：①生成式基座策略，输入为部分目标（统称 prompt），可组合带时序的身体部位目标、速度指令与风格线索，输出多样且一致的全身动作；训练数据混合动捕数据、合成的恢复轨迹以及重定向后的场景交互动作，训练后策略被冻结。②可编程技能层：有状态程序在每个控制步把任务意图写成 prompt，相当于绘制粗略几何草图，由策略转化为协调运动；技能是普通代码，由编码智能体分析运动数据、拟合紧凑规则加以改进，无需任务特定策略训练。

Result: 在实体 Unitree G1 上，单一冻结检查点支持行走、恢复、遥操作物体抓取以及坐家具等任务；对比评估显示其到达成功率与抗推扰鲁棒性优于所测试的参考轨迹跟踪流水线。同一套方案在仿真角色上还产生了攀爬与特技动作。

Conclusion: Metatron 使编码智能体能够把已学得的运动能力通过代码转化为新技能，而无需进一步训练策略，从而实现“训练一次、以代码扩展任务”的通用人形全身执行层。

Abstract: Humanoid controllers often require task-specific training despite sharing the need for balance and whole-body coordination. We present Metatron, a promptable whole-body execution layer combining a generative base policy with a programmable skill layer. The base policy receives partial goals, collectively called a prompt, that can combine timed body-part targets, velocity commands, and style cues. It produces diverse whole-body motions consistent with these goals. Training combines motion capture, synthetic recovery trajectories, and retargeted scene-interaction motions. After training, the policy is frozen. Stateful programs called skills express task intent as prompts at each control step, drawing rough geometric sketches that the policy turns into coordinated movement. Skills are ordinary code, refined by coding agents that analyze motion data and fit compact rules without task-specific policy training. On the physical Unitree G1, one frozen checkpoint supports walking, recovery, teleoperated object pickup, and sitting on furniture. Comparative evaluations show higher reaching success and push robustness than the tested reference-tracking pipelines. The same recipe produces climbing and acrobatics on a simulated character. Metatron enables coding agents to turn learned motor capabilities into new skills through code, without further policy training.

</details>


### [12] [EARS-Info: Information-Aware Event-Driven Reallocation for Multi-Robot Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38040)
*Kabeh Mohsenzadegan,Vahid Tavakkoli,Kyandoghere Kyamakya*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出并验证EARS-Info多机器人探索控制器，在168,000回合、八城市地图实验中取得最高平均运行得分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向OpenStreetMap栅格地图的多机器人探索，需要在通信丢包条件下兼顾感知效率、可行重定位、返航安全与能耗，已有方法（如EARS-Safe）在加权发现率与能耗上仍有提升空间。

Method: EARS-Info是事件驱动蚁群重分配搜索的信息感知扩展，融合边际感知增益、可行重定位、返航感知路由、感知预留与缓存最短路；在八张城市地图上对比30种实现，共168,000回合，并以城市自助法分析得分差异。

Result: 在所有评估面板中平均运行得分最高；8机器人、3%通信丢包下较EARS-Safe得分提升5.43%，加权发现率由88.88%升至98.46%，平均能耗降低2.62%；八城市得分差均为正（p=0.0078125，95%城市自助区间[0.0159,0.0694]），5,600个EARS-Info回合无模拟事故。

Conclusion: 结果支持完整控制器的整体有效性，但各单独组件优势仍不确定；泛化性受地理复用、单一训练重复、地图粗糙及对比方法经适配等因素限制。

Abstract: We introduce EARS-Info, an information-aware extension of Event-Driven Ant Reallocation Search for multi-robot exploration on OpenStreetMap-derived grids. It combines marginal sensing gain, feasible relocation, return-aware routing, sensing reservations, and cached shortest paths. Across 168,000 episodes comparing 30 implementations on eight city maps, EARS-Info achieves the highest mean operational score in every evaluation panel. With eight robots and 3% communication dropout, it improves the score over EARS-Safe by 5.43%, increases weighted discovery from 88.88% to 98.46%, and reduces mean energy use by 2.62%. Score differences are positive in all eight cities ($p=0.0078125$; descriptive 95% city-bootstrap interval $[0.0159,0.0694]$), with no simulated incidents across 5,600 EARS-Info episodes. These results support the complete controller; individual component advantages remain uncertain. Generalization is limited by reused geography, one training replicate, coarse maps, and adapted comparison methods.

</details>


### [13] [Efficacy of Monocular Depth Estimation in Lunar Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38080)
*Michael W. Lanighan,Kenji Brameld,Seth Gee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究评估多种先进单目深度估计方法在分布外月球环境中的表现，利用NASA Ames POLAR立体数据集与Apollo 17表面影像，构建从模拟场地到真实场地的验证流程，结果显示部分方法可泛化生成高保真相对三维重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单目深度估计虽在分布内的陆地场景中能实现高精度三维重建，但其在分布外月球环境中的有效性尚不明确，需要系统性检验。

Method: 采用NASA Ames极地光学月球模拟重建（POLAR）立体数据集与Apollo 17表面影像，评估若干最先进的单目深度估计方法；建立模拟—现场（analog-to-field）验证流程，结合真值物理模拟做定量评估，并结合历史现场数据做定性评估。

Result: 定量与定性评估表明，部分最先进方法能够成功泛化，对非结构化月球表面生成高保真的相对三维重建；另一些方法则表现受限。

Conclusion: 单目深度估计在分布外月球环境中具备可行性，所选方法可实现可靠相对重建，所提出的模拟—现场验证流程为地外场景重建方法的评估提供了可复用范式。

Abstract: Recent advances in monocular depth estimation have demonstrated highly accurate 3D reconstructions within in-distribution terrestrial domains. This study investigates the efficacy of several state-of-the-art approaches to reconstruct out-of-distribution lunar environments using the NASA Ames Polar Optical Lunar Analog Reconstruction (POLAR) stereo dataset and Apollo 17 surface imagery. By leveraging these datasets, we establish a robust analog-to-field validation pipeline that evaluates quantitative performance against ground-truth physical analogs and qualitative performance against historical field data. Our results indicate that select state-of-the-art techniques can successfully generalize to produce high-fidelity relative 3D reconstructions of the unstructured lunar surface.

</details>


### [14] [Same Label, Different Interface: Beyond Action-Space Labels in Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38104)
*Arshia Sangwan,Zhongyang Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 在共享学习器与训练预算下，本文系统拆解"位置/速度动作接口"标签背后的执行通路（滤波、物理缩放、参考构造、反馈、时序与力矩限幅），并通过缩放校准对比证明接口差异不能仅凭标签归因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人学习文献常把位置动作与速度动作当作两种单一实验处理来比较，但标签本身并不决定机器人实际执行什么；有界策略输出在真正驱动运动前会经过滤波、物理缩放、参考构造、反馈、时序和力矩限制等被忽略的环节，导致对比结果的归因可疑。

Method: 在同一学习器与相同训练预算下，针对三个接触丰富的 Factory 任务（PegInsert、GearMesh、NutThread）比较五种关节空间接口，每条件独立训练 10 个策略。第一阶段固定位置反馈律，只改变被执行接口（单独改动物理动作缩放、去除指数滑动平均）；第二阶段跨反馈律比较，选取与位置接口局部原始力矩指令增益精确匹配的速度缩放做校准。

Result: 仅替换物理动作缩放即令样本中位成功率在各任务变化 0.645–1.000；移除 EMA 的影响依任务而变。力矩增益精确匹配的速度接口校准在 GearMesh 与 NutThread 上提升了样本中位数，PegInsert 中位数无变化；但 PegInsert 与 GearMesh 仍存在明显的样本中位差距，NutThread 的区间包含零。

Conclusion: 即便显式匹配原始力矩增益，反馈与保持动作动力学仍未对齐，无法据此建立位置—速度接口的训练结果等价性；动作接口对比应报告实际执行的完整通路，并明确说明所匹配的物理量。

Abstract: Robot-learning studies often compare position and velocity actions as though each label identified a single experimental treatment, yet the label alone does not determine what the robot executes. Before affecting robot motion, bounded policy output passes through filtering, physical scaling, reference construction, feedback, timing, and effort limits. We study how these omitted properties affect attribution under one shared learner and training budget. Across three contact-rich Factory tasks (PegInsert, GearMesh, and NutThread), we compare five joint-space interfaces, with ten independently trained policies per condition. We first remain within the same position-feedback law and vary the executed interface: substituting only the physical action scale as the directly modified component changes sample-median evaluation success by 0.645-1.000 across tasks, while removing an exponential moving average produces task-dependent changes. We then move to a cross-law comparison and choose a velocity scale that exactly matches the position interface's local raw-torque command gain. This calibration raises the sample median on GearMesh and NutThread, with no median change on PegInsert. Yet clear residual sample-median gaps remain on PegInsert and GearMesh, while the NutThread interval includes zero. Thus, even this explicit equality leaves feedback and held-action dynamics unmatched and does not establish position-velocity trained-outcome equivalence. Action-interface comparisons should therefore report the executed pathway and state the physical quantity they match.

</details>


### [15] [Minimum-Time Escape from the Conic Field of View of a Reeds--Shepp Pursuer: the Faster Evader](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38125)
*Ubaldo Ruiz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文完整求解了全向逃逸者在Reeds–Shepp追捕者前向锥形视场中、速度比ν>1、半角0<α<π/2时的最小时间逃逸博弈，给出逃逸时间的显式刻画与状态空间中的博弈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前工作只处理了较慢逃逸者的情形，较快速逃逸者（ν>1）下的最优逃逸时间与策略结构尚缺完整解析解，需对所有速度比与视场半角统一求解。

Method: 用特征线方法刻画逃逸时间：由标量方程根的选取确定取值；边界特征为半径ν的圆渐开线，止于尖点；用两个显式阈值判断其是否在尖点前到达轴线、以及跨特征跳跃是否在尖点前结束；追捕者采用较慢情形下的中继（relay）策略，并借助水平集与半拉格朗日数值计算进行验证比较。

Result: 逃逸时间被表示为非预期策略下的博弈值并适用于所有状态；在逃逸者扩散线端点E上方，逃逸者沿由支流汇聚的通用直线追逐折返的追捕者；当跳跃延伸至尖点时，主特征会在障碍附近折返；数值计算与解析值吻合。

Conclusion: 该研究给出了ν>1时该追逃博弈的完整闭式/半闭式解，揭示了渐开线特征与阈值分岔结构，并验证了追捕者的中继最优策略在快速逃逸者情形下依然成立。

Abstract: We continue the study of minimum-time escape of an omnidirectional evader from the forward conic field of view of a Reeds--Shepp pursuer, now for a faster evader, and solve the game for every speed ratio $ν>1$ and every half-angle $0<α<π/2$: the escape time is characterized by explicit characteristics and the selection of roots of a scalar equation, and it is the value of the game in nonanticipative strategies at every state. Above the end $E$ of the evader's dispersal line on the front axis, the evader chases the reversing pursuer along a universal line fed by tributaries. The characteristic from the boundary of the usable part is an involute of a circle of radius $ν$ ending at a cusp, and two explicit thresholds decide whether it reaches the axis before the cusp and whether the jump of the escape time across it ends before the cusp or runs to it; in the latter case primaries fold back next to the barrier. The pursuer's optimal strategy is the relay of the slower case. The evader follows the tributaries and switches once to a primary, or commits in advance to a heading certified by the scalar equation of the primaries. Level-set and semi-Lagrangian computations are compared with the value.

</details>


### [16] [SKiD-SLAM: Resource-Aware Distributed Multi-Robot LiDAR SLAM for Planetary Missions with Field Deployments at Analog Sites](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38141)
*Hogyun Kim,Juwon Kim,Dongjin Cho,Jiwon Choi,Geonmo Yang,Seokhwan Jeong,Jiyun Lee,Miryeong Park,Hyungtae Lim,Younggun Cho*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出SKiD-SLAM——一种面向行星探测场景的分布式多机器人LiDAR SLAM系统，并在韩国济州岛熔岩管洞穴与涟川行星模拟地形的实地部署中验证了其在250 kbps通信带宽约束下的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未来行星探测任务需机器人在严重通信受限、机载算力有限且无中心基础设施的环境中协同建图，现有SLAM系统难以满足真实任务的通信与计算预算，因此需要针对实际预算进行模块选型与系统集成验证。

Method: 调研真实行星任务的通信与计算预算，据此选择适配的机器人间位置识别、相对位姿估计与分布式轨迹融合模块，组装成分布式多机器人LiDAR SLAM系统；部署前在类行星环境的公开数据集及基于NASA JPL开源漫游车搭建的仿真器中验证，随后开展两次实地部署：轮式漫游车探索济州岛Majang熔岩管洞穴、四足机器人于KICT涟川行星模拟地形执行坑洞与岩石科学场景。

Result: 两次部署中，SKiD-SLAM均在250 kbps机器人间通信预算内稳定完成分布式多机器人LiDAR SLAM，展现出在严苛野外条件下的可行性，并公开了代码与实地数据。

Conclusion: SKiD-SLAM证明经任务预算驱动的模块选型可支撑真实行星探测场景下的分布式多机器人SLAM，为后续行星协同探测提供可复现的系统与数据基础。

Abstract: Future planetary exploration missions require teams of robots to collaboratively map unknown environments under severe communication limits, constrained onboard computation, and no central infrastructure. In this field report, we present SKiD-SLAM, a distributed multi-robot LiDAR simultaneous localization and mapping (SLAM) system for such environments, and report its deployment across two mission concepts: cave exploration and scientific surface exploration. We survey the communication and computation budgets of actual planetary missions and select modules that fit these budgets for inter-robot place recognition, relative pose estimation, and distributed trajectory fusion. Before field deployment, we verify the assembled system on public datasets resembling planetary environments and in a simulator built on NASA JPL's Open Source Rover. We then validate SKiD-SLAM through field deployments in South Korea: a team of wheeled rovers explores the Majang lava-tube cave on Jeju Island, and quadruped robots carry out a crater-and-rock science scenario on a planetary analog terrain at the Korea Institute of Civil Engineering and Building Technology (KICT) in Yeoncheon. Across both deployments, SKiD-SLAM reliably performed distributed multi-robot LiDAR SLAM within a 250 kbps inter-robot communication budget, demonstrating its feasibility under challenging field conditions. Supplementary materials, including code and field data, are available at https://sparolab.github.io/research/skid_slam/.

</details>


### [17] [Minimum Sufficient Communication for Independent Behaviour Tree Robot Teams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38176)
*Sherry Jiang,Oliver Obst*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出一个实验框架，用于在具有私有黑板、运行固定独立行为树的机器人团队中确定充分通信所需的最低程度；实验发现低竞争工况下零通信即为精确最小值，缩放工况下特定门控策略被确认充分，但正的最小值仍未确定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人团队运行固定且独立的行为树、黑板私有，此时通信量可以削减到什么程度尚缺可判定的方法；需要在给定策略族内，以统计上可辩护的方式判断某通信策略是否"足够"（成功的下降不超过全状态通信基准5个百分点）。

Method: 定义策略族，变化消息内容与准入频率，以"每机器人决策的接收者加权比特数"度量流量；先做密集扫描筛选候选策略，再在新的配对场景上重测候选与全状态参照，用单侧同时置信界判断成功是否保持在参照5个百分点内，同时判断更省流量的策略能否被排除为不充分；据证据输出四种结论之一：精确最小值、正区间、充分上界、未解决边界。

Result: 在四种仓库条件下测试八种内容策略：低竞争条件下零通信为精确最小值；缩放条件下，门控 q=0.992222 的无释放策略被确认充分，成功率96.33%，估计速率738.55比特/机器人决策，仅为全状态流量的0.544%；正的最小值仍未被解决。

Conclusion: 所提供的框架能在声明范围内给出可对证的"充分通信"判定，并显式区分已确认最小值、区间与未解决边界；结论严格限于所声明的策略族、采样门控值与运行条件。

Abstract: We present an experimental framework for determining sufficient communication in robot teams running fixed, independent Behaviour Trees with private blackboards. Within a declared policy family, we vary message content and admission frequency and measure traffic in recipient-weighted bits per robot decision. A dense sweep identifies candidate policies for comparison with full-state communication. We retest selected candidates and the reference on fresh, paired scenarios, using one-sided simultaneous confidence bounds to assess whether success remains within five percentage points of the full-state reference. The bounds also assess whether tested cheaper policies can be ruled out as insufficient. Depending on the evidence, the framework reports an exact minimum, a positive bracket, a sufficient upper bound, or an unresolved boundary. Experiments with eight content policies across four warehouse conditions identify zero communication as the exact minimum under low contention. In the scaled condition, the no-release policy at gate $q=0.992222$ is confirmed sufficient, achieving 96.33% success at an estimated rate of 738.55 bits per robot decision, or 0.544% of full-state traffic. The positive minimum remains unresolved. These claims apply only to the declared policy family, sampled gate values and operating conditions.

</details>


### [18] [Embodied at Sea: Imitation Learning for Multi-Task Underwater Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38199)
*Greta Perez-Haiek,Luca Macesanu,Kevin Angers,Mohammed Imam,Timothy Gambin,Christopher Clark*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文研究水下机器人操作中的行为克隆泛化问题：采集1616条水下操作数据训练14个通用VLA模型（DynamicVLA），在泳池1260次硬件rollout中10个模型超越基线、最佳达93.3%总体成功率；另在真实海洋考古场景（马耳他二战沉船与码头）对比单任务ACT与SmolVLA，ACT以47.9%对7.9%显著领先，并开源BLUEMBODIED KIT工具链。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 陆地环境中行为克隆与通用机器人策略已展现良好泛化能力，但水下视觉畸变与动态水流造成严重的域偏移，使通用架构难以迁移到真实水下现场操作，这一瓶颈尚未被系统研究。

Method: 在平静与湍流水域采集1616条水下操作数据，以不同本体感知观测配置训练14个通用视觉-语言-动作模型（DynamicVLA）；在泳池中对3个任务进行1260次可评分硬件rollout评估。另开展案例研究，在ROV上对比单任务ACT与通用VLA（SmolVLA）在海洋考古与保护（抓取、系泊）两任务上的表现，试验地点为马耳他二战沉船Coralita X127周边及Manoel Island Marina，共采集748条episodes、508次可评分rollout。

Result: 泳池实验中14个模型有10个优于基线，最佳模型总体成功率达93.3%（含部分成功）。海洋考古案例中，ACT以47.9%的合并成功率显著优于SmolVLA的7.9%，并在考古站点最成功任务上达到66.7%。同时开源了用于数据采集、模型训练与ROV策略部署的BLUEMBODIED KIT。

Conclusion: 通用VLA模型经合适本体感知配置与数据训练后可在水下操作中取得高成功率，但在真实海洋现场任务上，单任务专用策略（ACT）仍明显优于通用VLA；作者开源全套工具链以推动海洋现场机器人具身智能研究。

Abstract: Behavior Cloning has demonstrated promising generalization capabilities for robotic manipulation in terrestrial environments. However, marine visual distortions and dynamic currents create a severe domain shift that remains a key bottleneck in adapting generalist architectures to underwater field manipulation. To address this, 1616 episodes of underwater manipulation data were collected in calm and turbulent waters and were used to train 14 generalist Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models (DynamicVLA) with varying proprioception observation configurations. Across three tasks on 1260 gradable on-hardware rollouts in a pool, 10 models outperformed the baseline, with the highest-performing model achieving a 93.3% overall success rate, including partial successes. Results are also introduced from a case study evaluating the performance of a single-task Action Chunking Transformer (ACT) and a generalist VLA (SmolVLA) policy for two tasks in marine archaeology and conservation: grasping and mooring. Experiments are performed on an ROV on and around the WWII wreck Coralita X127 and at Manoel Island Marina, Malta. Across 748 collected episodes and 508 gradable on-hardware rollouts, the ACT policy substantially outperformed SmolVLA (47.9% vs. 7.9% pooled success rate), earning a 66.7% success rate on the most successful task at the marine archaeological site. To enable further contributions to embodied intelligence in marine field robotics, the BLUEMBODIED KIT used to collect data, train models, and deploy policies on the ROV has been open sourced.

</details>


### [19] [CuraEval: Evaluating Demonstration Curation on Mixed-Skill Robot Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38221)
*Jiuyi Xu,Meida Chen,Yang Ye,Yangming Shi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CuraEval：将示范分数与固定等量随机子集、全量数据训练进行受控对比，发现PSD分数退化为长度的近似代理，长度选择的小幅增益在严格评估协议下不稳健，无任何策展条件显著优于全量训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模仿学习数据来自技能水平不一的示范者，现有选样方法用平滑度、多样性或对策略性能的影响来打分，但选择性协议与评估协议不一致，导致难以判断这些方法是否真的优于简单基线。

Method: 提出CuraEval框架，对示范分数做数学分析，并在混合技能的robomimic数据上用Diffusion Policy与BC-RNN进行受控比较：与固定等量随机子集、全量数据训练对比，并联合改变checkpoint汇报方式、rollout次数与评估种子。

Result: PSD分数仅取决于回合长度与位置幅值的平方，与长度排序几乎一致（square上ρ=0.999）。DP下取最短50%示范使square成功率由随机选择70.7%升至76.5%；几何熵LOO选择器在25%保留率下把can由94.7%降至74.0%，square为41.7%，低于三个随机子集的59.8–62.5%。长度选择在75%保留率下按原协议由76.7%升至86.7%，但联合改变评估设置后由80.5%降至79.5%。Holm校正后无策展条件显著优于全量训练。

Conclusion: 现有示范选择方法的增益大多来自与简单基线（长度、随机子集）比较时的不公平评估设置；在严格等量随机与稳健评估协议下，策展数据的优势不显著，而置信区间仍不排除存在有用增益，提示需标准化选样与评估协议。

Abstract: Robot imitation learning uses demonstrations from operators of varying skill, but selecting training data remains challenging. Existing methods score demonstrations using smoothness, diversity, or estimated influence on policy performance, but differences in selection and evaluation protocols make it difficult to determine whether they outperform simple baselines. We introduce \textbf{CuraEval}, a framework combining mathematical analysis of demonstration scores with controlled comparisons against fixed size-matched random subsets and full-data training on mixed-skill robomimic data using Diffusion Policy and BC-RNN. We show that the evaluated PSD score depends only on episode length and squared position magnitudes, and produces nearly the same ranking as episode length on \textit{square} ($ρ=0.999$). With Diffusion Policy, selecting the shortest 50\% of demonstrations improves \textit{square} success from 70.7\% under random selection to 76.5\%. Compared with random selection, our adapted geometric-entropy LOO selector reduces success at 25\% retention from 94.7\% to 74.0\% on \textit{can} and achieves 41.7\% on \textit{square}, below the mean success rates of all three independently sampled random subsets (59.8--62.5\%). On the same 3 \textit{square} Diffusion Policy training seeds, length-based selection at 75\% retention improves success over full-data training from 76.7\% to 86.7\% under the original protocol, but lowers it from 80.5\% to 79.5\% after checkpoint reporting, rollout count, and evaluation seed change jointly. No evaluated curated condition significantly outperforms full-data training after Holm correction, although confidence intervals still permit useful gains. Code is available at https://github.com/jiuyixu25/CuraEval.

</details>


### [20] [HERON: Real-Time Obstacle-Aware Reachability Maps for Mobile Manipulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38233)
*G. Dupoiron,F. Michaud,J. P. Roberge*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出HERON——基于3D U-Net的神经网络，从实时深度相机预测2 cm分辨率的障碍物感知可达性图，在Jetson AGX Orin上以约115 ms（8.57 Hz）完成2.56 m立方体范围的预测，接近cuRobo真值精度（Pearson 0.99）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动操作机器人通常不在操作过程中移动基座，因为需要实时知道手臂在障碍物环境中对周围空间的可达程度。现有可达性图（RM）方法速度慢、分辨率粗（通常5 cm）或忽略真实障碍物，难以支撑在线使用。

Method: 构建3D U-Net（HERON），以cuRobo的GPU加速逆运动学计算出的RM为监督标签，先在同成场景训练，再用真实深度数据训练；部署于Jetson AGX Orin，使用TensorRT与INT8量化，从单目深度相机直接预测手臂周围2.56 m立方体的障碍物感知RM，分辨率2 cm。

Result: 平均预测耗时115 ms，实测系统频率8.57 Hz，接近障碍物图10 Hz的更新率，而用cuRobo计算相同地图约需90 min。相对cuRobo真值，FP32模型95%的体素误差在51类中的1类以内，INT8模型在3类以内；Pearson相关系数0.99，二次加权kappa高于0.9。精度下降主要发生在工作空间边界处。

Conclusion: 神经网络可在嵌入式硬件上以在线速率、高分辨率地预测障碍物感知的可达性图，足以支持移动操作机器人在操作中实时重定位基座，但工作空间边界处仍需改进。

Abstract: Mobile manipulator robots rarely reposition their base during manipulation, because doing so requires real-time knowledge of how well the arm can reach its surroundings among obstacles. Reachability maps (RMs) encode this knowledge, but existing methods are slow, coarse (typically 5 cm), or ignore real obstacles. This article asks whether a neural network can predict an obstacle-aware RM at 2 cm resolution from a live depth camera, on embedded hardware, fast enough for online use. We present HERON, a 3D U-Net trained on RMs computed with the GPU-accelerated inverse kinematics of cuRobo, first on synthetic scenes and then on real depth data, and deployed on a Jetson AGX Orin with TensorRT and INT8 quantization. HERON predicts the RM of a 2.56 m cube around the arm in 115 ms on average, for a measured system rate of 8.57 Hz, close to the 10 Hz update rate of the obstacle map, whereas computing the same map with cuRobo takes about 90 min. Against the cuRobo ground truth, 95% of the voxels lie within 1 class out of 51 with the FP32 model and within 3 classes with the INT8 model, with a Pearson correlation of 0.99 and a quadratic weighted kappa above 0.9. Accuracy degrades mainly at the workspace boundaries.

</details>


### [21] [SimRetract: Zero-Shot Sim-to-Real Robotic Assistance for Retractor Take-Over in Open Surgery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38234)
*Xucheng Wang,Zhizhou Yang,Sung Eun Kim,Romain Hardy,Michael P. Sampson,Aakash Reddy,Ankit Pal,Xiaoman Zhang,Luyang Luo,Pranav Rajpurkar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SimRetract 首次实现开放手术中医师-机器人协作任务（牵开器接管）的零样本 sim-to-real 迁移：仅用仿真示教训练策略，机器人在 50 次呈现中抓取成功率 90%、交接时保持牵开状态 78%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实示教的模仿学习受采集与标注成本高、环境变化下性能退化所限，因此需要一种无需人类示教、也无需真机微调即可迁移的手术辅助策略。

Method: 提出 SimRetract 框架，生成面向迁移设计的仿真示教：策略输入为掩码后的器械图像与抓取几何，学习从偏离标称状态中恢复，并以留有余量的方式动作以抵抗执行漂移；随后零样本部署到真机。研究还通过 10 种策略配置、1200 次仿真与真实试验对 sim-to-real 差距做组件级定位（消融）。

Result: 零样本部署下抓取成功率 90%（50 次），交接保持牵开 78%；对未见牵开器表现出向相似抓取几何器械泛化的潜力。仿真中各类消融的抓取率为 80–96%，而真机上仅去掉器械掩码即骤降至抓取 22%、保持 12%，从而将差距定位到具体组件。

Conclusion: 面向迁移的设计使无需人类示教的手术辅助任务零样本 sim-to-real 迁移成为可行；器械掩码等设计对真机性能至关重要。

Abstract: We demonstrate the first zero-shot sim-to-real transfer for a surgeon-robot collaboration task in open surgery: retractor take-over. A robot arm takes a retractor from the surgeon's hand and preserves retraction at handoff, using a policy trained only on simulated demonstrations, without human demonstrations or real-world fine-tuning. Imitation learning from real demonstrations is limited by collection and labeling costs, and by performance degradation when the environment changes. Our framework, SimRetract, generates simulated demonstrations designed for transfer: the policy observes masked instrument images and grasp geometry, learns to recover from off-nominal states, and acts with margin for execution drift. Deployed zero-shot, it grasps the retractor in 90% of 50 surgeon-defined phantom presentations and preserves retraction at handoff in 78%. Results on unseen retractors suggest potential for generalization to instruments with similar grasp geometry. Across ten policy configurations and 1,200 simulated and real trials, we localize the sim-to-real gap to components: in simulation, grasp rates across ablations span 80-96%, while on the real robot removing the instrument mask alone drops performance to 22% grasped and 12% held. Transfer-oriented design thus makes zero-shot sim-to-real transfer feasible for a surgical assistance task without human demonstrations. Code is available here: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/simretract-58D2.

</details>


### [22] [MATCH: Execution-Aware Hierarchical Reinforcement Learning for Vision-and-Language Navigation in Continuous Environment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38248)
*Shuhao Ye,Yufei Wei,Demao Wang,Shichao Zhai,Rong Xiong,Yue Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MATCH框架联合学习语言条件规划器与目标条件底层动作执行器，在VLN-CE任务上以0.5B参数达到R2R-CE和RxR-CE的最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分层方法多只优化规划器而固定执行模块，导致规划请求与执行能力不匹配，在连续环境的碰撞约束下难以兼顾指令一致性与可靠执行。

Method: 构建执行感知拓扑图表示依赖起点的执行选项，融合执行器价值估计与已实现结果记忆；规划器借此选择执行请求并通过有界连续偏移细化目标；设计转移感知的密集宏奖励，平衡沿参考路线有序推进与执行残差；采用分阶段课程，最终同步进行on-policy更新，执行器在规划器生成的请求上学习，规划器适应执行器当前行为。

Result: 以0.5B参数规模在R2R-CE与RxR-CE基准上取得state-of-the-art性能，初步的真机部署实验也展示了实际应用潜力。

Conclusion: 联合学习规划与执行、并对二者行为进行同步对齐，是提升连续环境下视觉语言导航可执行性与指令跟随能力的有效途径，且该方案在参数效率与真实部署上具备可行性。

Abstract: Vision-and-Language Navigation in Continuous Environments (VLN-CE) requires instruction-consistent planning and reliable execution under collision constraints. Yet many hierarchical approaches optimize the planner while keeping execution fixed. We introduce MATCH, a framework that jointly learns a language-conditioned planner and a goal-conditioned primitive-action executor. An execution-aware topological graph represents start-dependent execution options and integrates executor value estimates with realized outcome memory, enabling the planner to select execution requests and refine their targets through bounded continuous offsets. A transition-aware dense macro reward balances ordered progress along the reference route against execution residual, encouraging executable requests without substituting local success for instruction following. A staged curriculum culminates in synchronized on-policy updates, with the executor learning on planner-generated requests and the planner adapting its requests to the executor's current behavior. With 0.5B parameters, MATCH achieves state-of-the-art performance on the R2R-CE and RxR-CE benchmarks. Preliminary real-robot deployments further demonstrate its practical potential.

</details>


### [23] [Recursive Self-Improvement in Physical AI: Mechanisms, Evidence, and Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38266)
*Xiaofeng Han,Kaize Luo,Zenghuang Fu,Zichong Zhu,Zhe Feng,Changwei Wang,Rongtao Xu,Weiliang Meng,Xiaopeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文从"持久能力"与"能力改进过程"的联系出发，对物理AI（具身系统）中的递归自我改进（RSI）研究进行综述，提出学习者更新与改进器的功能区分、L0-L3分级量规，以及共同数据与交互式对比评估方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身系统可从部署经验中更新策略、世界模型、技能与可执行程序，使一次交互惠及后续任务；当系统还能修订并复用其数据获取、反馈或学习流程时，经验便改变了产生能力的流程，形成递归自我改进。但现有方法（策略学习、想象经验、记忆与技能累积、可执行harness等）分散，缺乏统一的评估框架来判断增益能否在持续部署中真正累积。

Method: 1）阐明学习者更新（learner updates）与改进器（improvers）的功能区别；2）比较策略学习、想象经验、记忆与技能累积、可执行harness等范式，并分析其与课程、奖励、研究计划的修订关系；3）以接触有效性、恢复、保持与资源成本作为增益累积的判断标准；4）提出L0-L3量规（任务局部适应、持久能力增益、可继承的流程修订、经验证的改进器增益）；5）结合代表性基准与同一研究内的对照实验；6）提出共同数据与交互式对比，从共享学习者初始状态评估参考改进器与修订后改进器，分离经验的使用与获取。

Result: 给出了物理AI中递归自我改进的概念框架与分级评估量规，通过代表性基准和同一研究内的对照将各项区分与既有结果对应起来，并提出可分离"经验使用"与"经验获取"的对比实验设计。

Conclusion: 该视角将多样方法、物理运行条件与评估证据联系起来，为具身递归自我改进（embodied RSI）提供统一理解框架；并公开综述文献列表（https://github.com/nkd-lkz/physical-ai-rsi-survey）供后续研究使用。

Abstract: Embodied systems can update policies, world models, skills, and executable programs from deployment experience, allowing one interaction to benefit later tasks. When a system also revises and reuses its data-acquisition, feedback, or learning procedures, experience changes the process that produces capability, introducing recursive self-improvement. This paper surveys research in physical AI through the connection between persistent capability and its improvement process. We first explain the functional distinction between learner updates and improvers, then compare policy learning, imagined experience, memory and skill accumulation, and executable harnesses. These approaches connect to revisions of curricula, rewards, and research programs in different ways. Contact validity, recovery, retention, and resource costs determine whether the resulting gains accumulate during continued deployment. An L0-L3 rubric distinguishes task-local adaptation, persistent capability gain, inherited process revision, and verified improver gain. Representative benchmarks and within-study contrasts relate these distinctions to existing results. We further propose common-data and interactive comparisons that evaluate reference and revised improvers from shared learner starts, separating the use of experience from its acquisition. This perspective connects the diverse methods, physical operating conditions, and evaluation evidence, underpinning embodied RSI. A comprehensive list of studies in this survey is available at https://github.com/nkd-lkz/physical-ai-rsi-survey.

</details>


### [24] [Compositional Diffusion Planning with Chunk Selection and Reachability Measure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38291)
*Chanyoung Park,Youngjoong Kim,Jaesik Park*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出选择性组合扩散规划器SCD，用自监督可达性分类器构建chunk图并寻路，缓解模式平均导致的不连续问题，在OGBench上取得SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组合式扩散规划（采样短轨迹chunk再拼接为长轨迹）在长时程任务上表现优异，但重叠chunk的平均会引发mode-averaging问题，导致轨迹不连续；现有方法虽尝试解决，仍有相当比例的计划不可行。

Method: 1) 提出基于可达性度量的选择性组合扩散规划器SCD；2) 设计自监督的可达性分类器，在离线数据与生成的chunk上联合训练，以缓解分布不匹配并减少生成数据带来的偏差；3) 以边代价表示相邻chunk是否可达构建图，并在图中搜索高效路径；4) 生成桥接轨迹并细化组合轨迹，实现平滑过渡。

Result: 在OGBench拼接任务上，相比所研究的基线方法，实际减少了不兼容的模式组合与模式平均问题；跨任务成功率提升，在HumanoidMaze-Large与Giant任务上达到SOTA。

Conclusion: 以可达性为准则进行chunk选择与图搜索，可有效抑制模式平均与不可行组合，结合桥接与轨迹细化提升长时程扩散规划的可行性与成功率。

Abstract: Compositional diffusion planners have demonstrated remarkable success on long-horizon diffusion planning by sampling short trajectories, namely chunks, and composing them into a long trajectory. However, averaging overlapping chunks can induce the mode-averaging problem, resulting in discontinuous trajectories. Several works aim to address this problem, whereas a non-negligible portion of plans remains infeasible. In this work, we propose Selective Compositional Diffuser (SCD), handling this problem by measuring reachability. To this end, we present a self-supervised learning method for the reachability classifier. This is trained on offline data and generated chunks jointly to address potential distributional mismatch, while reducing the bias inherited from the generated data. Based on this, we construct a graph whose edge costs represent whether consecutive chunks are reachable, and traverse the graph to find effective yet efficient routes. Lastly, we generate bridging trajectories and refine the composed trajectory for smooth transitions. We evaluate our method on OGBench stitching tasks. It practically reduces incompatible mode compositions and the mode-averaging problem compared to the studied baselines. Across tasks, SCD improves success rates, reaching state-of-the-art results on HumanoidMaze-Large and Giant tasks.

</details>


### [25] [What Should a Navigator Remember? Spatiotemporal Memory for Zero-Shot Vision-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38318)
*Yuhang Peng,Zhikai Wang,Ziyi Wang,Jiaru Zhong,Xiaoji Zheng,Jingzhi Huang,Yi Wang,Jiangtao Gong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文研究8B规模零样本视觉语言导航系统中应保留哪些信息以及如何组织，提出结合任务、时间、空间三类互补记忆的导航智能体（8B观察者+8B推理者+几何控制器），在R2R-CE val-unseen上优于所评估的Qwen3类零样本基线，并在Unitree Go2实机上验证了迁移效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零样本VLN要求智能体在无导航专项训练的情况下利用历史观测。核心问题在于：对于中等规模（8B）导航系统，究竟应保留什么信息、以何种结构组织，才能有效支撑后续决策。作者从语义信息视角出发，强调"保留证据、使证据对推理者可访问、获取新观测"三者之间的区分。

Method: 构建三模块智能体：①指令进度（任务记忆）；②锚定到位姿的分层压缩观测历史（时间记忆）；③累积几何（空间记忆/地图）。8B观察者负责总结观测，8B推理者基于三类记忆选择路点意图或请求额外视角，几何控制器执行选定目标。另以LAW2成对指令干预作为行为响应评估。

Result: R2R-CE val-unseen上完整系统在所评估的Qwen3类零样本基线中SR最高；移除时间记忆导致SR下降且平均路径长度从16.9m骤增至39.1m；移除空间地图与规划组件也降低SR。Unitree Go2实机在走廊与办公环境中成功率高于后训练基线。LAW2干预实验显示其在端点成功之外还具备指令跟随行为响应。

Conclusion: 结果表明，将任务进度、观测历史与几何信息保留在结构化记忆中，可使较小的开源模型实现有效的零样本导航，且具备较好的真实环境迁移与指令跟随能力。

Abstract: Zero-shot vision-and-language navigation (VLN) requires an agent to use past observations without navigation-specific training. We study what information an 8B-scale navigation system should retain and how it should be organized for subsequent decisions. Our agent combines instruction progress, hierarchically compressed observation history anchored to poses, and accumulated geometry in complementary task, temporal, and spatial memories. An 8B observer summarizes observations, and an 8B reasoner uses the memories to select waypoint intents or request additional views, a geometric controller executes the selected targets. A semantic information perspective motivates the distinction between retaining evidence, making it accessible to the reasoner, and acquiring further observations. On R2R-CE val-unseen, the complete system achieves the highest among the evaluated Qwen3-based zero-shot baselines. Removing temporal memory reduces SR and sharply increases average path length from 16.9 to 39.1m, removing the spatial-map and planning component reduces SR as well. Real-world experiments on a Unitree Go2 show that our zero-shot method achieves higher success rates than the post-trained baseline in both corridor and office environments, suggesting better transfer to the evaluated real-world settings. As a complementary evaluation, LAW2 uses paired instruction interventions to examine behavioral responses, instruction following ability beyond endpoint success. These results support preserving task progress, observation history, and geometry in structured memory for zero-shot navigation with smaller open models.

</details>


### [26] [Proprioceptive Finger Force Estimation for Reliable Box Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38351)
*Junha Cha,Chanwoo Kim,Kisang Park,Yeoncheol Jang,Doyoung Kim,Seungmin Kim,Kyungjae Lee,Sungjoon Choi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 FEEL 方法，利用本体感知历史估计逐指外力大小，并结合不确定性进行连续力自适应，从而提升抓取放置的可靠性与无损率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可靠的箱子抓取放置需要既保持足够接触力又不产生过载损伤；作者假设逐指力反馈相比开环手指指令能提升操作可靠性，但部署时难以加装专用接触传感器。

Method: 提出 FEEL（Finger External-Force Estimation from Proprioceptive SignaLs），一种不确定性感知的残差方法：将标定后的虚拟弹簧估计与由混合密度网络（MDN）预测的残差分布相结合，从本体感知历史估计逐指外力大小，部署时无需专用接触传感器；估计力用于逐指连续自适应，预测不确定性作为本体感知力估计歧义的经验信号。

Result: 在十指、0–20 N 接触力范围内，平均 RMSE 为 0.251 N，相比虚拟弹簧降低 92.8% 误差，相比端到端 MDN 降低 9.4%；在箱子抓取放置任务中，力自适应保持任务成功率 10/10，并将无损结果与可靠性从 7/10 提升至 10/10。

Conclusion: 逐指力反馈与基于不确定性感知的连续力自适应可显著提升抓取放置的可靠性和无损率，且无需额外接触传感器即可部署。

Abstract: Reliable box manipulation requires successful placement without damage, which depends on maintaining sufficient contact force without excessive loading. We hypothesize that per-finger force feedback improves manipulation reliability relative to open-loop finger commands. To this end, we present Finger External-Force Estimation from Proprioceptive SignaLs (FEEL), an uncertainty-aware residual method that estimates per-finger external force magnitude from proprioceptive histories. Our method combines a calibrated virtual-spring estimate with a residual distribution predicted by a Mixture Density Network (MDN) and requires no dedicated contact sensors during deployment. The estimated force is used for continuous per-finger adaptation, while predictive uncertainty is evaluated as an empirical signal of ambiguity in proprioceptive force estimation. Across ten fingers and contact forces spanning 0-20 N, our method achieves a mean RMSE of 0.251 N, reducing the error by 92.8% relative to the virtual spring and by 9.4% relative to an end-to-end MDN. In box pick-and-place, force adaptation maintains task success at 10/10 and increases both damage-free outcomes and reliability from 7/10 to 10/10. The project page is available at https://finger-force-estimation.pages.dev/

</details>


### [27] [CSO: Refining Robotic Policies via Skill Distribution Alignment and Skill-Grained Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38357)
*Zhiyuan Xiang,Xiang Deng,Wanjie Tao,Xu Chen,Zhao Jielun,Weili Guan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出两阶段后训练框架 Cascaded Skills Optimization (CSO)：先用拒绝采样监督微调对齐观测到技能的映射，再冻结观测编码器、以每个完整技能为单位计算裁剪的重要性比率做策略优化，从而缓解 VQ-VAE 类技能先验的量化误差问题。轻量 QueST 风格策略将 LIBERO 成功率从 80.1% 提升至 97.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将连续动作离散化为离散技能（如 VQ-VAE）已成为机器人操作的重要范式，但量化误差导致技能先验分布次优、性能下降。直接用强化学习做精炼又面临两个问题：编码器更新不稳定，以及重要性采样中的粒度困境（以技能为粒度还是以底层动作为粒度难以取舍）。

Method: 两阶段后训练框架：(1) Rejection-Sampling Supervised Fine-tuning，通过拒绝采样筛选成功在线轨迹，用监督微调使模型的“观测→技能”映射与之对齐；(2) Skills Policy Optimization，冻结观测编码器，为每个完整技能计算独立的裁剪重要性比率，实现稳定高效更新。

Result: 在轻量 QueST 风格策略上，LIBERO 总体成功率由 80.1% 提升至 97.5%；在 RoboTwin2.0 上性能提升；五个真实单臂任务平均成功率达 61.0%。与 FAST、离散 BEAST 及固定缓冲区 IQL 的对比进一步验证了该两阶段后训练策略的有效性。

Conclusion: 分阶段解耦“分布对齐”与“策略精炼”，并以技能为粒度做裁剪重要性采样，是修复离散技能先验的有效途径，且无需改动骨干策略即可获得显著增益，具备良好的可插拔性与迁移性。

Abstract: Discretizing continuous actions into discrete skills using methods like VQ-VAE has emerged as a powerful paradigm for robotic manipulation. However, quantization errors yield a suboptimal distribution for the skill prior, degrading its performance. While reinforcement learning offers a path for refinement, its direct application faces unstable encoder updates and a granularity dilemma in importance sampling. To address these challenges, we introduce Cascaded Skills Optimization (CSO), a two-stage post-training framework. First, Rejection-Sampling Supervised Fine-tuning aligns the model's observation-to-skill mapping with the distribution of successful online trajectories via supervised fine-tuning. Second, with the observation encoder frozen, Skills Policy Optimization computes an independent, clipped importance ratio for each complete skill, enabling stable and efficient updates. With a lightweight QueST-style policy, CSO raises overall LIBERO success from 80.1% to 97.5%, improves performance on RoboTwin2.0, and achieves 61.0% success across five physical single-arm tasks. Additional comparisons with FAST, discrete BEAST, and fixed-buffer IQL further validate the effectiveness of our two-stage post-training strategy.

</details>


### [28] [ELEVATE: Agentic Controller Acquisition from Task Failure for Quadrupedal Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38388)
*Shiqi Liu,Radu Corcodel,Ding Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 ELEVATE 框架，利用任务程序精化中的持续失败来识别并自主习得缺失的四足全身控制能力，构建可复用控制器库，在仿真任务中达近满分成功率，并零样本迁移到真实四足机械臂。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四足机械臂可通过全身交互扩展工作空间，但任务规划智能体的能力受限于其既有的控制器库；当任务超出预训练控制器覆盖范围时，现有方法无法完成。因此需要一种机制，让智能体能识别并自行补足缺失的全身控制能力。

Method: ELEVATE 以任务程序精化过程中反复出现的失败为信号，判断是否缺少某种物理能力；若判定为缺失，则由编码智能体构建并迭代精化强化学习问题，指定行为专属奖励、课程学习与终止条件；训练并通过验证的控制器被写入能力库，与已有控制器组合用于重试原任务。

Result: 从单一预训练 loco-manipulation 控制器出发，在七个仿真任务序列中自主获得墙面支撑抬升、支撑式操作、墙面下降等控制器，并在后续抓取与物体操作任务中复用；在超出初始控制器工作空间的五个任务上，五次独立运行平均成功率为 98.4%–100%，而控制器库固定的编码智能体基线均失败。所合成的任务程序与习得控制器无需真机训练或微调即迁移至实体四足机械臂，货架取物 18/20、地面到货架放置 16/20 成功。

Conclusion: 结果表明，基于失败驱动的能力自主获取与库化复用，可有效突破任务规划智能体受限于既有控制器的瓶颈，并具备从仿真到真实四足机械臂的零样本迁移能力。

Abstract: Quadrupedal manipulators can extend their workspace through whole-body interaction, but task-planning agents' capabilities remain limited by their available controllers. We present ELEVATE, a framework that uses persistent failures during task-program refinement to identify and acquire missing whole-body control capabilities. When these failures suggest a missing physical capability, a coding agent formulates and iteratively refines a reinforcement-learning problem, specifying behavior-specific rewards, curricula, and termination conditions. Validated controllers are added to the capability library and composed with existing controllers to retry the task. Starting from a single pretrained loco-manipulation controller, ELEVATE autonomously acquires controllers for wall-supported elevation, supported manipulation, and wall descent across a sequence of seven simulation tasks. These controllers are reused in subsequent reaching and object-manipulation tasks. On the five tasks beyond the initial controller's workspace, ELEVATE achieves mean success rates of 98.4%-100% across five independent runs, while coding-agent baselines with a fixed controller library fail to complete these tasks. The synthesized task programs, together with the acquired controllers, also transfer to a physical quadrupedal manipulator without real-robot training or fine-tuning, achieving 18/20 successes in rack retrieval and 16/20 in floor-to-rack placement. Videos and demonstrations are available at https://agentic-quad-elevate.github.io/public/.

</details>


### [29] [When "No Worries" Went Wrong: HRI Lessons for Foundation-Model Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38400)
*Eliahu Khalastchi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文基于一个为期三年的 ROS 2 人机交互联合项目经验，提出五条设计启示（注意力、知识、承诺、具身时序与行动权限），指出现有基础模型虽提升感知与推理能力，但不足以保证情境变化真正影响最终触达人的行动；为此提出轻量级审查方法 TRACE，通过替换单一交互前提并追踪至决策或执行边界，输出可落地的架构发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型虽赋予机器人更强的环境感知、情境推理与行为生成能力，但能力本身不能保证变化的情境会塑造最终到达人的行动：证据可能从未被获取，目的或约束可能在规划与执行中丢失，系统甚至在行动已不再服务其原目的时仍报告“成功”。论文由此提出要从工程实践中提炼可复用的人机交互架构教训。

Method: 以三年 ROS 2 人机交互联合项目（集成了随技术演进而出现的大语言模型与视觉模型）的工程经验为基础，通过文中机器人案例（如拒绝邀请后的时序、在访客身旁向主人报告访客到达）进行现象归纳，提出五条设计启示：注意力、知识、承诺、具身时序与行动权限；并在此基础上设计 TRACE 方法——一种轻量级审查，改变一个交互前提，沿其追踪到决策或执行边界，从而产出可操作的架构发现；同时对已发表的 LLM 生成行为树系统进行有界分析，说明可用的澄清机制可能被闲置。

Result: 归纳出五条设计启示；TRACE 能够以单一前提变更的追踪方式产出可落地的架构发现；对已发表 LLM 生成行为树系统的有界分析显示，系统中存在澄清机制却未被使用，说明“能力可用”不等于“能力被触发”，暴露了从感知证据到行动执行之间的断层。

Conclusion: 工程上的关键教训不在于保留曾经暴露问题的计算流水线，而在于保留责任与可问责性的边界；系统设计应确保情境证据能贯穿到行动及其后果，避免在目的失效时误报成功。

Abstract: When a person declined our robot's invitation to play, Alice turned her shoulder away and said "No worries, it's all good." Only after a brief pause did she drive off. The words and gestures were plausible but their order changed the message. In another episode, Alice informed a host that his visitor had arrived---while the visitor stood right beside him. Both came from a three-year ROS 2 HRI consortium that integrated large language and vision models as they emerged. Today's foundation models give robots broader capabilities to perceive their surroundings, reason about situations and generate behavior. Yet capability alone does not ensure that a changed situation shapes the action that reaches a person. Evidence may never be acquired, or a purpose or constraint may fail to survive planning and execution. A system may even report Success when an action no longer serves its intended purpose. Drawing on the consortium's engineering experience, we develop five design lessons about attention, knowledge, commitments, embodied timing and action authority. We then introduce TRACE, a lightweight review that changes one interaction premise, follows it to a decision or enforcement boundary, and produces an actionable architecture finding. A bounded analysis of a published LLM-generated behavior-tree system illustrates how an available clarification mechanism can go unused. The engineering lesson is to preserve accountability boundaries, not the computational pipelines that once exposed them.

</details>


### [30] [SpaceAct: Benchmarking 3D Spatial and Temporal Reasoning via Closed-Loop Action and Active Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38405)
*Chenglong Shi,Zekai Yuan,Yue Song,Jon Pu,Will Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SpaceAct交互式基准与SAPA智能体框架，用闭环物理动作和主动感知评估MLLM的3D时空推理，发现即使有有效计划，模型仍受限于3D视觉 grounding 与动作精修。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态大模型评测多为静态图像上的被动VQA或符号规划，无需真实执行、常提供特权坐标，且忽略碰撞、静力学与时序约束，无法检验模型在3D空间中的真实推理能力。

Method: 构建基于积木与机械谜题的SpaceAct基准：108个验证实例、36个任务族、6大类，不提供特权坐标，并设诊断模式（+plan、+state、no-camera、blind、L7 levers）。任务要求模型转动相机解遮挡、读取场景内标尺、在扫掠碰撞与静力学校验下输出6-DoF位姿，并通过分屏回滚反馈自我纠正；动态任务在思考时冻结时间并给5帧频闪。配套SAPA时空主动探测智能体框架。

Result: 10个前沿MLLM在44个最难实例上得分4.8–79.1；7个模型最多解出27.3%的6-DoF任务。前三模型加参考计划（+plan）弥合44%差距但碰撞仍达5.2次/回合，加精确3D状态（+state）弥合90%且碰撞降至0.5；另四个模型+plan由10.9升至35.3，+state升至81.8；46%的失败为碰撞或观察循环。

Conclusion: SpaceAct与SAPA将评测推进到闭环物理交互，表明MLLM即使拥有有效计划，仍受3D视觉grounding与动作精修瓶颈制约，并为主动多视角探索与显式3D grounding提供测试平台。

Abstract: To solve problems in 3D space rather than merely describe images, multimodal large language models (MLLMs) need 3D spatiotemporal reasoning: reconstructing 3D geometry from occluded 2D views, anticipating physical dynamics, and executing precise, closed-loop actions. Prior studies test passive VQA on static images or symbolic planning, requiring no execution or supplying privileged coordinates while ignoring collision, statics, and timing. We introduce SpaceAct, an interactive benchmark evaluating 3D spatiotemporal reasoning via closed-loop physical action and active perception, with SAPA, a Spatiotemporal Active-Probing Agent framework. Built on interlocking bricks and mechanical puzzles, it has 108 verified instances across 36 task families in six categories, without privileged coordinates, and diagnostic modes (+plan, +state, no-camera, blind, L7 levers). In brick-bridging or aperture-threading, models rotate the camera to resolve occlusion, read in-scene rulers, output 6-DoF poses under swept-collision and statics checks, and self-correct via split-screen rollback feedback (dynamic tasks freeze time during deliberation and give five strobe frames). Across 10 frontier MLLMs on the 44 hardest instances, scores span 4.8-79.1 (seven solve at most 27.3% of 6-DoF tasks); for the top three, a reference plan (+plan) closes 44% of the gap while leaving collisions at 5.2 per episode, whereas adding exact 3D state (+state) closes 90% and cuts collisions to 0.5; for four others, +plan lifts the mean score from 10.9 to 35.3 and +state to 81.8; and 46% of failures are collision or observation loops. SpaceAct and SAPA advance evaluation to closed-loop physical interaction, showing that even with a valid plan, MLLMs remain bottlenecked by 3D visual grounding and action refinement, while offering a testbed for active multi-view exploration and explicit 3D grounding.

</details>


### [31] [Skeleton-Guided Geometry-Aware Auto-Labeling for Robotic Grasping: From 4-DoF Learning to 6-DoF Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38443)
*Ali Sabzejou,Farbod Azimmohseni,Mehdi Tale Masouleh,Ahmad Kalhor*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于骨架的自动抓取标注方法，生成4-DoF与6-DoF抓取关键点与标注，并训练轻量网络SkelGG-CNN，在多个数据集与真实机器人实验中取得高成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人抓取依赖大规模人工标注数据集，标注成本高昂，因此需要无需人工干预、可自动生成一致抓取标注的流程。

Method: 提出基于直骨架（straight-skeleton）的算法生成双指抓取关键点并识别稳定可行抓取区域；构建自动标注流水线；提出4-DoF的SkelGG-CNN从骨架引导中学习几何抓取特征；并将自动标注扩展到6-DoF，开发可直接作用于点云的广义准三维骨架化算法。

Result: 使用自动生成4-DoF标签，SkelGG-CNN在Jacquard V1、V2和Cornell上分别达95.42%、96.55%、97.1%；GraspNet新划分上6-DoF自动标注平均精度18.33，与SOTA基于模型方法相当；仿真中6-DoF在Dex-Net达98.4%、EGAD!达89.1%；Delta并联机器人真实实验单物体97.4%、杂乱场景95.0%，无需域适应即优于SOTA。

Conclusion: 4-DoF流水线可跨环境适应，标注接近人类水平，支持小数据集高效训练轻量模型；广义6-DoF方法为杂乱3D场景提供可扩展、几何感知的标注。

Abstract: Robotic grasping relies on large manually annotated datasets, which are costly to create. We introduce a straight-skeleton-based algorithm for generating two-finger grasp keypoints and identifying regions suitable for stable, feasible grasps. Our automated pipeline produces consistent grasp annotations without human intervention. We also propose the Skeleton-based Generative Grasp Convolutional Neural Network (SkelGG-CNN), a 4-DoF architecture that learns geometric grasp features from skeleton-based guidance. To extend auto-labeling to 6-DoF grasping, we develop a generalized quasi-3D skeletonization algorithm that operates directly on point clouds. Using automatically generated 4-DoF labels, SkelGG-CNN achieves accuracies of 95.42%, 96.55%, and 97.1% on Jacquard V1, Jacquard V2, and Cornell, respectively. On the GraspNet novel split, 6-DoF auto-labeling achieves an average precision of 18.33, comparable to state-of-the-art model-based approaches. In simulation, the 6-DoF method achieves success rates of 98.4% on Dex-Net and 89.1% on EGAD!. Real-world experiments with a Delta parallel robot show success rates of 97.4% on single household objects and 95.0% in cluttered scenes, outperforming state-of-the-art methods without domain adaptation. The 4-DoF pipeline adapts across environments and produces annotations approaching human-level precision, enabling efficient training of lightweight models on small datasets. The generalized 6-DoF method provides scalable, geometry-aware annotations for cluttered 3D scenes.

</details>


### [32] [OmniQuad: a Wheeled-Legged Hybrid Robot with Omnidirectional Wheels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38502)
*Francesco Iotti,Alok Ranjan,Franco Angelini,Manolo Garabini*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 OmniQuad——一种将麦克纳姆轮与腿部运动相结合的混合机器人，通过定制轮足、EM-Act 执行器组装整机，并设计控制方案，在室内外环境中进行了实验评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轮式与腿式各有优劣：腿式机器人地形适应性强但速度慢，室内实用性受限；轮式机器人平地效率高但难以越过台阶等障碍。将二者融合于同一平台有望兼得两者优势，而此类混合系统仍是活跃研究方向。

Method: 1）设计一种集成麦克纳姆轮的定制"轮足"，借助全向移动能力提升狭小空间机动性与任务执行中保持朝向的能力；2）使用该轮足与 EM-Act 执行器完成整机装配；3）开发相应的控制方案以驱动 OmniQuad。

Result: 通过在室内和室外环境中开展的实验试验，对所开发控制方案及整机性能进行了评估（摘要未给出具体量化指标）。

Conclusion: OmniQuad 展示了麦克纳姆轮与腿式运动结合的可行性与潜在优势，为兼顾机动性、全向性与地形适应性的混合机器人平台提供了一种设计思路。

Abstract: Combining wheeled and legged locomotion within a single robotic platform offers the potential to harness the advantages of both systems. The development of such hybrid systems remains an active area of research. While legged robots exhibit remarkable adaptability for traversing uneven and rugged terrains, their relatively low speed limits their practicality in indoor environments. Conversely, wheeled robots demonstrate higher efficiency on flat surfaces but often encounter difficulties when navigating obstacles such as steps. This paper introduces OmniQuad, a novel hybrid robot that integrates mecanum wheels with legged locomotion to exploit the benefits of both modalities. Mecanum wheels provide omnidirectional mobility, enhancing the robot's manoeuvrability by enabling agile movement in confined spaces and the ability to maintain a consistent orientation during task execution. In this work, we first present the design of a custom wheeled-foot featuring mecanum wheels. Subsequently, we describe the complete robot assembly, comprising these wheeled feet and EM-Act actuators. Finally, we detail the control scheme developed to govern the OmniQuad and evaluate its performance through experimental trials conducted in both indoor and outdoor environments.

</details>


### [33] [SaiVLA-JEPA: Evaluating Future Latent Prediction in JEPA-Conditioned VLA Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38508)
*Rufeng Xiao,Ruihao Xiao,Wei Chen,Xinhai Sun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 SaiVLA-JEPA 系列策略，将冻结 V-JEPA 特征接入 VLM+扩散Transformer动作专家，在 LIBERO-Plus 上验证：当前 JEPA 条件化带来约 82.5% vs 71.6% 的成功率提升，而未来表征预测监督在给定训练预算下无显著收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: JEPA 通过预测表征而非像素来建模视觉场景演化，具有紧凑性优势。作者想检验：当一个 VLA 策略已经使用 V-JEPA 特征时，这种预测式目标能否进一步改善闭环控制性能，以及"预测未来"的辅助监督是否真的有用。

Method: 构建 SaiVLA-JEPA 策略族：视觉语言模型 + 扩散 Transformer 动作专家 + 冻结 V-JEPA 编码器特征输入。训练时加入两个轻量辅助头预测未来表征，推理时移除，动作采样器保持不变。在 10,030 对 LIBERO-Plus episode 上进行三组对比：无 JEPA 特征、仅当前 JEPA 条件化、额外未来监督。另评估两种适配变体：SaiVLA-JEPA-TCA（适配潜在上下文）与 SaiVLA-JEPA-TPA（适配部分参数），并与冻结策略及目标对照比较。

Result: 当前 JEPA 条件化成功率达约 82.5%，显著高于无 JEPA 的 71.6%；额外的未来表征监督在评估的训练预算下未带来可检测的整体增益。TCA 的"上下文重试"收益是在更大尝试预算下的系统级结果；TPA 相对冻结策略和目标对照没有一致优势。

Conclusion: JEPA 特征作为条件输入对闭环控制有效，但以表征预测损失为形式的"未来监督"并未转化为闭环性能提升。因此，评估潜在预测目标应以配对闭环 rollout 为准，而非仅依赖潜在空间的预测损失。

Abstract: Joint-embedding predictive architectures (JEPAs) learn to predict representations rather than pixels, offering a compact way to model how visual scenes evolve. We ask whether this predictive objective improves closed-loop control when a vision-language-action (VLA) policy already uses V-JEPA features. We call our JEPA-conditioned flow policy family SaiVLA-JEPA. It combines a vision-language model with a diffusion transformer action expert and receives features from a frozen V-JEPA encoder. Two lightweight auxiliary heads predict future representations during training and are omitted at inference, leaving the action sampler unchanged. We compare policies without JEPA features, with current JEPA conditioning, and with additional future supervision on 10,030 paired LIBERO-Plus episodes. Current JEPA conditioning reaches about 82.5% success versus 71.6% without JEPA, while future supervision provides no detectable aggregate gain at the evaluated training budget. We separately evaluate SaiVLA-JEPA-TCA, which adapts latent context, and SaiVLA-JEPA-TPA, which adapts selected parameters. The context-retry gain is a system-level result under a larger attempt budget; parameter adaptation shows no consistent advantage over the frozen policy and target controls. These results motivate assessing latent-prediction objectives through paired closed-loop rollouts rather than latent loss alone.

</details>


### [34] [Force-Driven Hook Disassembly via Constraint-Aware Object-Centric Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38513)
*Nadeen Elhadad,Takuya Kiyokawa,Kensuke Harada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出约束感知强化学习框架，通过接触力在线推断可行的无损拆卸方向，并结合粒子滤波信念实现从仿真到真实机器人的零样本迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 报废产品数量增长使自动化拆卸需求上升，但钩状连接存在方向多变、受载变形、几何被遮挡等问题，仅靠视觉无法确定可行的无损拆卸方向。

Method: 在机器人无关、以物体为中心的仿真中，用简化的轴孔配合（peg-in-hole）对训练策略；策略以接触力为输入并耦合粒子滤波信念，提供时间积分的方向信息以减少冗余探索动作；随后零样本部署到真实机器人，迁移至具有未见过的多阶段约束结构的真实连接。

Result: 在四项真实任务（从轴孔配合到报废空调内风叶与前盖）上验证：完整方法成功率分别为100%、100%、90%、75%，且每项任务路径效率最高；相对未使用信念的策略，空调前盖成功率由45%提升至75%。

Conclusion: 力驱动的强化学习可在无需产品专用模型或示教的情况下实现拆卸，说明力觉信息与时间积分信念对处理隐藏几何约束的有效性。

Abstract: The growing volume of end-of-life products has intensified the need for automated disassembly, yet hook-type connections remain challengin: their orientations vary, they deform under load, and their geometry is hidden, so vision alone cannot identify a feasible non-destructive disassembly direction. This work proposes a constraint-aware reinforcement learning framework that infers feasible extraction directions online from contact forces. The policy is trained in a robot-agnostic, object-centric simulation on a simplified peg-in-hole pair and deployed zero-shot on a physical robot, where it transfers to real connections with unseen, multi-stage constraint structures. Because instantaneous contact forces are noisy and locally ambiguous, the policy is coupled with a particle filter belief that provides the policy with temporally integrated directional information and reduces redundant exploratory motion. The approach is validated on four real-world tasks, from peg-in-hole pairs to the inner propeller and front cover of an end-of-life air-conditioning unit. The full method achieves success rates of 100%, 100%, 90%, and 75% with the highest path efficiency on every task, and raises the success rate on the air-conditioning cover from 45% to 75% relative to a policy trained without the belief. These results indicate that force-driven reinforcement learning can enable disassembly without product-specific models or demonstrations.

</details>


### [35] [A Multi-Layer Navigation System for Smart Wheelchairs with a Limited Input Interface](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38569)
*Piero Simonetto,Anna Polato,Francesco Pasti,Muhammad Rameez ur Rahman,Sebastiano Vascon,Nicola Bellotto,Emanuele Menegatti,Stefano Tortora,Luca Tonin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种面向智能轮椅的多层导航系统（MLNS），在未知环境中通过虚拟传感器驱动的势场法避障、模型预测控制生成轨迹、开放词汇视觉识别目标，并用优先队列进行目标选择，在真实轮椅上以10名受试者（含故障输入场景）验证了鲁棒性与有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能轮椅依赖人机接口或脑机接口解读用户高层意图，但在未知环境中导航和准确理解用户指令仍很困难；指令或环境误判会导致用户沮丧、不信任系统甚至产生不安全状况。因此需要一种在指令有限甚至存在错误时仍能安全执行并合理解读用户意图的导航方案。

Method: 构建多层导航系统：1）Virtual Sensors-Driven Potential Fields（ViSPF）方法进行障碍检测，并考虑轮椅实际尺寸；2）Model Predictive Control（MPC）模块用于轨迹生成；3）开放词汇计算机视觉算法用于目标识别；4）基于优先队列（PQ）的目标选择系统。

Result: 在真实智能轮椅上开展实验，10名无残障受试者参与，包含输入接口故障场景；结果表明系统在真实环境中具有鲁棒性和有效性，显示出在辅助技术中实际应用的潜力。

Conclusion: 该多层导航系统能够在未知环境中安全导航，并在用户指令有限或有误时仍合理地将意图置于安全语境中，验证了其在辅助技术领域的应用潜力。

Abstract: Smart wheelchairs are robotic devices that gather information from the surroundings and allow people to safely drive through various environments. They usually rely on the interpretation of high-level user's intentions, typically decoded through human-robot interface (HRI) or brain-machine interface (BMI). However, navigating in an unknown environment and accurately understanding user commands remain significant challenges. Misinterpretation of commands or the environment can lead to user frustration, distrust of the system, or even unsafe situations. In this paper, we present a novel Multi-Layer Navigation System (MLNS) for a smart wheelchair that enables navigation in unknown environments while interpreting and safely contextualizing user's intentions even in the case of limited and faulty commands. The stack relies on a Virtual Sensors-Driven Potential Fields (ViSPF) method for obstacle detection that accounts for the wheelchair's actual size, a Model Predictive Control (MPC) module for trajectory generation, and an open-vocabulary computer vision algorithm for target identification. Finally, a system based on Priority Queue (PQ) was implemented for target selection. We validated our system on a real smart wheelchair with experiments involving 10 participants without disabilities, including scenarios with faulty input interfaces. The results demonstrate the robustness and effectiveness of our system in real-world environments, proving its potential for practical applications in assistive technologies.

</details>


### [36] [Explore Where It Matters: Targeted Exploration for Efficient Rover Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38657)
*Jacob Levy,Arthur Bouton,Tristan D. Hassler,Hari Nayar,David Fridovich-Keil*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出在线自适应框架TERRA，通过奖励加权预测损失引导探索，快速定位并修正对任务关键的模型误差，实现少样本sim-to-real适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未来行星漫游车需穿越陡峭、可变形地形，滑移、沉陷与地形形变严重威胁移动能力；仿真虽能刻画部分轮-土相互作用，但未建模的可变形地形现象造成sim-to-real差距，必须在部署时适应；而实际作业几乎不允许大量试错，因此需要高效利用少量部署数据完成适应。
