Method: TERRA为在线适应框架：(1) 以奖励加权的预测损失对仿真预训练的世界模型进行在线修正；(2) 训练一个损失预测器，估计未来何处会出现与性能相关的预测误差；(3) 在MPC中将该信号作为探索引导，主动寻求能暴露任务相关模型误差的部署数据。

Result: 仿真中，在严重的分布外预训练条件下，TERRA约5次试验内达到约90%的爬坡成功率，并在奖励、完成时间与首次成功所需能量上优于探索基线；在ERNEST漫游车实物上，于可变形火星模拟斜坡上3至5次真实试验内恢复了主动悬挂的协调运动。

Conclusion: 面向奖励相关性、主动暴露模型误差的在线适应，可在极少试验预算下显著缩小可变形地形下的sim-to-real差距，具备实车部署可行性。

Abstract: Future planetary rovers will traverse steep, deformable terrain where slip, sinkage, and terrain deformation can threaten mobility. Although simulation can capture some wheel-soil effects, unmodeled deformable-terrain phenomena create sim-to-real gaps requiring deployment-time adaptation. Yet, rover operations leave little room for extensive trial-and-error. We introduce TERRA (Targeted Exploration for Reward-Relevant Adaptation), an online adaptation framework that efficiently seeks deployment data exposing task-relevant model errors and corrects them. TERRA adapts a simulation-trained world model with a reward-weighted prediction loss and trains a loss predictor to estimate where future performance-relevant errors are likely to occur, using this signal to guide exploration during MPC. In simulation, TERRA reaches approximately 90% slope-climbing success within five trials under severe out-of-distribution pretraining conditions, outperforming exploration baselines in reward, completion time, and energy to first success. On the ERNEST rover, TERRA recovers coordinated active-suspension locomotion on a deformable Mars-simulant slope within three to five real-world trials. Project website: https://terra-explore.github.io

</details>


### [37] [Quantize by Doing: Task-Guided Mixed-Precision Quantization of Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38661)
*Jiuyi Xu,Xiao Hu,Meida Chen,Song Wang,Yang Ye,Yangming Shi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 QbD-VLA：用短闭环评测中的任务成功率指导 VLA 模型的混合精度量化（权重/激活位宽分配），并显式考虑组件间耦合；在 4 个 VLA 模型与 LIBERO 四个套件上以约 4 bit 权重、<6 bit 激活保持较强任务性能，但在 SIMPLER 上相比均匀量化增益小，真机（Franka Research 3）上分配策略在 X-VLA 上未检测到成功率损失、在 π0.5 上失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 模型内存开销大，训练后量化（PTQ）可压缩内存，但降低位宽会损害任务表现。已有方法依赖校准数据的敏感度估计来分配位宽，而直接用任务成功率指导混合精度分配的研究不足——原因在于闭环评测代价高，且某一组件量化效果依赖于其他组件的精度（组件间耦合）。

Method: 1) 采用短闭环评测得到的任务成功率作为量化配置的指导信号，而非仅依赖校准数据敏感度；2) 在分配权重与激活位宽时显式建模组件之间的交互效应；3) 面向 VLA 模型的多组件结构做混合精度搜索与分配。

Result: 实验覆盖 4 个 VLA 模型与 LIBERO 全部四个套件，在权重平均约 4 bit、激活平均少于 6 bit 的条件下保持较强任务性能；在 SIMPLER 基准上相对均匀量化提升有限；在 Franka Research 3 真机上，仿真选出的分配方案在 X-VLA 上未检测到成功率下降，但在 π0.5 上失败。

Conclusion: 结果支持用任务成功率指导 VLA 混合精度量化这一思路的有效性，同时表明仿真/短闭环评测选出的方案在真机上未必可靠，部署前必须进行真实环境评测。代码已开源。

Abstract: Post-training quantization (PTQ) reduces the memory footprint of vision-language-action (VLA) models, but lowering bit widths while maintaining task performance remains challenging. Existing work has used sensitivity estimates from calibration data to assign bit widths to different model components. However, using task success directly to guide mixed-precision allocation is underexplored because closed-loop evaluations are costly and the effect of quantizing one component depends on the precision of other components. To this end, we propose \textbf{QbD-VLA}, a task-guided mixed-precision quantization method that uses task success from short closed-loop evaluations to assign weight and activation bit widths while accounting for interactions between model components. Experiments covering four VLA models and all four LIBERO suites show strong task performance at average precisions of approximately 4 bits for weights and fewer than 6 bits for activations. On SIMPLER, the gain over uniform quantization is small. On a Franka Research 3 robot arm, simulation-selected allocations show no detected success-rate loss on X-VLA but fail on $π_{0.5}$. These findings support using task success to guide mixed-precision VLA quantization, while highlighting the need for real-world evaluation before deployment. Code is available at \href{https://github.com/jiuyixu25/QbD-VLA}{\texttt{github.com/jiuyixu25/QbD-VLA}}.

</details>


### [38] [Value Function-Guided MPPI for Hierarchical Planning and Control in Off-Road Autonomous Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38731)
*Anoushka Alavilli,Raymond Song,Guanya Shi,Jeff Schneider*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种双向信息流的分层规划与控制框架，用预训练RL控制器的价值函数作为MPPI规划器的隐式代价，并加入在线策略评估以适应分布偏移，在越野仿真中相较基线MPPI更安全、更有效且无需显式代价地图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有越野自主导航多采用分层架构，依赖显式代价地图与从高层规划器到低层控制器单向的信息流，使规划与真实执行代价解耦；作者希望打通规划与控制之间的信息通道，让低层控制器反过来引导轨迹选择。

Method: 高层为MPPI风格的规划器，确定最优轨迹并将路点传给预训练的低层RL控制器；MPPI不依赖显式代价地图，而直接以RL控制器的价值函数作为隐式代价，从而让MPPI充当轨迹探索机制、由价值函数引导搜索；针对训练与评估间的分布偏移，引入在线策略评估过程，使价值函数适应未见过的驾驶经验。

Result: 在高保真越野驾驶仿真器中，于多种挑战性地形上同基线MPPI控制器对比，所提方法以更安全、更有效的方式到达目标位置，同时去除了对显式代价地图的需求，并实现了规划与控制更紧密的耦合。

Conclusion: 用RL价值函数替代显式代价地图实现双向耦合的分层规划控制是可行且有效的，在线策略评估可缓解价值函数的分布偏移；该框架为越野导航提供了无需代价地图、规划与控制紧耦合的新思路。

Abstract: Autonomous navigation in off-road terrain is commonly achieved using hierarchical control and planning architectures that rely on explicit cost maps and one-way information flow from a high-level planner to a low-level controller, decoupling planning from the true execution cost experienced during control. We propose a hierarchical planning and control framework in which information flows bidirectionally, allowing the low-level controller to guide trajectory selection at the planning level. The highest level of the hierarchy is a Model Predictive Path Integral (MPPI) control-style planner that determines optimal trajectories and passes these waypoints to a pretrained low-level reinforcement learning (RL) controller. Rather than drawing from an explicit cost map, our MPPI planner leverages the value function of the RL controller as an implicit cost representation. In this formulation, MPPI serves as a trajectory exploration mechanism, while the low-level controller's value function guides the search. To account for the distribution shift seen by the value function between training and evaluation, we propose an online policy evaluation process that adapts the value function to unseen driving experiences, and we demonstrate this adaptation in unseen off-road simulation environments. We evaluate our approach in a high-fidelity off-road driving simulator across a range of challenging terrain conditions and compare its performance against a baseline MPPI controller. The results demonstrate that our method achieves safer and more effective navigation to goal locations while eliminating the need for explicit cost maps and enabling tighter coupling between planning and control.

</details>


### [39] [AtomTree: A Hierarchical Framework for State-Aware Embodied Instruction Following with LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38744)
*Haotian Zhai,Tianming Sha,Junnan Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AtomTree 是一个面向交互环境具身指令跟随的分层 LLM 规划框架，基于当前符号状态增量构建"子目标—动作"树，并支持停滞时的分层回溯重规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法要么依赖大规模标注轨迹，要么用少样本/零样本 LLM 提示一次性串行推理出完整计划；这些方法依赖语言常识、从初始指令即锁定计划、仅在失败后才更新，导致鲁棒性与状态感知能力不足。

Method: AtomTree 增量构建子目标与动作的树结构；每一步由 LLM 依据当前符号状态决定是继续分解子目标还是输出可执行的低层动作；引入轻量记忆模块，汇总过往执行结果以供后续决策复用；当低层动作停滞时触发分层回溯重规划，回到父节点并从最新状态重新生成子树计划。

Result: 在 ALFRED 基准的零样本评测中，AtomTree 优于此前的基于 LLM 的规划器。

Conclusion: 通过状态感知的增量式树搜索与分层回溯重规划，AtomTree 避免了单次串行规划沿错误轨迹持续漂移的问题，提升了具身长程任务规划的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Embodied instruction following in interactive environments requires agents to interpret natural language commands and complete long-horizon tasks. Existing planners either rely on large labeled trajectories, or use few-shot and zero-shot LLM prompting that produces a whole plan in a single serial reasoning process. These approaches depend on linguistic common sense, commit to a plan from the initial instruction, and only update after failures, which weakens robustness and state awareness. We present AtomTree, a hierarchical framework for state-aware task planning with LLMs. AtomTree incrementally builds a tree of subgoals and actions, where the LLM decides at each step, from the current symbolic state, whether to further decompose or to output a grounded low-level action. A lightweight memory module summarizes past outcomes for reuse in later decisions. When a low-level action stalls, AtomTree triggers hierarchical backtracking replanning to return to the parent node and regenerate the subtree plan from the latest state, preventing continued drift along an incorrect trajectory. In zero-shot evaluation on the ALFRED benchmark, AtomTree outperforms prior LLM-based planners. Project page: https://zhaihaotian.github.io/atomtree-website/.

</details>


### [40] [Inertial Steering and Motion Prediction of Tracked Vehicles on Low-Adherence Surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38888)
*Mathis Turgeon-Roy,Mohamed Ounally,Veronica Vannini,Olivier Roy,François Pomerleau*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文研究在低附着（冰面）环境下，利用运动质量产生的惯性效应为履带车辆转向，而非将其视为干扰；建立了带被动拖车的铰接履带车动力学模型，并提出混合动-运动学模型，最终在无传统转向机构的单履带车上实现惯性转向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统履带车运动模型依赖无滑移假设，在低附着表面上失准；而现有做法通常把滑移引起的惯性效应当作需抑制的扰动。作者希望反过来主动利用惯性效应实现转向，为低附着环境下的履带车提供一种替代转向机制。

Method: 1）建立带被动拖车的铰接履带车动力学模型，以捕捉拖车运动带来的惯性效应；2）在冰面进行实验，与运动学基线对比；3）提出混合动力学-运动学模型，融合两种方法的预测以适配不同表面与工况；4）将内部质量视为无摩擦拖车，用所提动力学模型预测单履带车的惯性转向行为，揭示两种构型的动力学相似性。

Result: 在冰面实验中，动力学模型优于运动学基线；混合模型能同时利用两种方法的预测，适应不同表面与情形；单履带车通过内部控制质量的运动在冰面成功产生转向，且该行为可由所提动力学模型预测。

Conclusion: 结果证明了惯性转向作为低附着环境下履带车辆替代转向机制的可行性，并揭示了拖车构型与内部质量构型之间的动力学类比关系。

Abstract: Tracked vehicles are widely used in off-road applications, but their conventional motion models often rely on no-slip assumptions that become inaccurate on low-adherence surfaces. Rather than treating inertial effects arising from slip as a disturbance to be minimized, this work investigates how inertial effects arising from a moving mass can be deliberately exploited for steering on low-friction surfaces. A dynamic model for an articulated tracked vehicle with a passive trailer is developed, allowing inertial effects from trailer motion to be captured. The approach is experimentally evaluated on ice, where our dynamic model outperforms the kinematic baseline. We also propose a mixed dynamic-kinematic model to adapt predictions to both surface and situation by exploiting predictions from both methods. Finally, we demonstrate inertial steering on a mono-track vehicle without a conventional steering mechanism, showing that controlled movements of an internal mass can generate turning motion on ice. The resulting behavior can be predicted using the proposed dynamic model by representing the internal mass as a frictionless trailer, revealing the analogous dynamics between the two configurations. These results demonstrate the feasibility of inertial steering as an alternative steering mechanism for tracked vehicles operating in low-adherence environments.

</details>


### [41] [Selective Sliding Mode Control Enhances Robustness of Aerial Robots Under Disturbances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38930)
*Anoop Kiran,Thales C. Silva,Nora Ayanian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种有依据的方法，用于判断在四旋翼级联控制架构中哪些控制回路应当采用鲁棒控制：通过反演运动方程从受扰PID悬停飞行中恢复扰动信号，识别并量化被激励的轴，再仅在关键处部署鲁棒控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四旋翼在风阵与负载变化下控制困难。一阶滑模控制与匹配/非匹配不确定性划分虽是经典方法，但都无法告诉工程实践者：级联控制器中哪些回路应做鲁棒化才能维持抗扰、如何整定这些回路、以及嵌入式计算平台上的额外代价。对所有轴统一施加鲁棒控制会无差别地处理扰动，在无扰动轴上徒增整定工作量；而在欠驱动飞行器上，某些轴方向的扰动根本无法被直接抵抗。

Method: 提出一种判定鲁棒控制应置于何处的原则性方法，全部控制均板载运行。在扰动下进行PID悬停飞行，通过反演运动方程恢复作用扰动；利用恢复的信号识别并量化被激励的轴；随后仅在关键轴部署鲁棒控制。并在Crazyflie 2.1四旋翼上开展吊挂负载与斜风扰动实验，辅以增益扫描验证。

Result: 在吊挂负载与斜风扰动下，跟踪性能的改善集中于规则所选的轴；增益扫描证实，切换增益能降低匹配轴上的误差，但对被激励的非匹配轴没有效果，与分析预测一致。

Conclusion: 该方法为级联架构中鲁棒控制的配置位置提供了可操作的判据，避免了无差别的全轴鲁棒化及其整定负担，并明确了鲁棒控制在欠驱动轴上的作用边界。

Abstract: Quadrotor control under disturbances such as wind gusts and payload variations remains challenging. First-order sliding mode control (SMC) and matched/unmatched uncertainty distinction are classical approaches; however, neither tells a practitioner which loops in a cascaded controller should be made robust to sustain disturbances, how to tune those loops, nor what the additional costs are on an embedded processor. Applying robust control uniformly across all axes addresses disturbances indiscriminately, adding tuning effort on axes where no disturbance acts. On inherently underactuated vehicles, disturbances along some axes cannot be opposed directly at all. We propose a principled methodology for deciding where robust control belongs in a cascaded architecture, with all control running onboard the vehicle. We recover the acting disturbance from PID hover flights under disturbance by inverting the equations of motion, use the recovered signal to identify and quantify the excited axes, and deploy robust control where it counts. Experiments on a Crazyflie 2.1 quadrotor under slung-payload and oblique-wind disturbances show tracking improvements concentrated on the axes the rule selects, and gain sweeps confirm that the switching gain reduces error on matched axes but has no effect on an excited unmatched axis, as the analysis predicts.

</details>


### [42] [Learning Event-based Obstacle Avoidance via Differentiable Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38967)
*Chenlu Shou,Yunfan Ren,Jiaxu Xing,Davide Scaramuzza*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于CUDA事件模拟器的可微仿真管线，训练从事件帧直接映射到控制指令的端到端策略，实现四旋翼在未知环境中的零样本真实避障，包括低光照条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统帧相机在快速运动下存在运动模糊、动态范围有限和延迟问题；事件相机虽具亚毫秒延迟、高动态范围和低运动模糊优势，但用于闭环控制仍困难。现有方法要么采用"先估计深度再映射控制"的级联架构带来误差传播，要么依赖昂贵的离线预训练后再在线微调。

Method: 构建基于CUDA的事件模拟器，利用GPU并行在训练循环内高效渲染事件；将其集成到可微仿真管线中，使梯度可直接通过可微物理模型反传，提升样本效率；据此训练端到端策略，将事件帧直接映射为控制指令，无需中间深度估计。

Result: 在真实四旋翼上实现零样本部署，在辅助里程计与无里程计两种设定下均实现稳健避障，并在传统相机通常失效的低光照条件下同样有效。

Conclusion: 事件相机结合可微仿真与端到端策略学习，可绕开级联深度估计与离线预训练的成本，为快速运动与低光照下的四旋翼避障提供可行且样本高效的方案。

Abstract: Reliable obstacle avoidance is essential for safe quadrotor flight in unknown environments, yet conventional frame-based cameras remain challenged by motion blur, limited dynamic range, and latency under fast motion. Event cameras, with sub-millisecond latency, high dynamic range, and negligible motion blur, offer a promising alternative, but leveraging them for closed-loop control remains difficult. Existing approaches either adopt cascaded architectures that first estimate depth and then map it to control commands, introducing error propagation, or achieve end-to-end control through costly offline pretraining before online fine-tuning can even begin. We address these challenges with a CUDA-based event simulator that exploits GPU parallelism to render events efficiently within the training loop, integrated into a differentiable simulation pipeline that enables direct gradient backpropagation through the differentiable physics model, improving sample efficiency. This allows us to train an end-to-end policy that maps event frames directly to control commands, without intermediate depth estimation. We validate our approach through zero-shot real-world deployment on a quadrotor, achieving robust obstacle avoidance in both odometry-assisted and odometry-free settings, including under low-light conditions where conventional cameras typically fail.

</details>


### [43] [Open-World Object Motion Anticipation via Meta-Learned Semantic Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38993)
*Mason Peterson,Gage MacLin,Camilla Tabasso,Jonathan P. How,Joshua Migdal,Tye Brady,Taskin Padir*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种元学习的图神经网络，利用物体的语义、几何与关系历史，从少量在线观测预测新环境中物体何时开始运动，仅用仿真训练即可迁移到实机。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人导航需预判附近物体运动；缺乏对新物体（如叉车被操作以移动托盘）的在线适应能力，导致规划要么过于保守低效，要么过于冒险碰撞。现有跟踪与预测研究多针对已知物体。

Method: 元学习 + 图神经网络；以物体的语义、几何及相互关系历史为输入，基于少量在线观测预测物体即将开始运动的时刻；仅在仿真中训练。

Result: 能准确预判训练时未见物体的运动；无需微调即迁移到硬件；在仿真与实机实验中优于视觉语言模型和类频率基线；闭环导航仿真中实现无碰撞通行，耗时与最快但易碰撞的规划器相差不超过4秒。

Conclusion: 元学习图网络可让机器人从少量观测在线适应新物体，实现安全高效的导航决策。

Abstract: Anticipating the motion of nearby objects is critical for safe and efficient robot navigation. Without a proper understanding of what may move, a robot may plan overly conservative, inefficient trajectories to guarantee safety, or it may be overly risky, resulting in unacceptable collisions. While tracking and predicting the motion of known objects has seen significant progress, little research addresses how a robot should adapt online to novel objects, including anticipating how one object may move another (e.g., a person operating a forklift to move a pallet). To enable this capability, we propose a meta-learned graph neural network that leverages a semantic, geometric, and relational history of objects to accurately predict, from a few online observations, when objects will begin to move in new environments. We demonstrate that our method, which is trained only in simulation, accurately anticipates the motion of objects unseen at training time, transfers to hardware without any fine-tuning, and outperforms a vision-language model and a class-frequency baseline in both simulation and hardware experiments. Our method's accurate prediction enables collision-free traversals in a closed-loop navigation simulation, while remaining within four seconds of the fastest, collision-prone planner.

</details>


### [44] [Self-Supervised Doppler-Guided RF Odometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39005)
*Kunzhe Song,Jialuo Du,Jingkai Lin,Huacheng Zeng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RFWalk 是一个仅依赖射频（RF）传感、自监督的机器人自运动里程计框架，将距离-方位帧间的稠密对应建模为时空图上的随机游走，用时间循环一致性学习，并引入多普勒一致性损失排除物理上不合理的对应，最后通过鲁棒的 SE(2) 配准恢复自运动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RF 传感可在黑暗、烟雾和雾霾中实现机器人自运动估计，但多径效应和移动反射体导致跨帧匹配高度歧义；现有方法往往依赖辅助惯性传感器或真值位姿监督，限制了实用性与泛化能力。

Method: 提出 RFWalk：将距离-方位帧之间的稠密对应建模为时空图上的随机游走，并利用未标注序列通过时间循环一致性进行自监督学习；引入多普勒一致性损失，用 RF 传感器自身测得的径向速度约束由对应关系诱导的运动，从而剔除虽循环一致但物理上不合理的对应；随后采用鲁棒 SE(2) 配准恢复自运动，抑制不确定对应及独立运动物体带来的干扰。

Result: 在无位姿标签、无辅助传感器条件下，RFWalk 在静态场景中与有监督基线具有竞争力；在含移动行人的动态场景中，绝对轨迹误差降低 58%。

Conclusion: RFWalk 表明仅凭自监督 RF 数据即可实现稳健的里程计估计，多普勒一致性约束与鲁棒配准是应对歧义匹配和动态干扰的关键，为恶劣视觉条件下的机器人定位提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Radio-frequency (RF) sensing enables robot ego-motion estimation in darkness, smoke, and fog, yet RF odometry remains challenging because multipath and moving reflectors make cross-frame matching highly ambiguous. Existing methods often rely on auxiliary inertial sensors or ground-truth pose supervision. We present RFWalk, a self-supervised, RF-only odometry framework that models dense correspondences across range-azimuth frames as random walks on a space-time graph and learns them from unlabeled sequences through temporal cycle consistency. To rule out correspondences that are cycle-consistent but physically implausible, we introduce a Doppler consistency loss that constrains correspondence-induced motion using radial velocity measured by the RF sensor itself. Ego-motion is then recovered through robust $\mathrm{SE}(2)$ registration that suppresses uncertain correspondences and those arising from independently moving objects. Without pose labels or auxiliary sensors, RFWalk remains competitive with supervised baselines in static scenes and reduces absolute trajectory error by 58% in dynamic scenes with moving pedestrians.

</details>


### [45] [Catch It All!: Generalizable Dynamic Catching with a Dexterous Hand](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39044)
*Arth Shukla,Kehlani Fay,Huaze Liu,Darin Djapri,Alexiy Buynitsky,Nicklas Hansen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 Catch It All!，一个面向人形机器人的仿真到现实接物框架，仅用一个第一人称相机即可泛化并适应未知物体与不可预测的接触动力学，实现下手与上手接物。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 任意物体接取需要泛化到新抛射物，并在有噪声的视觉观测下实时适应不可预测的接触动力学；现有方法难以应对物体几何、形变、弹性等物理属性差异，也难以在接近硬件极限（如手腕力矩低至 5 Nm）时安全高频执行。

Method: 学习可泛化到几何、形变、弹性等物理属性各异物体的策略；通过精心设计的抓取奖励学习能实时适应物体位姿与形状的有效抓取；引入安全感知课程学习（safety-aware curricula），使动作可在接近硬件极限处安全高频执行（含低至 5 Nm 的手腕力矩限制）；系统在 Unitree G1 上零样本部署，仅使用单个第一人称相机。

Result: 在 Unitree G1 上零样本部署后，Catch It All! 仅凭单个第一人称相机，成功对 9 个训练中未见过的物体完成下手与上手接物，包括低回弹毛绒玩具、半形变玩具与薯片袋，以及接触时高回弹的刚弹物体。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，借助抓取奖励设计与安全感知课程，单相机仿真到现实的接物策略可以在真实人形机器人上实现对未知物体、多样物理属性与不可预测接触动力学的泛化与实时适应。

Abstract: Catching arbitrary objects requires generalization to novel projectiles and real-time adaptation to unpredictable contact dynamics under noisy visual observations. This paper presents Catch It All!, a sim-to-real catching framework for humanoid robots that generalizes and adapts to unseen objects with variable physical properties for underhand and overhand catches, using only one egocentric camera. We learn policies which generalize to unseen objects with varying geometry, deformability, elasticity, and physical properties. Effective grasps which adapt real-time to object poses and shapes are learned through careful grasp reward design. We provide safety-aware curricula which enable safe, high-frequency action execution near hardware limits, including wrist torque limits as low as 5 Nm. We validate our system by deploying zero-shot on a Unitree G1. With only one egocentric camera, Catch It All! successfully performs underhand and overhand catches on 9 unseen objects not seen during training, including low-rebound plushes, semi-deformable toys and chip bags, and rigid-elastic objects with high rebound on contact.

</details>


### [46] [Robotic Data Inoculation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39054)
*Qiyang Li,Zhiyuan Zhou,Charles Xu,Sergey Levine*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 Data Inoculation (DI)，一种共享控制数据采集框架：人类提供闭环遥操作指令，同时一个残差策略在线扰动这些指令，从而在人类保持控制的前提下采集到覆盖更广、包含恢复示范的高质量数据，提升下游模仿学习/离线RL策略的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人学习依赖高覆盖的高质量示范数据，但缺乏有原则的高覆盖数据采集方法：纯在线RL探索代价高且不安全，DAgger式干预需要策略先偶然失败才能被纠正，现有采集多依赖启发式人工指令。

Method: 提出共享控制框架 DI：人类给出闭环遥操作命令，残差策略对命令施加扰动；该残差策略在持续增长的数据集上在线训练，使用与后续策略提取相同的奖励最大化目标，使扰动方向对齐"在该数据上训练出的策略会产生的偏差"；人类持续纠正提供恢复示范。

Result: 在三个具有挑战性的真实机器人任务上，DI 训练出的策略在成功率上一致优于或持平于行为克隆与离线RL训练的基线策略。

Conclusion: DI 是一种有效的机器人数据采集策略：通过让扰动与学习器未来错误对齐并由人类实时纠正，实现"疫苗式"数据增强，提升下游策略性能，且人类始终掌控机器人安全。

Abstract: Modern robot learning systems feed on high-quality demonstration data, and their performance hinges on coverage. Without good coverage, small errors compound and the policy can quickly go into out-of-distribution states from which it cannot recover. Yet there is no principled way of collecting high-coverage data, and data collection often relies on heuristic instructions to human operators. Purely online reinforcement learning (RL) explores at the cost of safety and sample efficiency, while DAgger-style interventions require the policy to fail by chance before a human can correct it. In this paper, we ask instead whether we can modify how the robot behaves during data collection to collect more useful data. We propose Data Inoculation (DI), a shared-control framework in which a human provide closed-loop teleoperation commands while a residual policy perturbs these commands. The residual policy is trained online, on the growing dataset, with the same reward-maximizing objective later used for policy extraction, so its perturbations steer data collection toward the deviations a policy trained on the data would make. These perturbations expose the robot to a wider range of states, while the human's continuous corrections provide recovery demonstrations, "inoculating" the dataset against the learner's future mistakes while the human remains in control. On three challenging real-world robotic tasks, DI consistently improves or matches the success rate of policies trained with both behavioral cloning and offline RL, showing DI as an effective data collection strategy for improving downstream policy performance.

</details>

*Jiahe Wang,Sultan Haidar Ali,Eric Weissman,Jiefeng Sun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种嵌入式形状感知框架，利用IMU与执行器反馈结合神经网络以400Hz实时重建连续体机器人形状，实现闭环形状控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续体机器人在受限、封闭环境中运行时无法依赖外部观测，现有嵌入式形状感知方法在重建精度、计算速度及与形状状态表示的兼容性上不足，难以支持实时形状控制。

Method: 系统研究四种关键设计选择：形状状态表示、网络架构、训练目标和传感输入；在一段与三段连续体机器人上训练超过5000个模型进行对比，并利用IMU与执行器反馈进行神经网络形状重建，实现400Hz闭环反馈，同时开展闭环控制与封闭空间实验。

Result: 一段与三段系统全局形状误差分别为0.9%L和1.7%L；闭环控制中一段末端跟踪误差6.1%L，三段全局形状误差2.3%L；而采用相同未补偿测量的IMU–PCC重建方法失败；封闭空间实验验证了无外部观测下的运行。

Conclusion: 该工作为将易获取的嵌入式传感转化为连续体机器人闭环控制所需的高速率反馈提供了实用框架。

Abstract: Controlling continuum robots' shape to operate in confined, enclosed environments is challenging, since it cannot rely on external observation. Existing embedded shape sensing methods, although they can provide shape reconstruction, are insufficient to enable real-time shape control due to limitations in reconstruction accuracy, computational speed, and compatibility with shape-state representation. In this work, we present an embedded shape-sensing framework that uses readily available IMU and actuator feedback to reconstruct the continuum-robot shape with a neural network, providing accurate shape feedback at $400~\mathrm{Hz}$ for physical closed-loop control. We systematically investigate four key reconstruction design choices: shape-state representation, network architecture, training objective, and sensing inputs, through more than $5{,}000$ trained models on one- and three-segment continuum robots. The selected framework achieves global shape errors of $0.9\%L$ and $1.7\%L$ (of the robot length) for the one- and three-segment systems, respectively. In closed-loop control, one-segment tip tracking achieves a $6.1\%L$ error and three-segment shape control achieves a $2.3\%L$ global shape error, whereas IMU--PCC reconstruction using the same uncompensated measurements fails. Enclosed-space experiments further demonstrate operation without external observation. These results provide a practical framework for translating readily available embedded sensing into high-rate feedback for continuum robot closed-loop control.

</details>

*Li Guan,Rachael Granberry,Sahar Aseeri,Sahar Rostami,Taylor Niehues,Sai Dhanooshi Marri,Mia Huang,Dingtian Zhang,Ziyi Kou,Jason Pursel,Eric Whitmire,Vatsal Mehta,Ashish Deshpande,Tristan Trutna,Reed Belcher,Emelia Black,Julian Blake,Roger Boldu,Sean Bonaventure,Einar Boyovich,Jordan Brennan,Elizabeth Casillas,David Christensen,Tyler Christensen,Nicholas Colonnese,Stuart Dealey,Gaige DeHaven,Katie Doran,Chris Doughty,Robert Drury,Silvia Espino,Gabriel Geyne,Beth Goodrich,Kyra Gould,Joe Grisso,Susana Guerrero,Andrew Gwozdziowski,Monica Ha,Ehsan Hajiesmaili,Kate Healy,Rose Hope,Michael Hurst,Claire Ikemiya,Daisy Isibor,Rick James,Yashasvi Joginapally,Samuel Johnson,Tyler Jorgensen,Kristy Jost,Ben Kamath,Slava Karulin,Alexander Keener,Woo Kim,Alison Krauskopf,Ankit Kumar,Joshua Lang,Lizzy Li,Cameron Lynch,Daniel Ma,Hector Martinon,Christina Matos,Keagan McCurdy,Lee Mincey,Stuart Nezlek,Tiffany Nguyen,James O'Hara,Jason Osborne,Jacky Pai,Roham Pardakhtim,David Perek,Alexandre Poulin,Nick Raines,Salwa Rasheed,Leah Resneck,Kevin Rhodes,Ergys Ristani,Douglas Roberts,Anik Sarker,Harrison Scarborough,Jonas Schmidtler,Brian Shieh,Benjamin Shuman,Garrett Simard,Forrest Smith,Udara Somarathna,Eliot Sosin,Jonathan Stewart,Kristine Strom,Adam Swett,Hal Talbot-Mantooth,Julia Tanner,Sai Sharan Thirunagari,Curtis Torrey,Simge Uzun,Eileen Wachs,Rylan Wasson,Henry Wei,Jesse White,Beau Woodbyrne,Antonio Woods,Noah Young,Chandler Yount,Joseph Zhang,Sean Keller*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出织物集成传感手套Tactus，同时具备标定触觉力感知与抗遮挡手部追踪，并建立首个跨平台触觉手套基准测试，在力检测灵敏度、跨被试一致性和遮挡下姿态精度上优于商用基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 采集自然状态下、带准确接触力与手部姿态的人类操作演示，对机器人灵巧操作策略与世界模型学习至关重要；但现有可穿戴平台无法同时提供经过标定的指尖力感知和抗遮挡手部追踪，且难以保持自然操作行为。

Method: 从三个设计支柱协同优化：(1) 薄型贴合3D编织形态（指尖堆叠厚度1.5 mm）以减小对自然操作的干扰；(2) 依据实证接触研究，在操作相关区域布置电容式触觉传感器；(3) 融合IMU的姿态追踪，在最有价值的手-物遮挡情形下维持精度。另有文档化标定流程（跨手套一致性）与逐场次标定（个体差异校正），并提出触觉力回归与手部姿态估计的标准化基准协议，用于Tactus与两款商用触觉手套的定量对比。

Result: 95%置信度下最小可检测力0.59 N，比商用基线低1.6–2.7倍；跨被试一致性0.87；操作中遮挡最严重时指尖关键点相对误差11.0 mm。据作者所知，这是该类平台首次定量跨平台评测。

Conclusion: 融合的触觉与姿态信号可支持物体属性估计、滑移检测和手-物交互的代表性记录，所得演示数据具备接触感知操作策略与世界模型所需保真度。

Abstract: Collecting naturalistic human manipulation demonstrations with accurate contact force and hand pose is essential for robot dexterous manipulation policy and foundational world model learning - yet no existing wearable platform provides both calibrated fingertip force sensing and occlusion-resilient hand tracking in a form factor that preserves natural manipulation behavior. We present Tactus, a textile-integrated sensing glove that co-optimizes three design pillars: (1) a thin, conformal 3D-knit form factor (1.5 mm fingertip stackup) designed to minimize interference with natural manipulation, (2) strategically placed capacitive tactile sensors at manipulation-relevant regions informed by empirical contact studies, and (3) IMU-fused pose tracking that maintains accuracy through the hand-object occlusions where manipulation data is most valuable. Tactus includes a documented calibration pipeline for cross-glove consistency and per-session calibration for subject-dependent correction. To enable objective comparison, we introduce standardized benchmarking protocols for tactile force regression and hand pose estimation, and apply them to Tactus and two commercially available tactile gloves - to our knowledge the first quantitative cross-platform evaluation of this kind. Tactus resolves a minimum detectable force of 0.59 N at 95% confidence, 1.6 - 2.7x lower than the commercial baselines, and with 0.87 cross-participant consistency, while our pose tracking sustains 11.0 mm fingertip keypoint relative error during manipulation, when occlusion is heaviest. We show that these combined signals support object property estimation, slip detection, and representative recording of hand-object interaction - yielding demonstration data with the fidelity required for contact-aware manipulation policies and world models.

</details>


### [49] [CAPTURER: A Dexterous Aerial Manipulator for Dynamic and Precise Grasping across Diverse Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39119)
*Meng Wang,Jindou Jia,Sicheng Zhou,Zeshuai Chen,Kexin Guo,Xiang Yu,Lei Guo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出CAPTURER——一种灵巧空中操作臂系统，可在多样化环境下实现对厘米级目标的抓取。系统集成机载感知、协同运动规划与抗扰控制，室内外实验验证了在超过1.5 m/s飞行速度下对直径仅1.5 cm的马克笔实现厘米级抓取精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有空中抓取系统多在固定场景下对静态或缓慢移动目标作业，而跨条件的高动态精细抓取面临目标运动、定位漂移、动态耦合与外部扰动等多重挑战，缺乏统一解决方案。

Method: 1）构建机载感知系统用于状态估计与目标检测，引入漂移校正与全局位姿变换提升飞行中的目标定位精度；2）提出协同运动规划器，依据相对目标状态协调空中平台机动性与机械臂灵巧性；3）设计抗扰控制器，补偿动态耦合、模型不确定性与外部扰动。

Result: 通过室内外静态与移动目标实验验证，CAPTURER在飞行速度超过1.5 m/s时，对直径仅1.5 cm的马克笔实现厘米级抓取精度。

Conclusion: CAPTURER表明，将感知漂移校正、平台—机械臂协同规划与扰动抑制控制相结合，可有效支撑多样化环境下的高速动态精细空中抓取，为空中操作臂的实际部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: As a representative form of embodied robotic interaction, aerial grasping requires close coordination between the aerial platform and manipulator. Most existing systems perform aerial grasping with static or slowly moving targets under fixed scenarios. Dynamic and precise grasping across different conditions introduces additional challenges, including target motion, localization drift, dynamic coupling, and external disturbances. In this work, we present CAPTURER, a dexterous aerial manipulator for centimeter-level grasping across diverse environments. To achieve the challenging goal, we first develop an onboard perception system for state estimation and target detection. Drift correction and global pose transformation are introduced to improve target localization during flight. A cooperative motion planner is proposed to coordinate the mobility of the aerial platform with the dexterity of the manipulator according to the relative target state. To maintain grasping accuracy under disturbances, we design a disturbance-rejection controller to compensate for dynamic coupling, model uncertainty, and external disturbances. The capability of CAPTURER is validated through indoor and outdoor experiments with both static and moving targets. The results demonstrate that CAPTURER achieves centimeter-level grasping accuracy for a marker pen measuring only 1.5 cm in diameter at flight speeds exceeding 1.5 m/s.

</details>


### [50] [Tracking Task Progress Through Errors and Recovery:A Benchmark and Recurrent Distributional Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39125)
*Chen Wang,Xinze Niu,Zihao Zhu,Keru Zhou,Yicheng Xu,Shihao Jin,Wenhao Wang,Bowen Jiang,Chang Zhou,Li Liu,Maoqing Yao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文开放了长时序机器人任务数据集 ReSTEP（两个操作任务、3156 条轨迹、语义阶段标签与帧级错误标注），并提出在线进度估计器 REMORA，通过循环视觉记忆、分布预测与因果峰值选择实现一致且能响应错误/恢复的进度跟踪，在两任务上的进度精度与时间一致性均优于 SARM 基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时序机器人操作策略需要可靠评估判断执行是推进、停滞、回退还是从错误中恢复；但真实世界机器人数据中，包含错误恢复过程并带帧级错误标注的数据集极度稀缺，导致进度监控难以训练与评测。同时现有进度估计器难以区分视觉相似的任务阶段，也难以在保持时间一致性的同时响应真实的失败与恢复。

Method: 1) 构建并开源 ReSTEP 数据集：覆盖两个操作任务共 3,156 条 episode，含语义阶段标签与帧级错误标注，涵盖成功执行、错误以及恢复过程，用于训练和基准测试进度监控器。2) 提出 REMORA 在线进度估计器，包含三个组件：循环视觉记忆（保留执行历史以消解视觉歧义）、分布预测（表达多种进度假设）、因果峰值选择（随时间追踪有支撑的假设）。

Result: 在两个 ReSTEP 任务上的实验表明，REMORA 在进度估计精度与时间一致性两项指标上均优于当前最先进的 SARM 基线。

Conclusion: 带错误恢复标注的真实机器人数据集的开放填补了进度监控评测的空白；结合记忆、分布化假设与因果峰值选择的在线估计框架，能够在保持时序稳定的同时及时反映错误与恢复，为长时序机器人任务的执行评估提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Operational strategies for long-horizon robotic tasks require reliable evaluation to determine whether execution is progressing, stalling, regressing, or recovering from errors. Yet real- world robot datasets that capture error recovery and provide frame-level error annotations remain scarce. To address this gap, we introduce and open-source ReSTEP, a dataset of 3,156 episodes across two manipulation tasks with semantic stage labels and frame-level er- ror annotations. ReSTEP supports training and benchmarking progress monitors through successful execution, mistakes, and recovery. Existing progress estimators can struggle to distinguish visually similar task stages and maintain temporally consistent predictions while responding to genuine setbacks. We therefore propose REMORA, an online progress esti- mator that combines recurrent visual memory, distributional prediction, and causal peak selection. Memory retains execution history to help resolve visual ambiguity, while distribu- tional prediction represents alternative progress hypotheses and causal peak selection tracks a supported hypothesis over time. Together, these components enable consistent progress tracking that remains responsive to errors and recovery. Experiments on both ReSTEP tasks demonstrate that REMORA outperforms the state-of-the-art SARM baseline in progress ac- curacy and temporal consistency.

</details>


### [51] [Actuator-Aware Observer Inputs for Cascade ADRC of Quadrotors Under Rotor Lag, Measurement Delay and Sensor Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39126)
*Xuan-Quy Dao,Ngoc-Bich Le,Le-Minh Nguyen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出"执行器感知"(AA)观测器输入方案：将转子滤波与测量延迟纳入LADRC扩张状态观测器的输入构造，在灵敏度与阶跃响应约束下设计固定增益，并验证其优于PID。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 采样级联LADRC中，扩张状态观测器收到的是瞬时指令，而实际延迟测量反映的是更早的、经转子滤波的加速度，二者不匹配导致观测误差与跟踪性能下降。

Method: 高度与姿态观测器使用经假定转子模型滤波、并与测量延迟对齐的受限虚拟输入；水平观测器使用由实测姿态重构的加速度。基于离散时间模型，在灵敏度、阶跃响应要求及各通道控制量/增量方差与级联内环噪声增量的稳态协方差上限下选取固定增益。开展成对设定点与八字轨迹试验，含PX4软件在环，并在EKF2后拟合噪声模型检验预测能力。

Result: 成对设定点试验中PID-Budget、AA-Budget、AA-HalfGain平均RMSE为65.5、19.2、16.9 mm；更严格共同级联上限下AA为29.9与26.4 mm，而转子指令增量约两倍的PID为72.3 mm。PX4 SITL复现结果。噪声模型对横滚/俯仰与偏航增量预测误差在5%内、总转子指令增量在14%内。EKF2栈上AA-Budget-E将RMSE由59.1 mm降至44.0 mm且转子指令增量更低（八字轨迹平均增量降低45%/15%）；AA-HalfGain-E仅在八字轨迹上优于PID-E。

Conclusion: 执行器感知观测器输入可在同等或更低的控制增量预算下显著降低四旋翼级联LADRC的跟踪误差，且结论在PX4 SITL与EKF2场景中一致；但证据均为仿真、协方差上限为名义调参约束，尚需实飞验证。

Abstract: In sampled cascade linear active disturbance rejection control (LADRC) of quadrotors, the extended-state observer typically receives the instantaneous command, although the delayed measurement reflects an earlier, rotor-filtered acceleration. This paper proposes actuator-aware (AA) observer inputs: altitude and attitude observers use limited virtual inputs filtered by an assumed rotor model and aligned with the measurement delay, and horizontal observers use acceleration reconstructed from measured attitude. Fixed gains are selected on the discrete-time model under sensitivity and step-response requirements and stationary covariance ceilings on each channel's control variance and increment and on the noise-induced inner-loop increment of the full cascade. In paired set-point trials, PID-Budget, AA-Budget and AA-HalfGain reach mean RMSEs of 65.5, 19.2 and 16.9~mm; under a stricter common cascade ceiling, the AA designs reach 29.9 and 26.4~mm versus 72.3~mm for a PID that uses roughly double their rotor-command increment. PX4 software-in-the-loop runs reproduce these results. Behind EKF2, a noise model fitted to the estimation errors predicts the roll/pitch and yaw increments within 5\% on held-out and out-of-sample runs and the total rotor-command increment within 14\%. Redesigned for this stack under the same sensitivity and response specification and estimator-aware ceilings, AA-Budget-E reduces the set-point RMSE from 59.1~mm (PID-E) to 44.0~mm with a lower rotor-command increment in every paired set-point and lemniscate run (mean S6 rotor-command-increment reductions relative to PID-E: 45\% before and 15\% after output quantization); AA-HalfGain-E beats PID-E only on the lemniscate. The evidence is simulation-based, and the ceilings are nominal tuning constraints.

</details>


### [52] [EgoContact: Evaluating Physical Intelligence with Egocentric Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39167)
*Adam Deryło,Alexey Gavryushin,Daniel Cremers,Marc Pollefeys,Xi Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 EgoContact，一个大规模第一人称视频数据集（含 2.25 万个人工核验的交互事件），用于通过"未来接触点预测"任务评估操作先验的泛化性与精确性，并证明其可作为记忆库提升实际机器人抓取成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第一人称视频虽已成为预训练机器人策略的可扩展数据来源，但作为"物理智能"验证工具仍被低估；其中关键能力是理解应在物体何处发生接触以实现预期交互，目前缺乏系统衡量该能力的评测手段。

Method: 构建 EgoContact 数据集：每个交互事件包含接触前观测、语义意图标签与像素级精确接触位置；以此定义未来接触点预测任务，对现有方法在多样化、真实野外场景中的泛化性与精度进行评估；同时将 EgoContact 作为现有 affordance-transfer 策略的记忆库加以应用。

Result: 现有方法往往难以预测精确的接触点，且在多样野外场景中有时即使允许多次预测也无法定位目标物体；将 EgoContact 用作记忆库后，抓取成功率在仿真中从 14.2% 提升至 31.2%，在真实机器人上从 16% 提升至 58%。

Conclusion: 第一人称视频数据不仅是预训练资源，也可作为物理智能的有效验证基准；EgoContact 既暴露了当前方法在接触点预测上的明显不足，又可通过简单复用显著提升下游机器人操作性能，具备评测与实用双重价值。

Abstract: Egocentric video has emerged as one of the most scalable sources of data for learning generalizable manipulation priors. Yet, despite its growing use in pretraining robotic policies, it has been underutilized as a validation tool for physical intelligence, a key component of which is understanding where to make contact with an object to achieve an intended interaction. We present EgoContact, a large-scale egocentric dataset for evaluating the generalizability and precision of learned manipulation priors through future contact-point prediction. EgoContact contains 22.5K manually verified interaction events, each with a pre-contact observation, semantic intent, and pixel-precise contact locations. We find that current methods often struggle to predict precise interaction contacts and sometimes fail to localize the target object in diverse, in-the-wild scenes, even when allowed multiple predictions. Furthermore, we demonstrate the usefulness of EgoContact's annotated interactions for robotic manipulation: simply using EgoContact as the memory bank of an existing affordance-transfer policy improves grasp success from 14.2% to 31.2% in simulation and from 16% to 58% on a real robot.

</details>


### [53] [EmbodiExp: Compositional Experience Transfer for Embodied Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39180)
*Zhifeng Gu,Ying Chen,Rui Jiang,Lei Yi,Bing Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出 EmbodiExp 框架，将具身任务中的经验抽象为"以结果为锚的子目标经验"，包含子目标、适用条件、可适配策略与可验证结果，并在执行中在线组合，同时利用失败轨迹中的局部成功。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经验可帮助具身智能体把高层计划落地为具体动作，但跨任务迁移困难：以往有效策略可能不适用于当前状态，或无法产生预期结果。作者观察到不同任务常共享子目标，且子目标完成情况可独立于整体任务成败进行评估，因此可在子目标层面实现经验的复用与迁移。

Method: 1）将经验表示为"以结果为锚的子目标经验"，含四要素：子目标、适用条件、可适配策略、可验证结果；2）执行时，匹配适用条件的经验用于引导动作生成；3）已验证的结果可作为后续经验所需的前置条件，实现经验的在线组合；4）基于子目标级的结果归因实现失败利用：失败轨迹中仍可提取已验证的局部成功，以及针对特定条件"应避免何种动作"的指导。

Result: 在多个具身基准上，采用基于折分（fold-based）的协议并冻结经验库进行实验，验证了 EmbodiExp 的有效性。

Conclusion: 在子目标层面构建可验证、可组合的经验，是提升具身经验跨任务迁移性与利用率（包括利用失败数据）的可行路径；冻结经验库下的折分实验表明该方法具备稳定的泛化表现。

Abstract: Experience can help embodied agents turn high-level plans into grounded actions, but transferring it across tasks remains challenging: previously effective strategies may be inapplicable to the current state or fail to produce their intended outcomes. We present EmbodiExp, a framework for constructing transferable experiences and composing them online during embodied task execution. Our key observation is that tasks often share subgoals whose completion can be assessed independently of overall task success. We therefore introduce an outcome-grounded subgoal experience that specifies a subgoal, applicability conditions, an adaptable policy, and a verifiable outcome. During execution, applicable experiences guide action generation, while verified outcomes can establish the conditions required by subsequent experiences, enabling online composition. Subgoal-level outcome attribution further enables failure utilization: failed trajectories can still contribute verified local successes and condition-specific guidance on actions to avoid. Experiments on multiple embodied benchmarks under a fold-based protocol with frozen experience banks demonstrate the effectiveness of EmbodiExp.

</details>


### [54] [Event2Reward: Learning Robotic Rewards from Progress Relations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39212)
*Junnan Nie,Zitian Fan,Jiachen Zhang,Junyi Lao,Likui Zhang,Chenghao Liu,Xiaoyang Lu,Yunbo Wang,Yixue Zhang,Linbo Zhai,Jiayi Li,Mengzhe Wang,Xichang Wu,Bin Wang,Chang Liu,Tianle Zhang,Songfang Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Event2Reward，用绝对时间标签锚定进度尺度、用轨迹内相对进度关系约束局部变化，从约10万条真实机器人记录中训练进度模型并作为势函数做奖励塑形，在LIBERO四个套件40个任务上平均成功率提升18.16个百分点，真机上30分钟训练后三类精细插入任务达60.0%、三类物体操作任务达90.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常见做法把归一化的演示时间当作进度标签，隐含假设任务进度单调递增。但策略探索中进度可能停滞甚至回退，这类模型会把回退误判为进展，从而给导致进度损失的动作发放正塑形奖励。真实记录中的次优与失败执行虽有纠正价值，却难以给其状态标注绝对进度值。

Method: 关键观察是停滞、回退与恢复即使在绝对进度未知时也建立了局部的相对进度关系。据此用归一化时间标签锚定进度尺度（绝对监督），用轨迹内事件关系约束局部变化（相对监督），联合训练进度预测器；预测结果作为势函数用于奖励塑形。同时从约100K条原始真机执行记录（含成功、次优、失败）构建带事件标注的数据集，无需逐帧绝对进度标签。

Result: 相同训练预算下，LIBERO四个套件40个任务上平均策略成功率比仅用稀疏任务奖励的基线高18.16个百分点；真机上30分钟实际训练后，三个精细插入任务平均成功率60.0%、三个物体操作任务90.0%，分别比同预算稀疏奖励基线高36.7和23.3个百分点；消融显示绝对进度监督与相对进度关系互补，联合监督下游策略学习更好。

Conclusion: 绝对进度监督与轨迹内相对进度关系是互补的两类信号，联合使用可显著提升塑形奖励质量与下游策略学习效率；无需逐帧绝对标签即可从真实、含失败的执行数据中学习可用的进度模型。

Abstract: Learning task progress from robot demonstrations provides a practical way to construct dense shaping rewards for robotic reinforcement learning. A common approach uses normalized demonstration time as progress labels, implicitly assuming monotonically increasing task progress. During policy exploration, however, actual task progress can stall or even regress. Such models may mistake regressions for progress, assigning positive shaping rewards to actions that cause progress loss. Real suboptimal and failed executions offer corrective experience, but assigning absolute progress values to their states is difficult. Our key observation is that stalls, regressions, and recoveries establish local relative progress relations even when absolute progress is unknown. We introduce Event2Reward, which uses normalized temporal labels to anchor the progress scale and these within-trajectory relations to constrain local changes, combining absolute and relative progress supervision. Its progress predictions serve as potentials for reward shaping. We curate an event-annotated dataset from approximately 100K raw real-robot execution records, covering successful, suboptimal, and failed executions, without requiring framewise absolute progress labels. Under the same training budgets, Event2Reward improves average policy success by 18.16 percentage points over a baseline using only sparse task rewards across 40 tasks in four LIBERO suites. On real robots, average success after 30 minutes of wall-clock training reaches 60.0% on three precise insertion tasks and 90.0% on three object manipulation tasks, exceeding the sparse-reward baseline under the same budget by 36.7 and 23.3 percentage points, respectively. Ablation studies further show that absolute progress supervision and relative progress relations are complementary, with joint supervision yielding better downstream policy learning.

</details>


### [55] [RAIBO-Wagon: Real-Time Navigation System for a Legged Robot Towing a Wagon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39267)
*Insung Yang,Eunchae Kim,Jungwoo Hur,Seunghun Jeon,Donghyuk Choi,Jemin Hwangbo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种将条件流匹配（CFM）与基于学习前向动力学模型（FDM）的MPPI控制相结合的导航框架，用于四足机器人拖拽欠驱动挂车的受限空间自主导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有足式机器人研究多关注机器人对自身的控制，而机器人与外部物体（尤其是欠驱动物体）耦合后动力学更加复杂。拖车在狭窄空间的避障导航需要实时求解窄可行域内的路径，现有方法难以兼顾实时性与可行性。

Method: 1）用CFM模型生成轨迹，缓解MPPI的初始化难题并加速收敛；2）在学到的FDM上运行MPPI，通过迭代优化恢复超出CFM训练分布的可行解；3）将框架集成为RAIBO-Wagon（四足机器人+定制挂车）的全栈导航系统，全部感知与计算均机载完成。

Result: 在15米长的受限空间户外真实场景中完成验证，40次试验成功率85%，拖拽总载荷最高40 kg，接近RAIBO体重的100%。

Conclusion: CFM与FDM-based MPPI的互补组合可有效解决欠驱动耦合系统的受限空间实时导航问题，并在真实世界全机载条件下展现出较强的实用性与负载能力。

Abstract: Legged robots have demonstrated advanced capabilities across a wide range of real-world tasks, yet much of this progress concerns the robot controlling only itself. Once the robot is coupled to an external object -- particularly an underactuated one -- the dynamics of the combined assembly become more complex. Towing a wagon is a common practical scenario, where coupled dynamics make reliable navigation challenging. This difficulty is amplified in confined spaces, where collision-free navigation requires finding a narrow set of feasible paths under strict real-time constraints. To address this, we propose a navigation framework that combines Conditional Flow Matching (CFM) with Model Predictive Path Integral (MPPI) control over a learned Forward Dynamics Model (FDM). The CFM model mitigates the initialization problem of MPPI and accelerates its convergence, while MPPI with the FDM recovers feasible solutions beyond the CFM model's training distribution by iterative refinement. We integrate the framework into a full-stack navigation system for RAIBO-Wagon, a quadruped towing a custom wagon, and validate it in real-world outdoor experiments on 15 m-long courses through confined spaces, with all sensing and computation performed fully onboard. The system achieves an 85% success rate across 40 trials, towing a total load of up to 40 kg -- nearly 100% of RAIBO's body weight.

</details>


### [56] [CuBot-STT: Simultaneously Transport and Tighten for Lattice-type Modular Reconfiguration Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39279)
*Chongxi Meng,Da Zhao,Yanmin Zhou,Runjie Shen,Zhipeng Wang,Bin He*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 CuBot-STT，一种将模块搬运、接口锁紧与释放集成于单一外部机器人的晶格型模块化可重构机器人系统；立方模块提供结构与24V配电，齿轮传动通过单一外部输入驱动四个螺旋锁点并保留内部空间；实验验证了解锁接口法向/切向承载300N/490N、六模块电气连通，并自主完成11模块晶格重构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专用搬运机器人可去除模块级重构执行器，从而降低模块重量与成本；但现有系统要么连接容量低，要么依赖占用功能部件空间的内部紧固机器人，因此需要一种兼顾高承载、保留模块内部空间的外置一体化搬运与锁紧方案。

Method: 系统上，立方模块提供结构支撑与24V配电，每个面用齿轮传动将单一外部输入转化为四个螺旋锁点运动；机器人采用交替足支撑、抓取模块的夹爪与驱动接口的拧紧头，控制器通过连续约束转移协调双足支撑与模块锁紧。规划上将重定位代价分解为解析下界与由专家引导、目标条件Q学习得到的残差，并用接触面A*搜索把重定位序列展开为满足连通性、非对称操作与足部站姿约束的机器人基元。

Result: 实验显示接口法向与切向容量分别为300N和490N，六个模块间保持电气连通，并实现11模块晶格的自主重构；规划基准表明其搜索效率优于纯解析引导与双向启发式搜索，且无需微调即可泛化到超出训练规模的晶格。

Conclusion: CuBot-STT证明了外置单一机器人可同时完成模块搬运、接口锁紧与释放，在保留模块内部空间的同时获得较高接口承载能力，并通过解析与学习结合的规划方法实现高效、可泛化的晶格重构。

Abstract: Lattice-type modular reconfigurable robots rearrange standardized modules across tasks. Dedicated transport robots reduce module weight and cost by eliminating module level reconfiguration actuators. But existing systems use low capacity connections or internal fastening robots that occupy space for functional components. This paper presents CuBot-STT, which integrates module transport, interface locking, and release in one external robot. Cubic modules provide structural support and $24\,\mathrm{V}$ power distribution. On each face, a gear transmission converts one external input into motion of four helical locking points, preserving internal space. The robot uses alternating foot support, a gripper for module handling, and a tightening head for interface actuation. The controller coordinates two-foot support and module locking through continuous constraint transfers. The planner decomposes relocation costs into an analytical lower bound and a residual learned through expert-guided, goal conditioned Q-learning. Contact face $A^*$ search expands relocation sequences into robot primitives satisfying connectivity, asymmetric manipulation, and foot stance constraints. Experiments demonstrate normal and tangential interface capacities of $300\,\mathrm{N}$ and $490\,\mathrm{N}$, electrical continuity across six modules, and autonomous reconfiguration of an 11-module lattice. Planning benchmarks show superior search efficiency over analytical guidance and bidirectional heuristic search, with generalization beyond training lattice sizes without fine-tuning.

</details>


### [57] [Cross-Controller Transfer via In-Context Behavior Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39280)
*Jonathan Yang,Chelsea Finn,Dorsa Sadigh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种通过"行为交互上下文"编码控制器语义的框架，使单一策略能在无需冲突动作解释干扰的情况下跨本体与跨控制器迁移机器人数据，平均成功率提升33个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用机器人策略通常为每个机器人假定单一控制器，但不同控制器会对同一动作赋予不同物理含义，完成任务所需的策略也可能不同，这阻碍了跨控制器数据复用与迁移。

Method: 构建覆盖六类控制器及增益、柔顺性变化的设定；以"指令动作—实际机器人运动"示例对编码控制器下的行为交互，用Transformer上下文编码器对这些交互进行总结，使单一策略可在上下文内自适应。

Result: 真实实验中，相较朴素数据混合平均成功率提升33个百分点；在未见控制器模式上达50%成功率，未见控制器增益上达52%；在控制器专属数据有限的任务上也能迁移；在USB插入、表面擦拭等接触丰富任务中，策略能同时调整动作与策略。

Conclusion: 结果为利用跨多样动作解释所采集数据构建通用机器人策略提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Generalist robot policies increasingly learn from data collected across diverse tasks and embodiments, yet typically assume a single controller for each robot. This assumption limits transfer because different controllers can assign different physical meanings to the same action and may require different strategies to complete a task. We study how to transfer robot data across both embodiments and controllers without allowing conflicting action interpretations to interfere with learning. Our setting spans six controller types as well as variations in controller gains and compliance. We propose a framework that encodes behavioral interaction under a controller through examples of commanded actions and the resulting robot motions. A transformer context encoder summarizes these interactions, allowing a single policy to adapt in context. Across real-world experiments, our method improves average success by 33 percentage points over naive data pooling, achieves 50\% success on unseen controller modes and 52\% on unseen controller gains, and transfers to tasks with limited controller-specific data. On contact-rich tasks such as USB insertion and surface wiping, behavioral interaction context enables the policy to adapt not only its actions but also its strategy. Our results provide a path toward generalist robot policies that can leverage data collected across diverse action interpretations. For more information, visit \href{https://controller-transfer.github.io}{https://controller-transfer.github.io}.

</details>


### [58] [OpenFlyV2: An onboard vision-language-action model for aerial perception and navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39287)
*Junli Liu,Zhongrui You,Chenhui Li,Zhen Li,Yuyang Tian,Qizhen Chen,Delin Qu,Haoming Song,Dong Wang,Xuelong Li,Zhigang Wang,Bin Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出OpenFlyV2，一个统一的机载视觉-语言-动作架构，用共享视觉-语言表征同时支撑语义理解、语言引导感知、空间推理与连续飞行控制，在Jetson Orin NX上以10Hz机载生成12个航点的动作块，零样本迁移到真实四旋翼，在四个真实环境中达到48%导航成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类认知基于共享的感知基础完成理解环境、注意所指实体、空间推理与行动等多种功能；而现有空中机器人系统往往只针对单一能力专门化、依赖机外计算，或无法在资源受限平台上持续闭环运行，缺乏类似的功能性组织。

Method: 1) 统一机载VLA架构：以共享视觉-语言表征支撑语义理解、语言引导感知、空间推理与连续空中控制；2) 紧凑的动作专家将共享多模态上下文转换为短时域飞行航点；3) 引入运动感知时序记忆与动作先验以降低闭环执行的计算开销；4) 构建仿真数据集，约7万条导航轨迹与6万条视觉-语言指令-响应对；5) 以12航点为一个动作块，在NVIDIA Jetson Orin NX上全机载10Hz运行。

Result: 模型在Jetson Orin NX上实现10Hz全机载闭环飞行；无任何真实空中训练数据情况下从仿真零样本迁移到真实四旋翼，在四个真实环境中取得48%导航成功率；结果表明广泛视觉-语言能力与连续闭环飞行可在资源受限空中平台上共存于单一模型。

Conclusion: OpenFlyV2证明统一的多模态功能组织可落地于机载资源受限平台，兼具视觉-语言泛化能力与实时闭环控制，代码将于https://github.com/Ideal-ljl/AeroBrain 公开。

Abstract: Human cognition flexibly draws on a shared perceptual foundation to understand environments, attend to referenced entities, reason about spatial relationships, and act. Endowing aerial robots with an analogous functional organization remains challenging, as existing systems are often specialized for individual capabilities, dependent on offboard computation, or unable to sustain closed-loop operation on resource-constrained platforms. We present OpenFlyV2, a unified onboard vision-language-action architecture that uses a shared vision-language representation to support semantic understanding, language-grounded perception, spatial reasoning, and continuous aerial control within a single model. A compact action expert converts the shared multimodal context into short-horizon flight waypoints, while motion-aware temporal memory and action priors reduce the computational cost of closed-loop execution. We construct \dataset{}, a simulation-based dataset comprising approximately 70,000 navigation trajectories and 60,000 vision-language instruction--response pairs. OpenFlyV2 generates action chunks, each comprising 12 waypoints, entirely onboard at 10 Hz using an NVIDIA Jetson Orin NX. It transfers zero-shot from simulation to physical quadrotor flight, achieving a 48\% navigation success rate across four real-world environments without real-world aerial training data. Extensive results demonstrate that broad vision-language competence and continuous closed-loop flight can coexist within a single model on a resource-constrained aerial platform. Code will be made publicly available at https://github.com/Ideal-ljl/AeroBrain.

</details>


### [59] [Language-Grounded Force/Torque Pretraining for Extrinsic Contact Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39314)
*Kazuki Takahashi,Hiroaki Murakami,Ryota Sakuma,Mitsuhiro Kamezaki,Matthew Ishige,Yoshihiro Kawahara*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出将双臂六轴力/力矩历史信号与冻结的语言空间对齐进行预训练，获得语义结构化的物理状态表征，可支持对动作、物体重量、抓取交互的读出，并能零样本识别接触与支撑状态以及对新接触事件做高效适配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精细操作中机器人需感知被抓物体是否与外界发生或改变接触，但外在接触难以通过视觉或局部接触几何直接观测。力/力矩信号蕴含该信息，却会随物体重量、抓取构型与机器人运动而变化，因此尚不清楚其能否提供可复用的物理状态表征。

Method: 利用仿真抓取-放置交互生成的监督信号，将双臂六轴力/力矩历史与冻结的语言空间对齐，预训练出语义结构化的物理表征；在仿真未见物体上用文本原型读出动作、重量与抓取交互；真实放置任务中采用无标签校准实现接触与支撑状态识别且不更新编码器；对预训练未包含的连接器插入任务，使用低秩适配进行高效微调。

Result: 仿真未见物体上，表征支持动作、物体重量与抓取交互的文本原型读出；真实放置中无标签校准实现接触与支撑状态识别，优于垂直力符号反转规则基线；连接器插入任务上低秩适配相比从头训练显著提升数据效率。

Conclusion: 力/力矩历史信号可支持对已知物理状态的直接读出，并能高效适配到新的外在接触事件，说明其可作为精细操作中可复用的物理状态表征。

Abstract: Fine manipulation requires robots to recognize when a grasped object makes or changes contact with its environment, yet such extrinsic contact is not always directly observable from vision or local contact geometry. Force and torque signals carry this information, but also vary with object weight, grasp configuration, and robot motion, leaving it unclear whether they can provide a reusable representation of physical state. Here we show that bilateral six-axis force/torque histories can be pretrained as a semantically structured physical representation by aligning them with a frozen language space using supervision generated from simulated pick-and-place interactions. On unseen objects in simulation, the learned representation supports text-prototype readout of robot action, object weight, and grasp interaction. In real-world placement, unlabeled calibration enables contact and support-state recognition without updating the encoder, outperforming a vertical-force sign-reversal rule baseline. For connector insertion, an event absent from pretraining, low-rank adaptation improves data-efficient learning over training from scratch. These results show that force/torque histories can support both direct readout of known physical states and efficient adaptation to new extrinsic-contact events.

</details>


### [60] [ProLoop: Progress-Aware Closed-Loop Framework via Structured State Relations for Long-Horizon Robotic Task Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39319)
*Runxin Zou,Qingmiao Liang,Jiatao Zhang,Xinyan Lv,Liangyu Qie,Meiyi Bu,Wei Song,Shiqiang Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ProLoop——一个进度感知的闭环框架，通过显式推断子目标进度（进行中/偏离/完成）来指导长程机器人任务执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程机器人任务通常被分解为语义子目标以简化规划，但基于LLM的规划中子目标进度往往只能从交互历史中隐式推断，估计不准确会误导执行并使局部错误不断传播。

Method: 从期望状态与执行状态之间的结构化关系显式推导子目标进度；使用可学习的状态关系监视器（State-Relation Monitor）评估这些关系，并聚合为子目标进度（进行中、偏离、完成）；所得进度指导执行适配，实现进度感知的闭环控制。

Result: 在AgentBoard ALFWorld和真实机器人平台上，整体成功率提升12.94%，Hard任务上提升15.46%，同时环境交互次数减少32.3%。

Conclusion: 显式的进度建模与闭环反馈可有效缓解隐式进度估计带来的误差累积，提升长程机器人任务的成功率与执行效率。

Abstract: Long-horizon robotic tasks are commonly decomposed into semantic subgoals to simplify planning. However, subgoal progress in LLM-based planning is typically inferred implicitly from interaction histories. Inaccurate estimates can misguide execution and allow local errors to propagate. To address this, we propose ProLoop, a Progress-Aware Closed-Loop framework that derives explicit subgoal progress from structured relations between desired and execution states. A learned State-Relation Monitor assesses these relations, which are then aggregated into subgoal progress as ongoing, deviated, or completed. The resulting progress guides execution adaptation, enabling reliable progress-aware control throughout execution. Experiments on AgentBoard ALFWorld and a real-world robotic platform show that ProLoop improves success rate by 12.94% overall and by 15.46% on Hard tasks, while reducing environment interactions by 32.3%.

</details>


### [61] [SONIC-K1: Porting a Universal Humanoid Motion Tracker to a Small Humanoid on a Single GPU](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39327)
*Kyrylo Kolesnichenko,Jong-Hoon Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 SONIC-K1，首个面向 Booster K1 人形机器人的开源权重全身运动跟踪器，在单张 GPU 小算力预算下从零训练，仿真跟踪几乎全部留出动作片段，真机上上半身与慢速动作表现良好，但迈步与转向可靠性不足，并对动作延迟敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将 SONIC 框架适配到更小型的人形机器人 Booster K1，验证在低算力预算下能否获得可用的全身运动跟踪能力，并公开代码、权重与评测工具以推动复现与对比。

Method: 把 SONIC 框架适配至 Booster K1，在单张共享 GPU 上从零训练跟踪策略，算力仅为 SONIC 报告值的一小部分；在 MuJoCo 中按 SONIC 的成功判据在留出动作片段与外部基准上评测，并在真实 K1 上进行部署测试，另开展仿真实验分析动作延迟敏感性。

Result: 仿真中可跟踪几乎所有留出动作片段，并在外部基准上表现良好；真实 K1 上上半身与慢速动作可跟踪，迈步与转向较不可靠；仿真实验显示对动作延迟存在敏感性。

Conclusion: 在适度训练预算下即可获得较广的全身运动跟踪能力，主要下一步是做定量的真机硬件评测；代码、策略权重与评测工具已公开。

Abstract: We present SONIC-K1, to our knowledge the first open-weights whole-body motion tracker for the Booster K1 humanoid, obtained by adapting the SONIC framework to this smaller robot. The policy is trained from scratch on a single shared GPU with a small fraction of the compute reported for SONIC. In MuJoCo simulation, it tracks nearly all held-out motion clips and performs well on external benchmarks under SONIC's success criterion. On the real K1, it tracks upper-body and slow motions, while stepping and turning remain less reliable, and simulation experiments show a sensitivity to action latency. These results show broad motion-tracking capability on a modest training budget and point to quantitative hardware evaluation as the main next step. Code, policy weights and evaluation tools are publicly available.

</details>


### [62] [Utility-WAM: Correcting Future-Induced Action Changes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39367)
*Feihong Zhang,Xi Lin,Yulong Shi,Zuxing Lu,Yanghong Mei,Zhaopeng Gu,Tingyu Yuan,Zihao Liang,Yinuo Wang,Zhaowen Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文分析"世界动作模型"(WAM)中预测未来视觉对动作的真实影响，发现其带来"未来诱导负迁移"的集中失效模式，并提出 Utility-WAM 进行原位纠偏与门控加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WAM 能以生成的视觉未来为条件驱动机器人动作，但这种额外上下文既非免费也非总是有益。现有聚合基准指标掩盖了未来条件带来的收益与代价分布不均的问题，作者希望厘清"预测未来到底改变了什么"。

Method: 以同一 WAM 检查点下"有未来条件"与"无未来条件"执行路径的动作差 Δa 为分析对象；提出 Utility-WAM：(1) 在冻结共享 WAM 上训练的残差适配器，将未来诱导的速度差对齐到真实动态引导，并对超出无未来基线的额外误差施加惩罚；(2) 轻量级生成前门控，复用反事实动作结果，选择性跳过视频生成。

Result: Δa 对平均成功率几乎无影响（LIBERO +0.8 点，RoboTwin 2.0 -0.8 点），却使推理延迟增加 1.7–1.9 倍；长程任务受益（LIBERO-Long +2.4 点），但精度任务严重退化（Press Stapler -54.0、Place Can Basket -34.0 点）。残差适配器将损失降至 6.0 与 14.0 点并保留长程前瞻；门控在 RoboTwin 跳过 75.5% 查询、延迟降低 38%，平均成功率提升至 RoboTwin 2.0 的 93.3%、LIBERO 的 98.9%。真实双臂任务中 60 次试验成功 53 次，FAST-WAM 为 43 次。

Conclusion: 预测未来视觉的价值高度任务相关：长程任务获益、精度任务受害，需针对性纠偏而非整体采用或弃用；Utility-WAM 同时实现了失效修复与计算削减，并在仿真与真实平台上验证有效。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) can condition robot actions on generated visual futures, but this additional context is neither cost-free nor uniformly beneficial. We examine what a predicted future actually changes: the action delta $Δa$ between the future-conditioned and future-free execution paths of the same model. Under a shared WAM checkpoint, this delta barely shifts average benchmark success ($+0.8$ points on LIBERO, $-0.8$ on RoboTwin~2.0), while generating visual futures increases inference latency by 1.7--1.9$\times$. These aggregate metrics conceal a concentrated failure mode that we term \emph{future-induced negative transfer}: while predictive deltas benefit long-horizon tasks ($+2.4$ points on LIBERO-Long) and remain negligible on most routine manipulations, they induce severe degradation on precision tasks---with \emph{Press Stapler} and \emph{Place Can Basket} losing 54.0 and 34.0 points, recurring as top failure modes across independently trained WAM variants. We introduce \textbf{Utility-WAM} for in-place correction of future-conditioned control. Its primary learning component is a residual adapter that repairs future-induced velocity deltas under a frozen shared WAM by aligning them with ground-truth dynamic guidance and penalizing excess error beyond the future-free baseline. The adapter selectively eliminates the majority of severe task drops (reducing losses to 6.0 and 14.0 points) while preserving long-horizon foresight. To eliminate superfluous video computation, a lightweight pre-generation gate reuses counterfactual action outcomes to selectively bypass video generation on 75.5\% of RoboTwin queries, cutting latency by 38\% while improving average success to 93.3\% on RoboTwin~2.0 and 98.9\% on LIBERO. On physical bimanual manipulation across three real-world tasks, Utility-WAM succeeds in 53 of 60 trials, compared to 43 for FAST-WAM.

</details>


### [63] [Information-Driven Collaborative Autonomy for Heterogeneous UAV/UGV Teams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39379)
*Joshua Yuriv Pilipovsky*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文研究无人机(UAV)与无人地面车(UGV)异构编队在含有未知数量潜在危险目标的区域内的协同规划问题，将空中搜索建模为遍历控制，用PMB近似目标后验，并以嵌套SCvx*-C-ADMM实现完全分布式的空地闭环协同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空地异构编队在未知、可能含危险目标的环境中作业时，需要在信息获取（探索与目标定位）和地面行进安全性之间取得平衡；现有方法难以同时处理动态相机视场、仅测角测量与数据关联不确定性、非凸非线性动力学以及多智能体分布式协同等挑战。

Method: 空中部分采用遍历控制，按空间信息密度分配传感努力，并纳入动态相机视场足迹与位姿相关的概率检测模型；仅测角测量经贝叶斯推断并对数据关联假设做边缘化，得到目标后验的Poisson多伯努利(PMB)近似（同时表示已检测与未检测目标），以引导空中感知；地面规划将基于置信集的机会约束目标规避与"空洞概率"目标结合；两编队均用嵌套SCvx*–C-ADMM（外层序列凸规划、内层分布式共识优化）以完全去中心化方式求解，处理非线性、不可分离目标与连续时间智能体间距约束；闭环方案随信息更新双方轨迹，并在剩余路线风险低于阈值时放行地面运动。

Result: 数值案例研究考察了感知几何与目标不确定性，比较了去中心化与集中式求解器，并展示了多UAV与多UGV的完整闭环协同。

Conclusion: 所提出的遍历感知—PMB信念更新—机会约束地面规划—分布式嵌套优化框架能够实现空地异构编队的闭环协同搜索与安全通行，去中心化求解具备可行性与有效性。

Abstract: This paper considers coordinating a heterogeneous team of unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) and unmanned ground vehicles (UGVs) traversing a domain containing an unknown number of potentially hazardous targets. The UAVs provide aerial sensing using onboard optical cameras, adapting their trajectories to ground routes and target information. We formulate the aerial search problem using ergodic control, which distributes sensing effort according to a spatial information density to balance exploration and target localization. The formulation incorporates dynamic camera field-of-view footprints and pose-dependent probabilistic detection models. Angles-only measurements are processed through Bayesian inference and marginalization over data-association hypotheses to obtain a Poisson multi-Bernoulli (PMB) approximation of the target posterior, representing both detected and undetected targets. This belief directs aerial sensing toward unexplored regions along the ground routes and viewpoints that improve target localization. Ground planning combines chance-constrained target avoidance, enforced through confidence sets, with a void-probability objective that favors routes unlikely to contain threats. We solve each team's optimization problem in a fully decentralized manner using a nested SCvx*--C-ADMM scheme, with outer sequential convex programming iterations and inner distributed consensus optimization, accounting for nonlinearities, nonseparable objectives, and continuous-time inter-agent separation constraints. A closed-loop scheme updates both fleets' trajectories as information becomes available and permits ground motion when the remaining route risk falls below a prescribed threshold. Numerical case studies examine sensing geometry and target uncertainty, compare decentralized and centralized solvers, and demonstrate full closed-loop collaboration between multiple UAVs and UGVs.

</details>


### [64] [UMI-Live for In-The-Wild Bimanual Robotics Data Collection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39385)
*Govind Velamoor,Adrian Lin,Sam Jung,Spencer Mateega,Brandon Y. Yang,Amish Sethi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 UMI-Live，一套开源的全身（双臂）UMI 数据采集全栈系统，集成相机与 LiDAR-惯性传感、板载计算与供电，无需外部跟踪设备或环境标定，并配套异步 SLAM 重建流水线、双臂 YAM 遥操作与 VLA 策略训练/推理软件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 UMI 设备虽可让专家无需遥操作真实机器人即可演示任务，但将演示数据用于训练仍依赖一致的硬件配置、标定与数据处理，门槛较高，缺乏一套可野外采集、开箱即用的完整方案。

Method: 设计定制 UMI-Live 硬件，将相机与 LiDAR-惯性传感、板载计算与电源一体化，采集时无需外部跟踪设备与场地标定；提供简洁的录制界面，由异步流水线并行完成 SLAM 重建并输出可直接训练的数据库；同时开源支持双臂 YAM 遥操作以及 VLA 策略训练与推理的软件。

Result: 通过微调 π_{0.5} 与 MolmoAct2 验证数据质量，在双臂抓取放置任务上分别取得 10/10 与 8/10 的成功率；并开源三项任务共 40 小时的 UMI-Live 数据，以及完整硬件设计、软件与复现指南。

Conclusion: UMI-Live 以全栈开源方式降低了野外双臂机器人数据采集与训练的门槛，实证表明其数据可直接支撑 VLA 策略的有效训练，具备良好的可复现性与推广价值。

Abstract: Universal Manipulation Interface (UMI) devices allow human experts to demonstrate tasks for robot learning without teleoperating physical robots. Using these demonstrations as training data still requires a consistent hardware setup, calibration, and data processing. We present UMI-Live, a full-stack open-source bimanual UMI system for in-the-wild data collection. We design our custom UMI-Live hardware to integrate camera and LiDAR--inertial sensing, onboard compute, and power, without external tracking equipment or calibration within the collection environment. We implement a simple user interface for recording data while an asynchronous pipeline performs parallel SLAM reconstruction and publishes training-ready datasets. We also release software that supports dual-YAM teleoperation and VLA policy training and inference. We validate UMI-Live data quality by fine-tuning $π_{0.5}$ and MolmoAct2, achieving success rates of 10/10 and 8/10, respectively on a bimanual pick-and-place task. Furthermore, we release 40 hours of UMI-Live data across three tasks, and complete hardware designs, software, and reproduction guides at our $\href{https://umi-live.github.io/}{\text{project website}}$.

</details>


### [65] [Decoding Geometry, Not Tokens: Efficient VLMs for Robot Affordance and Trajectory Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39416)
*Akila Ayanthi,Darshana Priyasad,Tharindu Fernando,Dimity Miller,Sridha Sridharan,Clinton Fookes,Peyman Moghadam*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 小型VLM中间层隐状态已含丰富空间信息，作者用冻结骨干+轻量几何预测头直接回归抓取框与轨迹点，替代自回归文本解码，在精度与效率上大幅优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 抓取位置与运动轨迹预测本质上是几何问题，但现有视觉语言模型把空间坐标转成文本 token 再自回归解码，忽略了空间结构、截断了连续精度，且多 token 解码时误差累积。作者想探究瓶颈究竟在解码方式还是特征本身。

Method: 作者以0.5–2B参数的现成小型VLM为对象，诊断发现中间隐藏层已编码丰富空间信息。于是设计一个轻量、几何兼容的预测头，附加在完全冻结的骨干上：从中间隐藏层读取特征，以任务指令调制一个可学习query，再通过视觉token上的交叉注意力进行精炼，从而把affordance框和轨迹路点直接回归为连续值，不修改骨干参数。在ShareRobot-Bench与AGD20K上评测。

Result: 2B变体相对自回归基线将轨迹误差降低逾60%，ShareRobot-Bench上affordance mAP提升85%；轨迹预测上以0.251对0.191的DFD接近RoboBrain，同时吞吐量高3.8倍。

Conclusion: 自回归解码是轨迹预测的主要瓶颈，而affordance预测还额外依赖特征深度与解码器结构；冻结骨干加轻量回归头的方案在指令条件下的二维几何预测中取得了良好的精度—效率折中。

Abstract: Predicting where to grasp and how to move is fundamentally a geometry problem. Vision-language models treat it as language generation instead. They tokenize spatial coordinates into text and decode them autoregressively. This mechanism ignores spatial structure and truncates continuous precision. Errors compound across coordinate tokens during decoding. In this paper, we show that compact off-the-shelf VLMs with 0.5-2B parameters already encode rich spatial information in their intermediate hidden states. Autoregressive decoding is the dominant bottleneck for trajectory prediction, while affordance prediction depends additionally on feature depth and decoder architecture. We expose the latent spatial information with a lightweight, geometry-compatible prediction head that attaches to a frozen backbone. It reads features from an intermediate hidden layer, conditions a learned query on the task instruction and refines it via cross-attention over the visual tokens to regress affordance boxes and trajectory waypoints directly as continuous values, without modifying the backbone. We evaluate on ShareRobot-Bench and AGD20K. Our 2B variant reduces trajectory error by over 60% and improves ShareRobot-Bench affordance mAP by 85% relative to the autoregressive baseline. It approaches RoboBrain on trajectory prediction (0.251 vs 0.191 DFD) at 3.8x higher throughput. These results demonstrate a favorable accuracy-efficiency trade-off for instruction-conditioned 2D geometric prediction.

</details>


### [66] [ScheduleGen: Demonstration Generation via Skill Scheduling for Multi-Arm Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39429)
*Neel Jawale,Stephan Pleines,Soha Pouya,Yuke Zhu,Ajay Mandlekar,Caelan Garrett*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 ScheduleGen，一种面向多臂机器人演示生成的集成规划与调度算法，通过显式考虑多臂约束解决现有生成方法产生不安全/失败演示的问题，在28个仿真任务变体上取得79.6%的数据生成成功率和65.5%的策略成功率，并实现零样本仿真到真实迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双臂与类人等多臂平台相比单臂系统具有更短的任务周期，但因多臂约束的存在而更难安全控制。训练通用多臂策略需要大规模数据集，而真实数据采集成本高、难以规模化，成为瓶颈。自动演示生成可用少量源演示合成新演示来缓解该负担，但现有多臂演示生成算法忽略多臂约束，导致生成的行为不安全且频繁失败。

Method: 提出 ScheduleGen，一个将规划（planning）与调度（scheduling）集成的多臂演示生成算法，在生成过程中显式建模并满足多臂约束（如避免臂间干涉、时序与资源冲突等），从而从少量源演示合成安全且可执行的新演示，用于训练通用多臂操作策略。

Result: 在28个仿真任务变体上评估，包括7个外部作者编写的 RoboDojo 任务以及先前系统无法处理的情景；平均数据生成成功率达79.6%，据此训练的策略平均成功率为65.5%。ScheduleGen 训练的策略优于基于最先进数据生成基线训练的策略，并在8个真实世界变体上通过零样本 sim-to-real 与 real-to-sim-to-real 迁移达到50–80%的成功率。

Conclusion: 将多臂约束纳入演示生成的规划与调度框架，可显著提升自动演示生成的安全性与成功率，并转化为更强的多臂策略；该方法在仿真与真实场景、包括需要双向迁移的设置中均得到验证，为规模化多臂操作数据生成提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Multi-arm robot platforms, including bimanual and humanoid robots, have the capability to complete tasks with lower cycle times than single-arm systems, but they are more difficult to safely control due to the presence of multi-arm constraints. The paradigm of training generalist robot policies from massive datasets is a promising approach to address general-purpose multi-arm manipulation, but the acquisition of large multi-arm manipulation datasets remains a bottleneck. Automated demonstration generation can alleviate this burden by synthesizing new demonstrations from a handful of source demonstrations, but existing algorithms ignore multi-arm constraints and as a result often induce unsafe and unsuccessful behaviors. We address this limitation by introducing ScheduleGen, an integrated planning & scheduling algorithm for multi-arm demonstration generation. We evaluate ScheduleGen on 28 simulation task variants, including seven externally authored RoboDojo tasks and settings inaccessible to prior systems; it achieves 79.6% mean data-generation success and trains policies with 65.5% mean success. ScheduleGen policies outperform those trained on a state-of-the-art data generation baseline, and achieve 50-80% success across eight real-world variants through zero-shot sim-to-real and real-to-sim-to-real transfer. Videos and more at: https://schedulegen.github.io/

</details>


### [67] [Ask to Evolve: A Unified Harness for Lifelong Embodied Navigation with Hybrid Action Space and Persistent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39446)
*Kai Sheng,Liuyi Wang,Jinlong Li,Haojie Dai,Chengju Liu,Qijun Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EvoLife：统一导航框架，融合混合动作空间、主动交互与持久空间记忆，在8个基准、4类任务上实现高成功率导航并支持经验积累复用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 让具身智能体自主行动、主动发起交互并通过经验理解环境，需要动作、交互与记忆三者协同，使每次经历既能指导当前决策又能为未来任务积累经验，而现有方法往往割裂处理这三者。

Method: 提出EvoLife统一导航框架，供多模态具身智能体自主选择如何移动、何时求助、携带何种经验。其混合动作空间支持路点导航、局部离散调整、像素级导航与目标导向导航；主动交互机制使智能体在遇到歧义或困难时主动请求澄清或协助；持久空间记忆将观测与对话绑定到具体地点，保留有效经验以指导后续重访。

Result: 在视觉语言导航、物体导航、交互式实例导航与连续多任务导航四类任务共8个基准上评测。每回合平均仅1.87次主动交互，在覆盖VLN与物体导航的四个100回合测试集上首次到访成功率达100%；完整序列SR在LHPR-VLN 50条序列上达98%、GOAT-Bench 50条序列上达94%；R2R重访设置下SPL从82.23%提升至93.31%；多日物理导航实验进一步展示了探索、主动求助与重访记忆地点的能力。

Conclusion: 结果表明，通过自主行动、主动交互以及经验的积累与复用，可推动具身智能体在物理世界中"活起来"的愿景，验证了动作—交互—记忆统一框架的有效性。

Abstract: Giving embodied agents the freedom to act autonomously, initiate interactions, and understand their surroundings through experience is a step toward bringing them to life in the physical world. Realizing this vision requires action, interaction, and memory to work together, so that each encounter can inform current decisions and contribute experience for future tasks. We present EvoLife, a unified navigation harness that enables a multimodal embodied agent to choose how to move, when to ask, and what experience to carry forward. Its hybrid action space supports waypoint navigation, local discrete adjustments, pixel-based navigation, and object-directed navigation. Active interaction allows the agent to seek clarification or assistance when it encounters ambiguity or difficulty, while persistent spatial memory binds observations and dialogue to places, retaining useful experience to guide subsequent visits. We evaluate eight benchmarks across four task families: vision-language navigation, object navigation, interactive instance navigation, and continuous multi-task navigation. With an average of only 1.87 active interactions per episode, our method achieves a first-visit success rate (SR) of 100% across four 100-episode sets spanning vision-language navigation and object navigation. Complete-sequence SR reaches 98% on 50 LHPR-VLN sequences and 94% on 50 GOAT-Bench sequences. In the R2R revisit setting, success weighted by path length (SPL) increases from 82.23% to 93.31% with retained experience. Multi-day physical navigation further demonstrates exploration, proactive help-seeking, and revisiting remembered places. Together, these results advance the vision of bringing embodied agents to life through autonomous action, proactive interaction, and the accumulation and reuse of experience.

</details>


### [68] [PRIME W0: Physical Priors from World Models for Embodied Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39528)
*Huabin Peng,Fagang Jin,Yancong Wei,Tengfei Ma,Chuhang Liu,Chao Yu,Liqing Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 PRIME W0，一个分层条件流匹配框架，通过逐层 KV 注入、异步世界模型子目标预测与历史帧语义桥接、以及协方差结构化噪声的流匹配三方面设计，把长时序上下文与 VLM 分层结构重新引入 VLA 策略，并在 LIBERO 与真机基准上取得 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 模型大多是前馈式的：动作仅以当前观测为条件，丢弃了长时序时间上下文，也没有利用 VLM 表征中的层级结构，导致长程任务表现受限。

Method: 三项协同设计：(1) 将预训练 VLM 的逐层 KV 注入自适应 MoE 动作专家；(2) 动力学感知条件化，包括异步世界模型子目标图像预测与 DINOv2 历史帧语义桥接；(3) 动作动态流形（ADM）流匹配，采用协方差结构化噪声以建模机器人运动学耦合。训练分四阶段：VLM 预训练、DAT/NTP 视觉运动预训练、带世界模型注入的 DiT 流匹配解码器组装、端到端 SFT。

Result: 在 LIBERO 与真机基准上达到当时最优性能，并通过系统性消融实验验证了各架构组件的有效性。

Conclusion: 将长时序上下文、世界模型子目标与运动学结构化生成统一到分层条件流匹配框架中，可有效提升 VLA 指令跟随与操作性能，各组件均有明确贡献。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models excel at instruction following, yet most remain feed-forward: they condition actions only on the current observation and discard both long-horizon temporal context and the hierarchical structure of VLM representations. We present PRIME W0, a hierarchical conditional flow-matching framework that closes this gap through three synergistic designs: (1)per-layer KV injection from a pretrained VLM into an Adaptive MoE action expert; (2)dynamics-aware conditioning via asynchronous world-model subgoal-image prediction and a DINOv2 historical-frame semantic bridge; and (3)Action Dynamic Manifold (ADM) flow matching with covariance-structured noise that respects robot kinematic coupling. PRIME W0 is trained in four stages---VLM pre-training, DAT/NTP visuomotor pre-training, DiT flow-matching decoder assembly with WM injection, and end-to-end SFT---and achieves state-of-the-art results on LIBERO and real-robot benchmarks, with systematic ablations validating each architectural component.

</details>


### [69] [From Reasoning to Action: Consequence-Grounded Alignment for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39543)
*Yixue Zhang,Wenke Xia,Kun Wu,Wenbo Yu,Pei Ren,Junnan Nie,Di Hu,Zhengping Che,Chang Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CoRAct 是一个"后果锚定"的推理到动作对齐框架，通过在动作噪声空间中进行奖励引导优化，显式对齐具身思维链（ECoT）与动作生成，在 RoboCasa、VLABench 及真实机器人任务上优于强 ECoT-VLA 基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 模型虽引入具身思维链（ECoT）来引导动作生成，但这种引导是隐式的：动作只是"以 ECoT 为条件"生成，并未被显式优化以与推理保持一致，导致推理与执行之间存在错位。

Method: 提出 CoRAct：1）将两类物理化 ECoT 组件转为可验证信号——movement 表示意图运动，waypoints 编码期望的空间后果；2）用统一的 Mixture-of-Transformers 联合建模 ECoT 推理、动作生成与动作条件下的未来预测；3）从采样噪声生成候选动作，依据运动一致性以及预测结果与指定 waypoints 的一致性计算对齐奖励；4）用这些奖励进行组相对（group-relative）优化，直接更新初始噪声分布，使动作生成朝与意图动作和预期后果一致的方向偏移。

Result: 在 RoboCasa、VLABench 和真实世界操作任务上，相比强 ECoT-based VLA 基线，任务成功率、泛化能力和推理-执行对齐程度均有提升；此外 CoRAct 能把外部提供的 ECoT 落地为可执行行为，借助强大视觉语言模型的知识实现对新场景的零样本适应。

Conclusion: 将 ECoT 从"隐式条件"转变为"可验证的奖励信号"并对动作噪声做奖励引导优化，是提升 VLA 推理-执行一致性的有效途径，同时为外部推理知识注入真实机器人控制提供了可行接口。

Abstract: Reasoning informs decision-making by connecting intended actions with their expected consequences. Recent vision-language-action (VLA) models incorporate embodied chains of thought (ECoTs) to guide action generation. However, such guidance remains implicit, as actions are conditioned on ECoTs rather than explicitly optimized to align with them.
  We introduce CoRAct, a consequence-grounded reasoning-to-action alignment framework that explicitly aligns reasoning and action through reward-guided optimization in action noise space. CoRAct formulates two physically grounded ECoT components as verifiable signals: movement specifies intended motion, while waypoints encode expected spatial consequences. A unified Mixture-of-Transformers jointly models ECoT reasoning, action generation, and action-conditioned future prediction. For candidate actions generated from sampled noise, CoRAct computes alignment rewards based on movement consistency and agreement between predicted outcomes and specified waypoints. These rewards guide group-relative optimization of the initial noise distribution, steering action generation toward consistency with intended actions and expected consequences.
  Experiments across RoboCasa, VLABench, and real-world manipulation tasks demonstrate improvements in task success, generalization, and reasoning-execution alignment over strong ECoT-based VLA baselines. CoRAct also grounds externally provided ECoTs in executable behavior, enabling zero-shot adaptation to novel scenarios by leveraging knowledge from powerful vision-language models.
  Project page and dataset: https://onlyxingxingzyx.github.io/CoRAct/

</details>


### [70] [Assuring Learning-Enabled Cyber-Physical Systems with Foundation Models: A Neuro-Symbolic Agenda for Testing, Verification, and Runtime Assurance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39547)
*Xi Zheng,Ivan Ruchkin,Hong Jin Kang,Tevfik Bultan,Vijay Ganesh,Taylor T. Johnson,Ziyang Li,Lei Ma,Aloysius K. Mok,Daniel Neider,Ruzica Piskac,Nalini Venkatasubramanian,Simon Thompson,Sanjoy Baruah*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出"基础模型保障栈"（Foundation Model Assurance Stack），主张对集成基础模型的学习使能信息物理系统从模型中心评估转向系统级保障，通过神经符号架构、语义接地的符合性测试、组合式验证与运行时保障三条路径，以符号抽象作为随机学习与形式推理之间的保障边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶汽车、无人机等学习使能CPS正越来越多地集成基础模型，但其保障实践是碎片化的，无法支撑系统级保证；需要在随机神经网络、符号与物理边界之间恢复工程控制，因而亟需从模型中心评估转向系统级保障。
