Method: 提出由三部分构成的基础模型保障栈：(i) 神经符号架构，用显式符号世界模型、推理层与接口约束基础模型；(ii) 语义接地的符合性测试，把形式规约和符号世界模型与基础模型辅助的规约挖掘、场景生成、测试合成结合；(iii) 组合式验证与运行时保障，针对形式可处理的组件与接口，采用模块化契约、等价性检查、经校验的合成、不确定性感知推理以及运行时监控与执行。其中符号抽象建立保障边界，使基础模型仅作为受约束的助手，其输出在可形式化规定正确性的地方被独立检查。

Result: 给出了贯穿系统生命周期的测试、验证与运行时证据的衔接框架，并识别出规约工程、抽象保真度、证据组合与生命周期认证四方面的研究挑战。

Conclusion: 文章提出分阶段议程，指向可信、可部署且可认证的学习使能信息物理系统。

Abstract: Learning-enabled cyber-physical systems (CPS), including autonomous vehicles and UAVs, increasingly incorporate foundation models, yet fragmented assurance practices provide insufficient support for system-level guarantees. We argue that restoring engineering control across stochastic neural, symbolic, and physical boundaries requires a shift from model-centric evaluation to system-level assurance. We introduce the Foundation Model Assurance Stack, connecting three directions: (i) neuro-symbolic architectures that constrain foundation models through explicit symbolic world models, reasoning layers, and interfaces; (ii) semantically grounded, conformance-based testing that combines formal specifications and symbolic world models with foundation-model-assisted specification mining, scenario generation, and test synthesis; and (iii) compositional verification and runtime assurance that target formally tractable components and interfaces through modular contracts, equivalence checking, verified synthesis, uncertainty-aware reasoning, and runtime monitoring and enforcement. Symbolic abstractions establish assurance boundaries between stochastic learning and formal reasoning, connecting testing, verification, and runtime evidence across the system lifecycle. Foundation models serve as constrained assistants whose outputs are independently checked wherever correctness can be formally specified. We identify research challenges in specification engineering, abstraction fidelity, evidence composition, and lifecycle certification, and outline a staged agenda toward trustworthy, deployable, and certifiable learning-enabled CPS.

</details>


### [71] [SimTO-Robust: Automated Design of Soft Grippers Robust to Grasp Pose and Object Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39560)
*Kurt Enkera,Joshua Pinskier,Marcus Gallagher,David Howard*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SimTO-Robust，将多物体、多抓取位姿的仿真抓取与接触力聚合引入拓扑优化，生成对几何与位姿误差鲁棒的软体夹爪，并经37个夹爪的物理实验验证其抗扰优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非结构化环境中的机器人夹爪需在感知与定位误差下稳定抓取不同形状尺寸的物体；作者希望以智能机械设计（形态优化）替代复杂控制与传感来实现鲁棒性，但现有计算设计框架未显式针对多物体几何与抓取位姿优化软夹爪形态。

Method: 将仿真驱动拓扑优化（SimTO）推广为SimTO-Robust：在多个物体与多个抓取位姿上模拟抓取，聚合接触力以引导夹爪形态优化；共生成6,646个设计，物理评测其中24个，并对比10个专用SimTO设计、2个刚性基线夹爪及1个用传统拓扑优化得到的通用软夹爪。

Result: 全部37个夹爪在含5个特征丰富物体、6种抓取位姿及递增外部扰动的抓取放置任务中测试；平均而言SimTO-Robust设计完成任务比专用SimTO设计与刚性基线更可靠，最佳SimTO-Robust设计承受的扰动多于其他所有被测夹爪。

Conclusion: 在仿真驱动拓扑优化中纳入多样化抓取条件，是提升真实世界软夹爪鲁棒性的简单有效途径；更广泛地说明鲁棒抓取可通过智能机械设计而非复杂传感与控制实现。

Abstract: Robotic grippers operating in unstructured environments must robustly grasp objects of different shapes and sizes despite perception and positioning errors. Soft grippers that can achieve this robustness through intelligent mechanical design rather than sophisticated control software offer a promising solution. However, no existing computational design framework explicitly optimises soft gripper morphology for robustness across object geometries and grasp poses. We introduce SimTO-Robust, a generalisation of simulation-driven topology optimisation (SimTO) that simulates grasps across multiple objects and poses and aggregates their contact forces to guide gripper morphology optimisation. We generate 6,646 designs and physically evaluate 24 of them alongside 10 specialised SimTO designs, two rigid baseline grippers, and a baseline generalist soft gripper produced using conventional topology optimisation. All 37 grippers are tested in a pick-and-place task involving five feature-rich objects and six grasp poses, with external disturbances of increasing magnitude applied during the task. On average, SimTO-Robust designs complete pick-and-place more reliably than specialised SimTO designs and the rigid baselines. The best SimTO-Robust designs also withstood more of the applied disturbances than any other tested gripper. These results show that incorporating diverse grasp conditions into simulation-driven topology optimisation is a simple and effective approach to improving real-world soft gripper robustness. More broadly, they show that robust robotic grasping can be achieved through intelligent mechanical design rather than sophisticated sensing and control.

</details>


### [72] [Kinematic-Tactile Aligned Dexterous Retargeting with Contact Wrench Space Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39582)
*Xinyuan He,Ziheng Xu,Yueyuan Chen,Yufan Ye,Yuanshuo Tan,Huiming Pan,Jianjun Meng,Shuo Jiang,Peter B. Shull*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一个三阶段分层框架，实现人手到机器人手的运动学-触觉联合重定向，在真实机器人倒水实验中达到96.7%重放成功率并优于代表性基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类演示是学习灵巧操作策略的自然且可扩展数据源，但现有重定向方法主要迁移人体运动学，物理交互背后的触觉信息很少能跨本体保留，限制了接触丰富任务的迁移效果。

Method: 以物体为中心的接触力旋量空间作为人与机器人触觉对齐的物理表示。框架分三步：先对齐手部运动学与接触几何；再由触觉力与接触几何构建人的力旋量支撑剖面；然后优化机器人指尖力目标以覆盖该支撑集，并转换为关节位置修正，得到可直接重放的机器人手轨迹。

Result: 在仿真与真实机器人上针对三种倒水条件进行评估；真实实验中重放成功率达96.7%，力旋量传递保真度高于代表性重定向基线。

Conclusion: 该框架实现了从人类演示到机器人手的运动学与触觉信息的物理落地迁移，为可扩展的接触丰富灵巧操作学习提供了路径。

Abstract: Human demonstrations provide a natural and scalable source of data for learning dexterous manipulation policies. However, existing retargeting methods primarily transfer human kinematics, while the tactile information underlying physical interaction is rarely preserved across embodiments. To address this limitation, we present a hierarchical three-stage framework for kinematic-tactile aligned dexterous retargeting. We use object-centric contact wrench space as a physical representation for tactile alignment between human and robot hands. The framework first aligns hand kinematics and contact geometry, and then constructs a human wrench support profile from tactile forces and contact geometry. Robot fingertip force targets are subsequently optimized to cover the human wrench support and converted into joint position corrections, yielding directly replayable robot hand trajectories. We evaluate the framework in simulation and real robot experiments across three pouring conditions. In the real robot experiments, our method achieves a 96.7\% replay success rate and higher wrench transfer fidelity than representative retargeting baselines. The framework enables physically grounded transfer of kinematic and tactile information from human demonstrations to robot hands, providing a path toward scalable learning of contact-rich dexterous manipulation.

</details>


### [73] [AI for Science in Space: Orbital Laboratories, Planetary Fieldwork, and Embodied Autonomy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39689)
*Zizhe Wang,Jia Deng,Xin Lu,Zehao Jia,Yi Huang,Yao Zhu,Jianing Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文是一篇批判性综述，系统梳理了从"学习太空数据"到"选择并执行太空实验"的AI4S进展，提出以"样本历史—科学决策—物理执行—主张特异性有效性"为核心的框架，主张以有效科学回报、任务覆盖度、干预成本和证据完整性来衡量太空自主科学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI4S正从解释已有数据扩展到设计与执行实验。太空环境（微重力与外星环境）能暴露地面实验无法复现的现象，但受限于样本稀缺、资源受限、干预机会有限，持续研究困难。地面自驱动实验室已提供优化与无人化操作的补充证据，而太空场景中观测、可执行动作与有效证据连续性方面仍存在空白，亟需统一视角评估太空机器人与自主系统的真实价值。

Method: 采用批判性综述与框架构建方法：覆盖生物学、材料、流体与热物理及自适应探索等领域，考察轨道实验室与行星原位科学；区分四类能力——从太空衍生数据中学习、选择科学行动、执行行动、保持实验有效性；借鉴地面自驱动实验室在优化、专用自动化、机器人操作与无人值守支持方面的经验；构建连接样本历史、科学决策、物理执行与主张特异性有效性的框架，并设计一个轨道设计实例、一个行星扩展案例以及匹配的评估方案，以分离感知、决策策略与物理行动的各自贡献。

Result: 综述识别出有界AI系统已展示的科学收益，并定位其在观测、可执行动作与有效证据连续性上的缺口；指出当必要干预超出已验证仪器能力且无法及时获得人力支持时，太空机器人可扩展自主科学的可达范围，但其价值须与专用机构、冗余设计和人机协同运行进行对照论证；所提出的评估方案能够分离感知、决策与执行三类贡献。

Conclusion: 应通过有效科学回报、任务覆盖度、干预成本和完整任务周期内的证据完整性来衡量太空自主性，而非仅以自动化程度论优劣；据此形成的研究议程为太空AI4S的可验证发展提供了评价基准。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence for science (AI4S) is extending from interpretation of existing data to the design and execution of experiments. Space offers distinctive scientific opportunities while restricting physical support. Microgravity and extraterrestrial environments expose phenomena that terrestrial experiments cannot fully reproduce; finite samples, constrained resources, and limited opportunities for intervention complicate sustained investigation. This critical review examines orbital laboratories and planetary in situ science across biology, materials, fluid and thermal physics, and adaptive exploration. We distinguish learning from space-derived data, selecting scientific actions, executing them, and preserving experimental validity. Terrestrial self-driving laboratories provide complementary evidence on optimization, dedicated automation, robotic manipulation, and the support needed for unattended operation. The synthesis connects scientific gains already demonstrated by bounded AI systems to gaps in observation, executable actions, and continuity of valid evidence. Space robots can expand the attainable scope of autonomous science when necessary interventions exceed validated instrument capabilities and timely human support is unavailable. Their value must be established against dedicated mechanisms, redundancy, and human-supported operation. We develop a framework connecting sample history, scientific decisions, physical execution, and claim-specific validity. A worked orbital design, a planetary extension, and matched evaluation schemes separate the contributions of sensing, decision policies, and physical actions. The resulting agenda measures autonomy through valid scientific return, task coverage, intervention costs, and evidence integrity across complete campaigns.

</details>


### [74] [AFast Robot Localization Method Based on Monte Carlo Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39708)
*He Zhaoqing,Hang Li,Xian Pu,Li Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于蒙特卡洛采样的在线粒子滤波定位方法，仅用车身前后左右四个测距传感器、无需航向信息，实现迷宫环境中移动机器人的较准确定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实受限环境下机器人往往无法依赖外部定位系统，因此需要仅依靠自身传感器的自主定位方案。

Method: 采用蒙特卡洛采样的在线粒子滤波方法，应用于迷宫环境中的移动机器人定位；系统只需要安装在车辆前、后、左、右四个方向的测距传感器，不需要任何航向信息。

Result: 仿真实验表明，即使在局部区域高度相似的迷宫中，车辆仍能实现相对准确的定位。

Conclusion: 该方法为复杂环境下的机器人定位提供了一种快速有效的途径。

Abstract: Localization is an essential capability of intelligent robots. However, in real-world constrained environments, robots often cannot rely on external localization systems. Therefore, this paper proposes an online particle-filter localization method based on Monte Carlo sampling. The method is applied to the localization of a mobile robot in a maze environment and requires only four range sensors mounted on the front, rear, left, and right sides of the vehicle, without the need for any heading information. Simulation experiments demonstrate that even in a maze where local regions are highly similar, the vehicle can still achieve relatively accurate localization, thereby providing a fast and effective approach for robot localization in complex environments.

</details>


### [75] [Teaching a Tree to Recover: Online Distillation of an LLM Supervisor with Counterfactual Replay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39775)
*Kristian Ceder,Kilian Freitag,Knut Åkesson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出将大语言模型（LLM）监管理事在线蒸馏为决策树，用反事实回放为恢复动作打标签，使机器人在仿真中遇到MPC冻结时能正确恢复或求助，且测试时零LLM查询。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全局规划器加局部MPC的机器人在非凸避障与有限时域下容易卡死，需要监督决策（重规划/等待/求助）；而每次都查询LLM速度慢、成本高，手写规则又难以覆盖长尾情形。

Method: 把LLM监管理事在线蒸馏为决策树：每个训练回合后用反事实回放将恢复动作标注为"必要/不必要/有害"，据此淘汰无用规则；新情形仍由LLM处理，经验证的恢复再转化为新规则。

Result: 在20个MPC单独会冻结的未见布局上，决策树在所有随机种子下至少正确处理18个（到达目标或在无可行路径时求助），性能与始终查询LLM的监管和专家规则相当，但测试时无需任何LLM调用。

Conclusion: 在线蒸馏是一种有前景的方案：决策树覆盖熟悉情形，LLM仅用于新情形，并可充当监督器的"设计者"而非仅运行时监督者；但其局限在于仅在仿真中验证、样本效率与长尾覆盖仍需进一步检验。

Abstract: Autonomous mobile robots commonly pair a global planner with a local model predictive controller (MPC), but nonconvex obstacle avoidance and a limited horizon can make the robot freeze in complex layouts. Deciding whether to replan, wait, or call for help is a supervisory decision. Recent work queries a large language model (LLM) supervisor on every anomaly, which is slow and costly. We instead distill the LLM online into a decision tree. After each training episode, counterfactual replay labels recoveries as needed, unnecessary, or harmful. These labels retire unhelpful rules. In simulation, on 20 unseen layouts where the MPC alone freezes, the tree handles at least 18 correctly across all seeds, either by reaching the goal or by calling for help when no feasible path exists. The tree matches both an always-query LLM supervisor and expert-designed rules, while issuing no LLM queries at test time. Handwritten rules cannot anticipate the long tail of situations a physical robot will encounter. Online distillation is a promising remedy: the tree handles familiar cases, and the LLM is consulted only for new ones, whose validated recoveries become new rules. Language models can thus also serve as designers of supervisors rather than only as runtime supervisors.

</details>


### [76] [Data-Driven or Model-Constrained? Comparing Airflow Estimation Methods under Sparse Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39789)
*Dominik Aigner,Thomas Wiedemann,Javier Monroy,Achim J. Lilienthal*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 系统比较四类气流场估计方法（GPR、图GMRF、两种基于Navier-Stokes的方法），在三种二维稳态仿真场景和不同采样密度下量化评估稀疏传感下的估计精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 气流驱动的平流是气体与气溶胶扩散的主要机制，准确估计气流场对通风监测、气源定位和污染物扩散分析至关重要。但实际中难以进行高分辨率空间测量，须依靠统计或物理先验补偿稀疏数据。现有方法在引入流体约束与环境几何方式上各异，却缺乏系统性比较，导致物理先验在稀疏传感下的潜在收益未被充分认识。

Method: 构建三类仿真二维稳态场景，在多种采样密度下定量评测四种估计器：高斯过程回归（GPR）、基于图的GMRF，以及两种基于Navier-Stokes方程的方法（其中一种为本文提出的强形式残差最小化变体）。

Result: 低采样密度下物理先验收益最大，可有效补偿缺失的空间信息；随传感器覆盖度提高，数据驱动的GPR竞争力逐渐增强；但在所有评估场景中，基于模型（物理约束）的估计器仍保持精度优势。

Conclusion: 物理先验在稀疏传感的气流估计中价值显著，尤其适用于测量受限场景；基于物理模型的估计器在精度上普遍优于纯数据驱动方法，为方法选择提供实证依据。

Abstract: Airflow-driven advection is the primary mechanism for gas and aerosol dispersion in real-world scenarios, making accurate airflow estimation essential for ventilation monitoring, gas source localization, and contaminant spread analysis. Since obtaining airflow maps via high-resolution spatial sensing is usually infeasible in practice, flow field estimation methods must cope with sparse measurements by introducing statistical or physical priors to compensate for limited data. Existing approaches mainly differ in how they incorporate fluid-flow constraints and environmental geometry. However, due to the lack of systematic comparisons across this spectrum of methods, the potential benefits of physics-based priors for airflow estimation under sparse sensing remain underexplored. To address this question, we quantitatively evaluate four airflow estimators across three simulated 2D scenarios under steady-state conditions and varying sampling densities. The methods range from Gaussian Process Regression and graph-based Gaussian Markov Random Fields to two Navier-Stokes-based approaches, including a novel strong-form residual minimization variant. Our results show that physical priors provide the greatest benefit at low sampling densities by compensating for missing spatial information. Although data-driven Gaussian Process Regression becomes increasingly competitive as sensor coverage increases, model-based estimators retain an accuracy advantage across the evaluated scenarios.

</details>


### [77] [Surrogate Uncertainty for Black-box Visual Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39798)
*Hanbo Zhang,Tianrun Hu,Cedar Site Bai,Anxing Xiao,David Hsu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种外部替代贝叶斯模型，将黑盒VLM的预测视为观测，在留出校准集的局部邻域上逐查询估计不确定度（分解为偶然与认知两部分），并结合保形预测或主动视觉grounding规划，提升校准与任务表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人需依据视觉观测理解语言指令以定位目标物体，而现有方法直接从黑盒VLM自身输出推导不确定度；但产生预测的同一套参数和结构会带来同样的偏差，导致不确定度不可靠。

Method: 给出形式化的贝叶斯问题定义，把VLM预测当作观测；引入外部替代贝叶斯模型，在留出校准集的局部邻域内量化逐查询不确定度并分解为aleatoric与epistemic；通过跨输入模态的层次化检索构建紧凑的联合空间邻域以缓解维数灾难；该估计器可与保形预测或基于模型的规划结合，用于机器人采取语言或物理动作的主动视觉grounding。

Result: 在多个grounding基准和先进VLM上，该方法一致地改善了校准质量与任务性能；在真实Fetch机器人上执行开放世界指令时，能够在自信动作与信息收集之间实现有原则的权衡，显著提升鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 不确定度应来自独立于VLM的外部校准机制而非模型自述；所提局部邻域贝叶斯估计框架可有效支撑可靠的主动视觉grounding。

Abstract: Visual grounding is crucial for robots to understand language instructions with visual observations. This paper addresses the problem of visual grounding uncertainties, where a robot uses a black-box vision-language model (VLM) to ground free-form natural-language instructions to target objects. Prevailing methods derive uncertainty from the VLM itself. Yet, outputs of the VLM, e.g., generated uncertainties, are from the same parameters and structures producing the predictions, and inherit the bias. We give a formal Bayesian definition of the problem that treats the VLM prediction as an observation. An external surrogate Bayesian model then quantifies per-query uncertainty over a local neighborhood of a held-out calibration set, decomposed into aleatoric and epistemic components. A hierarchical retrieval across input modalities realizes a compact joint-space neighborhood, mitigating the curse of dimensionality. The estimator further integrates with conformal prediction or model-based planning for active visual grounding, where robots are allowed to take verbal or physical actions to ground targets. Across grounding benchmarks and state-of-the-art VLMs, it consistently improves both calibration and task performance. On a real Fetch robot under open-world instructions, it enables principled trade-offs between confident action and information gathering, substantially improving robustness.

</details>


### [78] [PASA-KV: Attacking Action-Facing Visual Routes in World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39816)
*Zhongbo Wu,Jiakai Huang,Zheng Zhang,Siyu Xu,Kaixiang Chen,Xiaohong Liu,Shan You,Chang Xu,Tao Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究"世界动作模型"(WAMs)中内部表征扰动何时会真正导致行为失效，发现表征可扰动性与闭环脆弱性存在明显错配，并提出以"面向动作的读取(action-facing readout)"视角来选择脆弱K/V通路进行攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WAMs 越来越多地将内部视觉/世界表征与动作生成耦合，其"世界→动作"通路是鲁棒性的关键来源，但人们对其理解不足。直觉上认为表征位移越大、控制失效越严重，而作者希望检验这一假设是否成立，并解释扰动如何真正传导到动作层面。

Method: 先对内部表征施加扰动，比较不同视觉K/V通路的攻击效果与表征位移幅度；将错配归因于动作条件读取机制（key决定动作查询的读取位置，value决定读取内容）。据此提出 Tail-AV（短探测信号，用于识别脆弱通路）和 PASA-KV（先按面向动作的标准选择K/V目标，再训练新的任务共享攻击）。

Result: 攻击有效性在不同视觉K/V通路上差异显著，但更大的表征位移并不必然带来更强的控制失效；读针指导的目标选择在多类WAM系统以及一台实体机器人上得到验证，实现了较强的闭环攻击，并揭示出不同系统在抵抗力上存在显著差异。

Conclusion: 脆弱性应由扰动向动作生成的"路由方式"而非表征空间中的幅度来刻画；面向动作的读取视角能有效指导K/V目标选择，提升闭环攻击效果，并可用于比较不同WAM系统的鲁棒性差异。

Abstract: World action models (WAMs) increasingly couple internal visual or world representations with action generation, making their world-to-action pathways a critical yet poorly understood source of robustness. We investigate when perturbations to these internal representations actually become behaviorally consequential, and uncover a pronounced mismatch between representation perturbability and closed-loop vulnerability: attack effectiveness varies sharply across visual K/V routes, while larger representation displacement does not reliably imply stronger control failure. Our analysis attributes this mismatch to the action-conditioned readout, where keys determine where action queries read and values determine what content they receive. This motivates an action-facing view of vulnerability, in which the effect of a perturbation depends on how it is routed into action generation rather than on representation-space magnitude alone. We instantiate this view with Tail-AV, a short-probe signal for identifying vulnerable routes, and operationalize it in PASA-KV, which selects action-facing K/V targets before training a fresh task-shared attack. Experiments across multiple WAM systems and a physical robot validate readout-informed target selection, demonstrate strong closed-loop attacks, and reveal substantial system-level differences in resistance.

</details>


### [79] [Compressed Orientation Reachability Maps for Sub-Second Robotic Needle Entry Point Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39879)
*Prabhu Nithin Gollapudi,Andreas Maier,Franziska Mathis-Ullrich,Water Zhao,Jia Wang,Siming Bayer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于方向的可达性地图（ORM），预计算6自由度机械臂在188,873个工作空间位置的关节可行性并压缩为83 MB索引地图，配合JIT编译运行时查找，将经皮肝介入入口点规划延迟从13.5–26.9 s降至0.289–0.349 s，几何保真度保持高位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人经皮肝介入的多阶段轨迹规划中，确定运动学可行的穿刺入口点是关键计算瓶颈。现有方法依赖穷举逆运动学或随机搜索，每个目标耗时13–27秒，难以满足术中实时调整需求。

Method: 构建方向可达性地图（ORM）：离线预计算6自由度串联机械臂在188,873个空间位置的运动学可行性，将姿态可行性编码为紧凑的带索引地图（83 MB）；运行时以JIT编译流水线用数组查表替代在线优化求解。

Result: 每目标规划延迟由13.5–26.9 s降至0.289–0.349 s；相对基线可达点云，凸包体积重叠比均值0.933，alpha-shape体积比均值0.976。

Conclusion: ORM框架实现亚秒级入口点规划，为经皮肝消融中的机器人针刺提供高效计算框架。

Abstract: In the context of multi-stage trajectory planning for robotic percutaneous liver interventions, identifying a kinematically feasible entry point remains a critical computational bottleneck. Existing methods rely on exhaustive inverse kinematics or randomized searches, incurring delays of 13 to 27 seconds per target and limiting real-time intraoperative adaptation. This paper introduces an Orientation-Based Reachability Map (ORM) that precomputes the kinematic feasibility of a 6 Degrees of Freedom (DoF) serial manipulator across 188,873 workspace positions and encodes orientation feasibility into a compact, indexed 83 MB map. A Just-In-Time (JIT) compiled runtime pipeline replaces live optimization with near-instantaneous array lookups. Experimental evaluation demonstrates a reduction in planning latency from 13.5-26.9 s to 0.289-0.349 s per target while maintaining high geometric fidelity, achieving a mean convex hull volume overlap ratio of 0.933 and a mean alpha-shape volume ratio of 0.976 relative to the baseline reachable point cloud. The proposed ORM framework enables sub-second entry point planning and provides an efficient computational framework for robotic needle insertion in percutaneous liver ablation.

</details>


### [80] [Gaze-Assisted Supervisory Control for Lunar Robotic Arm Teleoperation Under Concurrent Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39998)
*Jiayu He,Yifan Li,Oliver R. Runswick,Ernest N. Kamavuako,Yunjie Jia,Peter D. Hodkinson,Yang Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种"视线辅助监督式控制"框架：操作者用视线指定岩石或放置目标，显式确认后由机器人自主完成有界的抓取—放置动作；在16人VR被试内实验中与手柄遥操作对比，任务时间由47.65s降至18.43s、脑力负荷显著下降，但精密放置精度仍以手动更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 月面样品处理中，车载机械臂可扩展人的作业范围，但连续手柄遥操作需要操作者反复进行视觉—运动对齐，且同时还需监控系统状态，认知负荷高、效率低。作者希望探索一种降低对齐负担、把任务级意图指定交给视线、把执行交给自主控制的混合主动式方案。

Method: 提出视线辅助监督式控制框架：视线提名目标（岩石/放置点）→显式确认授权→机器人在受限范围内自主执行抓取与放置。在任务模拟VR环境中开展2×2×3被试内设计实验（控制模式×放置难度×并行监控负荷），16名被试参与，与手动手柄遥操作对照，测量任务完成时间及各阶段占比、监控任务的响应准确率与速度、核心兴趣区之间的视线转移频率、NASA-TLX负荷，以及放置误差与放置成功率。

Result: 单块岩石平均任务完成时间由47.65s降至18.43s，其中72.5%的时间差来自抓取阶段；在所测试的听觉监控条件下，视线辅助监督式控制的平均比例性变慢更小，监控响应的准确率与速度未检出控制模式差异；核心兴趣区之间视线转移频率由49.98次/分降至27.73次/分，NASA-TLX原始分数由32.54降至18.26。但收益具有任务依赖性：在精密放置任务中，手动控制的平均放置误差更小、放置成功率更高。

Conclusion: 结果支持一种混合主动式权限分配：视线用于快速的任务级意图指定并触发自主执行，而在精度关键阶段保留手动微调。该框架适合作为月面样品处理中人机协作的设计原则，而非完全替代遥操作。

Abstract: Rover-mounted robotic arms can extend human reach during lunar sample handling, but continuous joystick teleoperation requires repeated visual-motor alignment while operators may also need to monitor system states. This paper presents a gaze-assisted supervisory control framework in which gaze nominates a rock or placement target, explicit confirmation authorizes the action, and the robot performs bounded autonomous grasp-and-place execution. The framework was evaluated against manual joystick teleoperation in a mission-analog virtual reality study with 16 participants. The study used a 2 x 2 x 3 within-subject design varying control mode, placement difficulty, and concurrent monitoring load. Mean recorded task completion time decreased from 47.65 to 18.43 s per rock, with 72.5% of this difference arising during grasping. Under the tested auditory monitoring conditions, gaze-assisted supervisory control showed less average proportional slowing, with no detected control-mode difference in monitoring-response accuracy or speed. The rate of gaze shifts between core areas of interest decreased from 49.98 to 27.73 shifts/min, while the raw NASA Task Load Index score decreased from 32.54 to 18.26. However, the benefit was task dependent. In precision placement, manual control yielded lower mean placement error and a higher placement success rate. These findings support a mixed-initiative allocation in which gaze enables rapid task-level intention specification and autonomous execution, while precision-critical stages retain manual refinement.

</details>


### [81] [How Much Should a VLA Trust Its Reasoning? Step-Level Guidance as a Measured Action-Value Function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40002)
*Dabin Seo,Eunhyek Joa*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 CoC-CFG 与 Q-adaptive guidance：将推理轨迹的引导强度作为可测量的 MDP 动作，用小型 critic 拟合动作价值来自适应调节每步引导权重，显著提升 VLA 驾驶策略性能并刷新公开 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理型 VLA 策略的补救方法只关注"推理是否正确"，而未回答"推理应以多大强度影响动作"。错误的推理迹会产生错误动作，甚至看似正确的推理也可能误导规划，因此需要一种可控、可调的解释性引导机制。

Method: CoC-CFG 在策略的完整上下文与去掉推理片段的上下文之间做无分类器引导，每个 Euler 步使用一个权重 w 来衰减或放大推理影响（w=1 还原原策略）。作者将该权重的选择建模为马尔可夫决策过程，其动作价值通过测量而非自举得到：在冻结专家、固定噪声下，应用一步权重后由当前控制器完成规划即可精确返回该权重的价值。Q-adaptive guidance 用小型 critic 拟合这些价值，并保守地改进控制器。

Result: 在 Alpasim 开环中优于所有固定权重及在线 RL（SAC、GRPO），闭环通过 300 场景中的 262 个（原策略 252）。在 Waymo 端到端驾驶基准的留出帧上，前三阶段优于所有固定权重、四种自适应引导方法、SAC 与 GRPO。隐藏测试集上，冻结的 Alpamayo 1.5 配合规则化相机与车辆转换及 Q-adaptive guidance 取得 8.135159 的 rater 反馈分数，超过最佳已发表方法的 8.079。

Conclusion: 引导强度本身是一个值得优化的问题；将其视为可测量价值函数的决策过程，可在不训练策略的前提下稳健提升推理型 VLA 的驾驶表现，并达到新的公开 SOTA。

Abstract: Reasoning vision-language-action (VLA) policies write a reasoning trace and then decode the action from it. A wrong trace yields a wrong action, and even a correct-looking trace can mislead the plan. Existing remedies ask whether the reasoning is right. We ask how strongly it should steer. CoC-CFG applies classifier-free guidance between the policy's context and the same context without the reasoning span. One weight per Euler step attenuates or amplifies the reasoning, and w=1 recovers the original policy. We treat the choice of this weight as a Markov decision process whose action values are measured, not bootstrapped. With the expert frozen and the noise fixed, applying a weight for one step and finishing the plan under the current controller returns that weight's value exactly. Q-adaptive guidance fits these values with small critics and improves the controller conservatively. In the Alpasim driving simulator it outperforms every fixed weight and online RL (SAC, GRPO) in open loop, and in closed loop it passes 262 of 300 scenes, against 252 for the original policy. On held-out frames of the Waymo end-to-end driving benchmark, its first three stages outperform every fixed weight, four adaptive-guidance methods, SAC and GRPO. On the hidden test set, a frozen Alpamayo 1.5 with rule-based camera and vehicle conversions and Q-adaptive guidance sets a new state of the art among published methods: 8.135159 rater feedback score, against 8.079 for the best published method.

</details>


### [82] [Humanoid Dodgeball: Learning Fast Dynamic Obstacle Avoidance Motions for Humanoids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40003)
*Aoqian Zhang,Chunzheng Wang,Haoran Chen,Annan Tang,Cheng Xiang,Fan Shi,Shuzhi Sam Ge*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Humanoid Dodgeball强化学习框架，使人形机器人能对高速来袭抛射物做出敏捷的全身闪避动作，同时保持稳定性与类人自然性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人要在动态环境中安全工作，必须能快速躲避快速移动的障碍物，同时保持稳定与物理可行的全身运动。但由于人形系统动力学维数高，且需同时兼顾反应式避障、动作自然性与控制鲁棒性，学习此类行为仍具挑战。

Method: 构建RL框架：采用威胁感知的奖励塑形（threat-aware reward shaping）驱动高动态全身规避动作；并引入多个方向特定的对抗式运动先验（adversarial motion priors），将学习行为正则化到稳定、多样且类人的运动风格上。

Result: 大量仿真与真机实验表明，该框架在不同障碍物速度与发射距离下显著提升动态避障性能，同时保持鲁棒且物理合理的人形运动。

Conclusion: 威胁感知奖励塑形与方向特定对抗运动先验的结合，可有效实现人形机器人敏捷、稳定且自然的动态闪避，并具备从仿真到硬件的可迁移性。

Abstract: Humanoids have become increasingly agile and capable in real-world environments. To operate safely in dynamic scenarios, they must rapidly evade fast-moving obstacles while maintaining stability and physically feasible whole-body motion. Learning such behaviors remains challenging due to the high-dimensional dynamics of humanoid systems and the need to jointly balance reactive obstacle avoidance, motion naturalness, and control robustness. In this work, we present Humanoid Dodgeball, a reinforcement learning (RL) framework for agile dynamic obstacle avoidance in humanoids. Our method enables highly dynamic whole-body evasive maneuvers against incoming projectiles through threat-aware reward shaping, while multiple direction-specific adversarial motion priors regularize the learned behaviors toward stable and diverse human-like motions. Extensive simulation and hardware experiments demonstrate that the proposed framework substantially improves dynamic obstacle avoidance performance across diverse obstacle speeds and launch distances, while preserving robust and physically plausible humanoid motions.

</details>


### [83] [LOGIV: Graph-Verified Execution with Causal Local Repair for Long-Horizon Robot Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40016)
*Enqiao Lu,Yuxiang Xie,Xingrui Yu,Yuyang Wang,Yipeng Gu,Zhenglin Wan,Pengfei Zhou,Wangbo Zhao,Yang You,Yuxiang Gao,Ivor Tsang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LOGIV是一个基于显式任务图的智能体机器人执行框架，将符号规划、基于观测的验证与具身动作连接起来，通过条件支撑边和资源顺序边在执行中持续校验物理有效性，并在失败后用依赖追溯确定修复内容，无需训练新组件即可提升任务成功率与条件恢复能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体机器人需将高层目标转为动作，同时维持后续步骤所需的物理条件。但"动作已完成"不等于"其效果会持续存在"：后续扰动可能破坏剩余计划仍假设成立的支撑关系。因此需要一种机制在执行过程中持续检查并修复这些物理依赖。

Method: 提出LOGIV图工程框架：1）将经过验证的计划编译成条件-支撑边与资源顺序边；2）执行期间检查这些边的物理有效性；3）失败后通过依赖追溯确定需恢复的内容；4）修复受保留的未完成动作约束，组合后的续接方案在执行恢复前先做符号验证；5）框架运行在现有VLA与WAM策略之上，不增加可训练组件。

Result: 在RoboTwin V2与BiCoord共20个双臂任务、三种执行骨干上，无注入扰动时平均任务成功率分别提升10.67和15.33个百分点；在预定义扰动下，两个基准上对每种骨干的任务完成率与条件恢复率均优于三个适配的恢复基线；三个真实机器人任务中成功试验由12/30提升至17/30。

Conclusion: 结果支持将显式、持续检查的任务依赖作为可靠智能体机器人执行的实用接口。该框架可与现有VLA/WAM策略即插即用，在仿真与真实场景中均能提升任务成功率和失败恢复能力。

Abstract: Agentic robots must turn a high-level goal into actions while keeping the physical conditions needed for future steps valid. A completed action does not ensure that its effects will persist: a later disturbance can invalidate support that the remaining plan still assumes. We present LOGIV, a graph engineering framework for agentic robot execution that uses an explicit task graph to connect symbolic planning, observation-based verification, and embodied action. LOGIV compiles a validated plan into condition-support and resource-ordering edges, checks their physical validity during execution, and uses dependency tracing to determine what must be restored after a failure. Repair is constrained by retained unfinished actions, and the combined continuation is symbolically validated before execution resumes. The framework operates above existing Vision-Language-Action (VLA) and World Action Model (WAM) policies without adding trainable components. Across 20 dual-arm tasks from RoboTwin~V2 and BiCoord with three execution backbones, LOGIV improves mean task success without injected disturbances by 10.67 and 15.33 percentage points, respectively. Under predefined perturbations, it achieves higher task completion and condition restoration than three adapted recovery baselines for every backbone on both benchmarks. On three real-robot tasks, successful trials increase from 12/30 to 17/30. These results support explicit, continuously checked task dependencies as a practical interface for reliable agentic robot execution.

</details>


### [84] [RoboSeed: Multidimensional Evaluation of Vision-Language-Action and World-Action Models: Capability, Resource Affordability, and Research Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40026)
*Xin Ma,Furong Chen,Xinlong Man,Bo Zhang,Yalong Guo,Haolan Lei,Chaochen Cui,Hongyuan Zhang,Mulin Chen,Yi Wang,Lap-pui Chau,Chuang Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 RoboSeed 多维评估框架，用能力、下游训练成本、部署成本、本地可验证研究价值、复现证据五个维度刻画 VLA/WAM 模型，并显式标注缺失证据与覆盖率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅凭成功率不足以指导不同算力预算与目标的研究者选择机器人动作模型，需要关于任务能力、适配成本、部署需求和结论可靠性的多维证据。

Method: 定义五个评估维度，将经验测量与审计标准通过固定归一化锚点映射为 0–100 画像，提供均衡、训练受限、部署受限三种偏好设置；用接口与协议检查判定比较是否成立；缺失测量显式保留，不完整的聚合分数不予给出，并用加权输入覆盖率标识证据缺口。框架应用于代表性 VLA 与 WAM 模型，依据本地训练、已发布权重评估、任务级审计和评估系统研究。

Result: 结果显示：骨干网络选择依赖预算；模型对适配配方敏感；语言接口与视觉任务接口表现存在差异。开源可复现评分代码与机器可读证据支持扩展至更多模型。

Conclusion: 研究给出维度级评估结论与后续测量优先级；由于服务轨迹不完整且协议异构，暂不提供总体数值排名。

Abstract: Choosing a robot action model requires evidence about task capability, adaptation cost, deployment requirements, and the reliability of reported conclusions. Success rates alone offer limited guidance to researchers with different computing budgets and goals. We propose RoboSeed, a multidimensional evaluation framework for vision-language-action (VLA) and world-action models (WAM), within a continuing program to train, evaluate, and openly release robot models with verifiable evidence for model selection.
  The framework defines five dimensions: capability, downstream training affordability, deployment affordability, locally verified research value, and reproduction evidence. It maps empirical measurements and audit criteria to 0-100 profiles using fixed normalization anchors, with balanced, training-constrained, and deployment-constrained preference settings. Interface and protocol checks establish when comparisons are appropriate. Missing measurements remain explicit, incomplete aggregate scores are withheld, and weighted input coverage identifies evidence gaps.
  We apply the framework to representative VLA and WAM models, drawing on local training, released-weight evaluation, task-level audits, and an evaluation-system study. The results reveal budget-dependent backbone choices, sensitivity to adaptation recipes, and differences between language and visual task interfaces. Reproducible scoring code and machine-readable evidence support extension to additional models. The study provides dimension-level assessments and priorities for further measurement, while incomplete serving traces and heterogeneous protocols preclude overall numerical rankings.

</details>


### [85] [Don't Risk It!: Trajectory-Conditioned Distributional Risk for LiDAR-Only Crowd Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40050)
*Mathai Mathew Pulicken,Laksh Nanwani,Basant Sharma,Phani Teja Singamaneni,Arun Kumar Singh,K. Madhava Krishna*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出 DRIP，一种仅依赖 LiDAR 的端到端人群导航规划框架，将占用栅格预测与"轨迹条件化的净空及不确定性估计"耦合，学习候选控制序列下最小机器人-障碍物净空的分布以评估碰撞风险，并在45人闭环场景中取得更高成功率、更少碰撞和更少冻结行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高效的人群导航需要提前预判动态的无碰撞空间，而现有方法往往依赖脆弱的目标跟踪（object tracking），在拥挤、遮挡频繁的场景中易失效。论文希望摆脱对跟踪的依赖，直接从 LiDAR 观测中获取可靠的风险判断。

Method: 提出 Don't Risk It Planner（DRIP）：以 LiDAR 生成的占用栅格历史为直接输入，先做占用预测（occupancy forecasting），再对每个候选控制序列预测"最小机器人-障碍物净空"的概率分布，从而同时输出净空估计与不确定性；训练时使用风险代价（risk cost），将预测不确定性直接锚定在真实碰撞结果上，而非仅做几何回归。

Result: 在与多达45名行人的闭环实验中，DRIP 相比当前最优基线取得更高导航成功率、更少碰撞次数，并显著减少机器人冻结（freezing）现象。

Conclusion: 结果表明，仅用 LiDAR、以碰撞结果为监督来学习净空分布与不确定性，可在不依赖目标跟踪的前提下实现更安全、更果断的拥挤人群导航，验证了"预测不确定性应由经验碰撞风险校准"这一思路的有效性。

Abstract: Efficient crowd navigation requires anticipating dynamic collision-free space without relying on fragile object tracking. We propose the Don't Risk It Planner (DRIP), an end-to-end LiDAR-only planning framework that couples occupancy forecasting with trajectory-conditioned clearance and uncertainty estimation. Operating directly on LiDAR-derived occupancy grid histories, DRIP predicts a distribution over minimum robot-obstacle clearance for candidate control sequences to evaluate collision risk. The model is trained using a risk cost that grounds predictive uncertainty directly in empirical collision outcomes rather than geometric regression alone. In closed-loop experiments with up to 45 pedestrians, DRIP achieves higher success rates with fewer collisions and reduced freezing behavior compared to state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [86] [Learning Safe Flow Matching Robot Policies from Binary Feasibility Oracles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40075)
*Jianming Ma,Rongjun Jin,Yizhi Chen,Yue Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对流匹配机器人策略生成的动作用户无法保证满足关节限位、避障等物理约束，且可行集只能通过黑盒可行性预言机访问的问题，提出一种预言机引导的可微安全防护机制，在前向传播中修正不安全更新、在反向传播中提取局部边界信息，实现训练与推理一致的安全策略学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人基础模型越来越多地采用基于流匹配的动作生成来实现通用操作，但生成的动作不保证满足物理约束（如关节限位、碰撞避免）。当可行集无法解析描述、只能通过黑盒可行性预言机获取时，该问题尤为困难，现有方法缺乏在该"仅预言机"设定下的安全保障手段。

Method: 在仅有二值可行性查询的设定下，提出预言机引导的可微安全防护（safeguard）：前向传播中利用二值可行性查询修正不安全更新；反向传播中从预言机提取局部边界信息，构造约束感知的学习信号；并与离散时间流匹配结合，使同一防护机制在训练和推理阶段共用，从而在无显式或可微约束模型的情况下实现前向—反向对齐的策略学习。

Result: 在障碍物感知的机器人操作实验中，方法实现100%的约束满足率，同时保持任务性能。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，仅依赖黑盒可行性预言机即可为流匹配机器人策略提供训练与推理一致的安全保障，在不牺牲任务表现的前提下达到完全约束满足，为一般操作场景下的安全动作生成提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Robot foundation models increasingly rely on flow-based action generation for general-purpose manipulation, yet the generated actions are not guaranteed to satisfy physical constraints such as joint limits and collision avoidance. This problem is especially challenging when the feasible set cannot be described analytically and is accessible only through black-box feasibility oracles. We study this practical oracle-only setting and propose an oracle-guided differentiable safeguard for safe flow-matching robot policies. The safeguard uses binary feasibility queries to correct unsafe updates in the forward pass, while extracting local boundary information to provide constraint-aware learning signals in the backward pass. Combined with discrete-time flow matching, the same safeguard is used during training and inference, enabling forward--backward aligned policy learning without explicit or differentiable constraint models. Experiments on obstacle-aware robotic manipulation demonstrate 100% constraint satisfaction while preserving task performance.

</details>


### [87] [Decision-Level Error Attribution and Depth-Aware Safety Evaluation of Monocular RGB Human Tracking for Autonomous Robot Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40199)
*Abdulahi Taiwo,Chijioke Cyriacus Ekechi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出用决策级指标（漏判/误停）评估单目RGB行人跟踪，并用两因子Shapley分解区分“安全规则”与“感知误差”的贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人近人作业依赖单目RGB跟踪，但几何精度指标无法反映其对停止/前进二值决策的影响，需量化决策错误究竟源于安全规则本身还是感知误差。

Method: 证明仅用横向距离是平面距离的下界（不漏危险但会产生多余停止）；提出两因子Shapley归因分离规则与感知贡献，并进一步区分漏检测与定位误差；在ETH/UCY行人视频上以模拟静止机器人、互斥的标定与评估行人、时间块bootstrap区间进行实验。

Result: 898名行人、三个场景中，横向规则在标注位置上无漏判但停止精度仅5.6–31.9%；两个带RGB感知场景中，标定地平面定位平面RMSE为0.59 m与0.51 m，优于几何基线（1.72/1.39 m）和标定Depth Anything V2（0.81/0.68 m）；检测召回0.43与0.26，漏检测是漏判危险的首要来源；2倍标准差安全裕度配合地平面定位在全部8个场景-阈值设置中漏判率不劣于横向规则，并在7个设置中减少多余停止。

Conclusion: 决策错误需按规则与感知两因子归因，感知瓶颈主要在检测召回而非定位；加安全裕度的地平面定位可同时控制漏判与误停。所有交互为模拟，结果为离线决策分类而非避碰演示。

Abstract: Monocular RGB tracking is useful for robots operating near people, but perception errors affect binary stop/proceed decisions in ways that geometric accuracy metrics do not capture. We examine how much decision error arises from the safety rule versus perception. We prove that lateral-only distance is a lower bound on planar distance: with accurate positions, it cannot miss dangers identified by the planar rule, but it produces unnecessary stops. We introduce a two-factor Shapley attribution separating rule and perception contributions, and further distinguish missed detections from localization errors. Experiments use ETH and UCY pedestrian videos with simulated stationary robots, disjoint calibration and evaluation pedestrians, and time-block bootstrap intervals. Across 898 pedestrians in three scenes, the lateral rule missed no dangers on annotated positions but achieved only 5.6-31.9% stop precision. In two scenes with RGB perception, calibrated ground-plane localization achieved 0.59 m and 0.51 m planar RMSE, compared with 1.72 m and 1.39 m for a geometric baseline and 0.81 m and 0.68 m for calibrated Depth Anything V2. Detection recall of 0.43 and 0.26 made missed detections the leading source of missed danger. A two-standard-deviation safety margin with ground-plane localization matched or improved on the lateral rule's missed-danger rate in all eight scene-threshold settings and reduced unnecessary stops in seven. All robot interactions are simulated, and outcomes are offline decision classifications, not demonstrations of collision avoidance.

</details>


### [88] [RoboAtom: Large-scale Atomic-Action Supervision for Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40207)
*Xueyuan Wei,Xuanxu Wang,Xudong Li,Yuping He,Wanshui Gan,Jiaqi Peng,Wenqi Guo,Jianjun Zhou,Junting Dong,Chunhua Shen,Haoyu Guo,Mulin Yu,Tao Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 RoboAtom-Data，为多来源机器人数据提供统一、时间对齐的原子动作标注，覆盖 145 万条轨迹、688 万条技能标注片段、16882.1 小时数据，并构建 RoboAtom-Bench 评测基准与 RoboAtom-Anno 标注模型，验证了原子动作监督对机器人控制的收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 细粒度动作标注可支持技能复用与组合泛化，但机器人数据集通常只给任务级指令；即使部分数据含子任务标签，格式与规范不一致也阻碍跨源联合训练。因此需要一个统一化、时间对齐的原子动作标注体系。

Method: 构建 RoboAtom-Data：采用统一标注协议、大规模人工标注、利用相同有序技能序列的重复执行进行标签传播，并配合多阶段质量控制。同时构建 RoboAtom-Bench 以标准化技能识别与时间边界定位评测，并训练自动标注模型 RoboAtom-Anno。

Result: RoboAtom-Anno 在 RoboAtom-Bench 与外部 WGO-Bench 上均优于所有被测开源与闭源模型；在机器人控制方面，原子动作监督结合分层执行在 LIBERO-Long、LIBERO-Plus 与 RoboTwin 上相较任务级基线提升任务成功率。

Conclusion: 统一的原子动作标注可有效支撑技能级机器人学习，作者将开源数据、基准、模型与构建流程以推动后续研究。

Abstract: Fine-grained action annotations can support skill reuse and compositional generalization in robot policies. However, robot datasets typically provide task-level instructions without subtask annotations. Although some include subtask labels, inconsistent formats and conventions hinder cross-source joint training. We propose RoboAtom-Data, which adds unified, time-aligned atomic-action annotations to existing data from multiple sources, covering 1.45 million trajectories, 6.88 million skill-labeled segments, and 16,882.1 hours. Our pipeline combines a unified protocol, large-scale manual annotation, propagation across repeated executions with the same ordered skill sequence, and multi-stage quality control. We evaluate the dataset through automatic atomic-action annotation and robot control. We construct RoboAtom-Bench to standardize evaluation of skill recognition and temporal boundary localization. Trained on the annotated data, RoboAtom-Anno achieves better automatic annotation performance than all evaluated open- and closed-source models on both RoboAtom-Bench and the external WGO-Bench. For robot control, atomic-action supervision paired with hierarchical execution improves task success over task-level baselines on LIBERO-Long, LIBERO-Plus, and RoboTwin. We will release the data, benchmark, model, and construction pipeline to support further research on skill-level robot learning.

</details>


### [89] [AcrossDEX 1.0: From Human Motion Pretraining to Bimanual Dexterous Manipulation across Hand Embodiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40210)
*Xiaoyu Yang,Nan Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AcrossDEX 1.0 是一个连接人类动作预训练、仿真专家学习、机器人示教采集与视觉闭环执行的双手灵巧操作学习系统，可在 Inspire（六通道）与 Allegro（十六关节）两种手部本体上共享腕部表征、分离手指场，并以直接回归/扩散/流匹配三种策略头进行闭环控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双手灵巧操作需要腕部运动、手指接触与物体运动之间的持续协调，而人类视频蕴含丰富的操作经验；但要把这些经验用于机器人控制，必须解决人手姿态与机器人指令的对齐，以及不同本体在运动学与接触动力学上的差异问题。

Method: 先在 HOT3D Aria 数据上做预训练：用五帧图像与前一秒的三十维手部状态标注，预测未来一秒的双手动作。迁移到机器人时保留视觉编码器、时序骨干与腕部参数，采用共享腕部表征、各手独立的手指场和有效性掩码，适配六通道 Inspire 手与十六关节 Allegro 手。机器人策略从正面图像、指令历史与任务条件预测六十个控制目标，执行动作前缀并从新观测重新规划。实验在开盖并复位、闭合盒盖下的物体重定向两类任务上比较直接回归、扩散与流匹配。

Result: 直接回归在开盖并复位任务上成功率为 40.6%，Inspire 重定向为 100.0%，Allegro 重定向为 93.3%；流匹配在 Allegro 重定向达到 90.0%。配对对比表明，用上一版规划约束重规划可提升生成式策略的连续执行表现。

Conclusion: 结果验证了同一套面向两种手部本体的通用适配流程可行，并说明接触协调与动作块连续性在双手灵巧操作中的关键作用。

Abstract: Bimanual dexterous manipulation requires continuous coordination of wrist motion, finger contact, and object motion. Human videos provide rich manipulation experience. Using this experience for robot control requires aligning human poses with robot commands and accounting for embodiment-specific kinematics and contact dynamics. We present AcrossDEX 1.0, a learning system that connects human motion pretraining, simulation-based expert learning, robot demonstration collection, and visual closed-loop execution. Pretraining on HOT3D Aria predicts one second of bimanual motion from five images and thirty annotated hand states over the preceding second. Robot adaptation preserves the visual encoder, temporal backbone, and wrist parameters. Shared wrist representations, separate finger fields, and validity masks support both six-channel Inspire hands and sixteen-joint Allegro hands. The robot policy predicts sixty control targets from frontal images, command history, and task conditions. It executes a prefix and replans from updated observations. We compare direct regression, diffusion, and flow matching on lid opening and return and closed-lid object reorientation. Direct regression achieves success rates of 40.6\% for opening and return, 100.0\% for Inspire reorientation, and 93.3\% for Allegro reorientation. Flow matching achieves 90.0\% on Allegro reorientation. Paired comparisons further show that constraining replanning with the previous plan improves the continuous execution of generative policies. These results demonstrate a common adaptation pipeline for two hand embodiments and highlight the roles of contact coordination and action-chunk continuity.

</details>


### [90] [LLM-guided Planar Loco-Manipulation for Quadruped Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40248)
*Georges Jetti,Michael Khayyat,Jean-Pierre Sleiman,Stefano Arrigoni,Francesco Braghin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文针对杂乱环境中需通过物理交互才能到达目标的机器人导航问题，提出语言条件的分层任务规划器（LoRA微调），联合选择技能、物体与几何目标，并配合端到端全身平面推挤策略，在仿真与硬件上均优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统导航假设存在无碰撞路径，但在含可移动障碍的杂乱环境中，到达目标往往需要主动的物理交互。经典方法依赖几何规则与任务特定逻辑，难以泛化；学习类方法则常把决策空间限制在低维运动指令或预定义动作集上，缺乏对"与哪个物体、以何种技能、作用于哪个几何目标"的联合推理能力。

Method: 高层：语言条件的任务规划器，在合成数据上用LoRA微调，输入自然语言任务与结构化场景表示，输出变长的参数化动作序列（联合决定技能、相关物体与几何目标），由底层强化学习策略执行。低层：提出端到端的全身平面推挤策略用于四足机器人。对比基线包括经典规划器、提示式前沿模型以及从零训练的模型。

Result: 规划器在交互式导航任务上优于经典规划器与提示式前沿模型；与从零训练模型的对比表明，在结构化表示下组合能力不需要预训练，而预训练能带来更可靠的几何目标。低层在仿真中达到93.3%成功率（分层基线83.5%），完成时间减少60%以上；硬件上45次试验中44次产生正确的技能组合，端到端成功34/45。

Conclusion: 扩展决策空间至技能—物体—几何目标的联合选择，配合结构化表示与语言条件下的轻量微调，可有效实现交互式导航；预训练的价值主要体现在几何目标预测的可靠性而非任务组合本身；端到端全身推挤策略显著提升效率与成功率，并在真实四足平台上验证了可行性。

Abstract: Robotic navigation typically assumes a collision-free route to the goal exists; in cluttered environments with movable obstacles, however, reaching the goal may require physical interaction. Classical solutions encode these behaviors through geometric rules and task-specific logic, while learning-based approaches often constrain the decision space to low-dimensional motion commands or predefined action sets. We instead consider a broader decision space, jointly selecting the skill, relevant object, and geometric target. Our language-conditioned task planner, fine-tuned with LoRA on synthetic data, maps natural-language tasks and a structured scene representation to variable-length sequences of parameterized actions, executed by low-level reinforcement-learning policies. The planner learns the geometric relations underlying interactive navigation and outperforms a classical planner and a prompted frontier model on interactive navigation tasks. Comparisons with models trained from scratch indicate that composition does not require pretraining when the representation is structured, whereas pretraining yields more reliable geometric targets. At the low level, we introduce an end-to-end whole-body planar pushing policy that achieves a 93.3% success rate in simulation, versus 83.5% for a hierarchical baseline, while reducing completion time by more than 60%. On hardware, the hierarchy yields the correct skill composition in 44/45 trials and end-to-end success in 34/45. Project website: https://anonymousprojects-dev.github.io/LLM-guided-LocoManipulation-Quadrupeds/

</details>


### [91] [Gaussian Distillation of Flow Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40271)
*Youssef Saied,Ziyad Sheebaelhamd,Claire Vernade,François Fleuret*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出高斯动作读出（GAR），仅用0.02%状态训练替换流策略最后一层，单次前向采样，速度提升至2倍且成功率接近教师模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流策略（flow policies）在机器人控制中表现强、能建模多峰动作分布，但推理需要多步积分，速度慢，且难以获得闭式似然。作者发现其连续动作分布实际上可被高斯很好地近似，因此希望在不重新训练整个网络的前提下获得快速、可解析的推理。

Method: 先分析流策略输出的动作分布，验证其可用高斯近似；据此提出GAR：冻结流策略其余网络，仅将最后一层替换为高斯读出层，并在极少量（0.02%的可用状态）数据上训练该读出层；推理时只需一次动作网络前向即可采样，并直接给出闭式似然。

Result: GAR 仅需一次网络前向采样，提供闭式似然，推理速度最高达原流策略的 2 倍，同时在任务成功率上接近原教师策略（near-teacher）。

Conclusion: 流策略的动作分布常近似高斯，因此无需完整多步流采样即可保持性能；用极少量状态训练轻量高斯读出层是一种高效、低成本的加速与似然化方案。

Abstract: Flow policies are among the strongest robot controllers and are well suited to modelling multimodal action distributions. Our analysis shows that the continuous-action distributions of the studied flow policies are well approximated by Gaussians. We propose the Gaussian Action Readout (GAR), which replaces a flow policy's final layer with a Gaussian readout trained on just 0.02% of eligible states, while keeping the rest of the network frozen. It samples with one action-network pass, gives closed-form likelihoods, and achieves near-teacher task success at up to twice the inference speed.

</details>


### [92] [MUSBRIC: LEGO-Compatible Pneumatic Muscle Bricks for Reconfigurable Musculoskeletal Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40285)
*Qiyao Wang,Yinglei Zhu,Liang Zhou,Wenzhe Gong,Xin-Jun Liu,Huichan Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出MUSBRIC——一个模块化硬件平台及其配套MuJoCo仿真器，使肌肉骨骼机器人可重复使用与快速重构：LEGO兼容端盖把纤维增强气动人工肌肉变为即插即用的"肌肉砖"，通过肘关节、机械手、15种腿部配置扫描及四足行走等演示，验证了机构分析、构型探索与功能构建的一体化设计流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 肌肉骨骼机器人结合柔性驱动器与关节骨架，但探索不同构型通常需要反复制作实物，成本高、周期长，缺乏可复用、可重构的硬件与仿真联合平台。

Method: 设计模块化硬件平台：LEGO兼容端盖将纤维增强可伸长气动人工肌肉封装为可复用的即插即用肌肉砖，直接连接标准LEGO件；通过实验辨识驱动器特性并建立对应的MuJoCo肌肉骨骼仿真；构建肘关节样机（滑轮导向实现可逆屈伸、机械限位稳定伸直姿态）、机器人手（柔性抓取、多关节构建）；在硬件与仿真中匹配扫描15种腿部构型，并组装四足演示行走。

Result: 肘关节样机实现随载荷变化的屈伸，验证滑轮导向使运动可逆、机械限位稳定伸直姿态；机器人手实现柔性抓取与多关节结构；15种腿部构型的硬件-仿真匹配扫描实现附着几何的系统性探索；四足机器人完成行走演示；覆盖机构分析、构型探索与功能机器人构建。

Conclusion: MUSBRIC以单一可重构系统贯通机构分析、构型探索与功能构建，降低肌肉骨骼机器人的制作与迭代门槛；硬件设计与仿真代码将开源，便于复现与后续开发。

Abstract: Musculoskeletal robots combine compliant actuators with articulated skeletons, but exploring alternative configurations often requires repeated fabrication. We present MUSBRIC, a modular hardware platform and accompanying MuJoCo-based simulator for reconfigurable musculoskeletal robots. LEGO-compatible endcaps turn fiber-reinforced extensile pneumatic artificial muscles into reusable, plug-and-play muscle bricks that connect directly to standard LEGO components. Experimentally identified actuator behavior supports corresponding musculoskeletal simulations. An elbow-joint prototype validates load-dependent flexion and extension, showing that pulley-guided routing enables reversible motion and a mechanical stop stabilizes the straight posture. A robotic hand demonstrates compliant grasping and multi-joint construction. Matched hardware and simulation sweeps across 15 leg configurations demonstrate systematic exploration of attachment geometry, while a quadruped assembled from four legs demonstrates walking. Together, these demonstrations span mechanism analysis, configuration exploration, and functional robot construction within a single reconfigurable system. The hardware designs and simulation code will be released as open source soon to facilitate reproduction and further development.

</details>


### [93] [TouchBridge: Tactile-Conditioned Human-to-Robot Transfer for Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40349)
*Haozhe Zhang,Gu Zhang,Zirui Zou,Yuzheng Chen,Jinlong Wu,Zixuan Hu,Jianke Zhang,Yucheng Hu,Zhecheng Yuan,Huazhe Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出硬件-软件协同设计的TouchBridge框架，通过触觉条件化的Tac-Retarget方法将人类演示直接转化为机器人训练数据，无需物体网格、位姿估计或物理仿真，在四个真实接触密集任务上以更快的采集速度达到接近遥操作数据的策略性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类演示是灵巧机器人操作的高效、可扩展数据来源，但传感与手部形态的差异使接触密集交互的跨具身迁移困难。现有基于物体感知或仿真的迁移流程依赖物体网格、位姿估计和物理仿真，成本高、难以扩展。

Method: 设计可穿戴人类演示接口与对应的机器人接口，在视觉与指尖触觉传感上建立跨具身对应关系；提出Tac-Retarget触觉条件化重定向方法，利用实测接触对齐指尖接触斑块并保持其在手内与手间的空间关系；结合视觉合成与相机锚定的动作转换，生成机器人域演示用于微调预训练VLA模型。

Result: 在四个真实接触密集任务上，仅用TouchBridge转换的人类演示微调的策略平均成功率达72.5%，使用等量遥操作机器人演示为77.5%，而人类演示采集速度快7.0倍；在相同策略与训练设置下，Tac-Retarget分别比DexPilot和UniDex高出25.0和42.5个百分点。

Conclusion: TouchBridge表明，借助触觉传感的跨具身对应，可在不依赖物体网格、位姿估计与物理仿真的情况下把人类演示规模化转化为机器人训练数据，性能接近遥操作数据且采集效率显著更高，显著优于现有重定向方法。

Abstract: Human demonstrations offer an efficient and scalable source of data for dexterous robot manipulation, but differences in sensing and hand morphology complicate the transfer of contact-rich interactions. We present TouchBridge, a hardware--software co-designed framework for human-to-robot data transformation. A wearable human demonstration interface and a corresponding robot interface establish cross-embodiment correspondence in visual and fingertip tactile sensing. We introduce Tac-Retarget, a tactile-conditioned retargeting method that uses measured contact to align fingertip contact patches and preserve their spatial relations within and across hands. Unlike object-aware or simulation-based transfer pipelines, Tac-Retarget requires no object mesh, object-pose estimate, or physics simulation for retargeting, enabling direct and scalable conversion of human demonstrations into robot-domain training data. Combined with visual synthesis and camera-anchored action conversion, TouchBridge produces robot-domain demonstrations for fine-tuning a pretrained vision-language-action (VLA) model. Across four real-world contact-rich tasks, policies fine-tuned solely on TouchBridge-converted human demonstrations achieve a 72.5% average success rate, compared with 77.5% using an equal number of teleoperated robot demonstrations, while human demonstration collection is 7.0x faster. Under the same policy and training setup, Tac-Retarget outperforms DexPilot and UniDex by 25.0 and 42.5 percentage points, respectively.

</details>


### [94] [JEDI: Joint-Embedding Demonstration Compression for In-Context Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40356)
*Jiazhao Liang,Zhewen He,Shuaihang Yuan,Hao Huang,Yi Fang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: JEDI 将演示压缩为隐变量槽，在冻结的视频-JEPA 空间中以预测查询回合下一阶段关键帧为目标训练，从而在上下文中容纳更多演示，并保留执行细节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 上下文模仿学习（ICIL）依赖测试时演示来适配机器人策略，但长且多模态的轨迹占用大量上下文，限制了可放入的示例数量。论文提出核心问题：压缩后的演示应当保留什么信息？

Method: 提出 JEDI：把演示压缩为隐变量槽（latent slots），训练目标是预测查询回合的下一阶段关键帧，预测在冻结的视频-JEPA 空间中进行；引入任务反事实与过程反事实使槽保留执行细节；用一个预测器从演示库中选择演示；最后用关键点 token 做后训练，实现人手条件对机器人的迁移。

Result: 在 LIBERO 与真实机器人上的实验表明 JEDI 支持 many-shot 上下文；LIBERO 的目标函数消融实验显示，相比仅动作压缩，成功率与演示跟随度均有提升。

Conclusion: 结果支持将"查询-未来预测"作为训练目标，用于在压缩演示中保留与执行相关的关键信息。

Abstract: In-context imitation learning (ICIL) adapts robot policies through test-time demonstrations, but long multimodal trajectories constrain how many examples fit in context. We ask what a compressed demonstration should preserve. We propose JEDI, which compresses demonstrations into latent slots trained to predict the query episode's next-stage keyframe in a frozen video-JEPA space. Task and process counterfactuals help the slots preserve execution details, while the predictor selects demonstrations from a bank. Post-training with keypoint tokens enables human-hand conditioning on robots. Experiments on LIBERO and real robots show that JEDI supports many-shot context; LIBERO objective controls show improved success and demonstration following over action-only compression. These findings support query-future prediction as a training objective for preserving execution-relevant information in compressed demonstrations.

</details>


### [95] [The Athlete as a Scientist of One: Agentic Bayesian Experimental Design for Individualized Physiology from Consumer Wearables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40370)
*Behnam Moradi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出 EpiCoach——一个把单个运动员（越野山地车手）作为可问责 N-of-1 科学研究对象的智能体系统，通过"证据门控假设注册表"+机制性数字孪生+约束实验式训练周处方，实现可审计、可被证伪的个性化健康建模，并用18个月数据做回顾回放与前瞻性验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有面向个人健康的 LLM 智能体只是读取可穿戴数据而后给出建议，却并不以可审计或被证伪的方式"学习"这个个体；个性化缺乏科学问责性，因此需要把 N-of-1 个体研究做成可记录、可检验的闭环。

Method: 1) 建立证据门控的假设注册表：关于该运动员的每条断言都必须在其发生前对未来训练课发出概率预测，信念只能经由有记录的统计拟合所计算的证据而更新。2) 由心率、GPS 与气压高度推断机制性数字孪生：滞后心率响应模型、带可学习时间常数的体能-疲劳模型、以及在自动发现的路段上测得的隐变量"技术下坡"技能状态。3) 智能体在负荷与安全约束下，以最大化信息增益为目标、围绕两次"仪器化"骑行设计下一训练周作为受控实验。4) 评估：18个月数据上的滚动起点回放基准 + 真实前瞻阶段（按周运行循环）。

Result: 回放基准显示：孪生模型在心率预测上优于标准预测器，但在路段用时上并不占优；智能体闭环与静态模型持平。前瞻阶段前三周注册表封存20条预测、评分14条：下坡预测在一次校正后校准良好，爬坡预测因运动员进步而偏悲观，安全官两次否决智能体提案。所有更正均被保留在注册表中。

Conclusion: EpiCoach 给出了数字健康中可审计、可证伪的个性化模板：预测先于事件、信念更新须由计算证据驱动、修正永不被删除；并开源注册表、基准与协议。同时也诚实地表明机制孪生与智能体闭环并非在所有任务上都优于简单基线。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents for personal health read wearable data and recommend, but do not learn about the individual in a way that can be audited or falsified. We present EpiCoach, an agentic system that treats one athlete, a cross-country mountain biker, as the subject of accountable N-of-1 science. Claims about the athlete live in an evidence-gated hypothesis registry: each must issue a probabilistic prediction about a future session before it occurs, and its belief moves only through computed evidence from a logged statistical fit. Predictions come from a mechanistic digital twin inferred from heart rate, GPS and barometric altitude: a lagged heart-rate response model, a fitness-fatigue model with learned time constants, and a latent technical-descending skill state, measured on discovered trail segments. The agent prescribes the next training week as a constrained experiment maximizing information gain subject to load and safety constraints, around two instrument rides. We evaluate the system two ways over eighteen months of data. A rolling-origin replay benchmark shows the twin beats standard forecasters on heart rate but not segment time, and the agentic loop matches a static model. A live prospective phase runs the weekly cycle: in its first three weeks the ledger sealed 20 predictions, scored 14, the descent forecast calibrated after one correction, the climb forecast pessimistic as the athlete improved, and the safety officer vetoed the agent twice. Every correction is preserved in the registry, a template for auditable, falsifiable personalization in digital health. We release the registry, benchmark and protocol.

</details>


### [96] [Securing the Robotics Supply Chain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40385)
*Cindy L. Bethel,Henrik I. Christensen,Odest Chadwicke Jenkins,George Pappas,Holly Yanco,Haley Griffin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文主张美国应制定一项为期十年、持续性的联邦机器人与硬件研究投资议程，以弥补当前在验证认证方法、基础硬件研究、规模化测试基础设施和人才队伍四方面的缺口，并强调机器人学不应仅被视为人工智能投资的下游受益者，而应作为独立供应链获得专门的联邦投入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前联邦投入与机器人领域实际需求之间存在显著缺口，且该领域长期被视为AI战略的附属品；硬件研究相对软件长期资金不足，验证与认证方法、规模化物理测试设施及人才储备均存在明显空缺，需要通过跨预算周期的长期承诺来填补。

Method: 政策分析与议程设计：识别缺口最突出的四个领域（不确定来源组件的验证与认证方法、资金不足的基础硬件研究、规模化系统测试所需的物理基础设施、支撑全链条的人才队伍），并提出以十年为周期的持续性联邦研究与投资框架。

Result: 明确了缺口的具体分布，即验证认证方法学、基础硬件研究、物理测试基础设施与人才队伍四个方向；并论证机器人学具备自身独立供应链与独特失效点，无法通过泛化的AI投资策略间接覆盖。

Conclusion: 应将机器人学作为独立的战略投资对象，制定持续十年的联邦研究投资议程，配套专项资源支持硬件基础研究、验证认证、测试设施与人才培养，而非将其简单归入更广泛的AI投资框架之下。

Abstract: This paper sets out what a sustained federal research and investment agenda would need to include to close that gap--not within a single budget cycle, but over the decade such an undertaking will realistically require. It identifies where the gap is most pronounced today: in the methods needed to verify and certify components of uncertain origin, in the foundational hardware research that has long been underfunded relative to software, in the physical infrastructure researchers need to test systems at scale, and in the workforce required to build and sustain all of it. Robotics should not be treated merely as a downstream beneficiary of a broader artificial intelligence investment strategy. It constitutes its own supply chain, with its own points of failure, and merits its own sustained federal commitment.

</details>


### [97] [Continuous-Action World Models: Learning Cross-Embodiment Spatiotemporal Dynamics Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40401)
*Liangyu Li,Qingwen Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CoAWM 提出一个跨本体、跨执行频率的连续动作世界模型框架：先通过函数预训练把不同本体、不同采样频率的轨迹映射到共享潜函数空间学习时空动力学先验，再将潜函数空间与世界模型主干连接，由观测、语言指令和当前机器人状态条件化预测潜动作函数，单一解码器可在任意查询时间输出末端执行器动作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人策略难以在异构本体与不同控制频率之间迁移，且不同来源轨迹采样频率不一致，限制了可预训练数据的规模与多样性。作者希望将共享动力学先验与任务条件化动作选择、时间采样解耦，从而获得跨本体、跨频率的统一基础。

Method: 两阶段学习：(1) 函数预训练——通过动作重建与不同时间采样视图间的一致性约束，把不同本体、不同采样频率的轨迹统一为共同表示，学习共享动力学先验，并借此扩大预训练数据来源；(2) 将预训练的函数空间连接到世界模型主干，以观测、语言指令与当前机器人状态为条件预测潜动作函数，单一解码器接受任意查询时间并返回对应末端执行器动作。

Result: 摘要未给出定量实验结果；声称的成效包括：能够容纳异构机器人轨迹以拓宽预训练数据来源并便于扩展到更大、更多样的数据集，以及通过分离共享动力学先验、任务条件化动作选择与时间采样，为跨本体、跨控制频率的机器人经验迁移提供统一基础。

Conclusion: 连续动作的潜函数空间表示是连接异构本体与异构控制频率的可行桥梁，将世界模型式预训练与时间连续的动作解码相结合，有望提升机器人经验迁移与数据规模化的能力。

Abstract: We introduce Continuous-Action World Models (CoAWM), a framework for learning cross-embodiment spatiotemporal dynamics priors that support robot action generation across embodiments and execution frequencies. At its core is a shared latent function space that captures the spatial and temporal structure of robot actions. We learn this space through two stages. First, function pretraining brings trajectories from different embodiments and sampling frequencies into a common representation, learning shared dynamics priors through action reconstruction and consistency across temporal sampling views. By accommodating heterogeneous robot trajectories, this stage broadens the range of data sources available for pretraining and facilitates scaling to larger and more diverse datasets. Second, we connect the pretrained function space to a world-model backbone, enabling observations, language instructions, and the current robot state to condition the prediction of a latent action function. A single decoder takes any query time and returns the corresponding end-effector action. By separating shared dynamics priors from task-conditioned action selection and temporal sampling, our approach provides a unified foundation for transferring robot experience across embodiments and control frequencies.

</details>


### [98] [Optimal Path Planning with Localization constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40429)
*Chinmay Mundane,Manav Mishra,PB Sujit*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种定位约束A*规划器，在无GPS环境下把定位不确定性作为硬约束进行无人机路径规划，保证最优性并优于RRT*与PRM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机在无GPS区域易丢失定位，需依赖航位推算并偶尔靠近蜂窝基站作为地标重置位置，因此需要在保证定位不确定性不超过给定上界的前提下，寻找最短起点—目标路径。

Method: 将不确定性作为搜索过程中的硬约束，构建"定位约束A*"规划器；使用扩展卡尔曼滤波（EKF）传播机器人状态与协方差，仅保留满足协方差上限的路径；并证明该方法不破坏A*启发式性质，启发式仍具可采纳性与一致性。

Result: 蒙特卡洛仿真与PyBullet四旋翼实验表明，相比RRT*与PRM，该方法找到的路径更短、更可靠，且始终满足不确定性约束。

Conclusion: 通过在A*搜索中显式引入EKF传播的定位不确定性硬约束，可在保持最优性理论保证的同时，为无GPS环境的无人机提供更优且可靠的路径规划方案。

Abstract: This paper studies UAV path planning in areas where GPS signals are not present. Because these drones can easily get lost when they don't have GPS, they must rely on dead reckoning and occasionally pass near cellular towers, which act as landmarks to reset and fix their position. The goal is to find the shortest path from a start to a goal location while keeping the estimation uncertainty within a given bound. To solve this problem, we propose a localization-constrained A* planner that treats uncertainty as a hard constraint during search. The robot state and covariance are propagated using an Extended Kalman Filter (EKF), and only paths that satisfy the covariance limit are considered. We also show that the proposed method does not affect heuristic used in A* and remains admissible and consistent, ensuring optimality among all feasible paths. Extensive Monte Carlo simulations and PyBullet quadrotor test show that the proposed method finds shorter and more reliable paths compared to RRT* and PRM, while always satisfying the uncertainty constraint.

</details>


### [99] [Improving Fine-Grained Manipulation through Demonstration Expansion and Execution Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40430)
*Jingxu Gu,Dongze Lian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DEED 通过结构化演示扩展与执行设计，将预训练视觉-语言-动作策略适配到 MetaFine 的五个细粒度操作任务，官方总体得分 85.55%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 细粒度操作要求机器人策略同时具备指令跟随、视觉鲁棒性和精确控制能力，而预训练 VLA 策略直接迁移到新任务时通常面临演示数据不足、视觉条件单一、动作难以落地为精确控制等问题。

Method: 方法分两部分：一是演示扩展，在同一初始场景下为不同指令生成演示，并在保持状态与动作目标不变的前提下，将每条物理轨迹在不同相机位置和光照条件下渲染；训练时采用分层采样策略，在指令、轨迹与视觉条件之间取得平衡进行监督式适配。二是执行设计，部署时通过逆运动学、按任务选择的夹爪映射将预测动作块转换为机器人指令，并采用闭环重规划。

Result: 官方 Overall 得分为 85.55%。固定策略评估表明，性能随执行时域、去噪预算和夹爪映射的变化而变化。

Conclusion: 演示扩展与执行设计相结合可有效提升预训练 VLA 策略在细粒度任务上的适配效果，且执行端超参数（执行时域、去噪预算、夹爪映射）对最终性能有显著影响，需要针对性调优。

Abstract: Fine-grained manipulation requires a robot policy to follow instructions, remain robust to visual variations, and translate predicted actions into precise robot control. We present Demonstration Expansion and Execution Design (DEED), a framework for adapting a pretrained vision-language-action policy to the five MetaFine tasks through structured demonstration expansion and execution design. DEED generates demonstrations for different instructions from the same initial scene and renders each physical trajectory under different camera positions and lighting conditions while preserving its original states and action targets. A hierarchical sampling strategy balances instructions, trajectories, and visual conditions during supervised adaptation. At deployment, DEED converts predicted action chunks into robot commands using inverse kinematics, gripper mappings selected for each task, and closed-loop replanning. DEED achieves an official Overall score of 85.55%. Fixed-policy evaluations show that performance varies with execution horizon, denoising budget, and gripper mapping.

</details>


### [100] [Adaptive search with path-constrained mobile sensors using multi-arm bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40431)
*Abraham P. Vinod,Parth K. Thaker,Gautam Dasarathy,Stefano Di Cairano*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文研究能量受限移动传感器团队在噪声在线测量下的区域检测问题，将其建模为多臂老虎机主动路径选择问题，提出迭代搜索算法与基于图的低基数路径覆盖构造方法，并给出有限时间与任意时刻的概率正确性保证及与非算法相关下界匹配的界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动传感器团队能量受限、需返回充电站并规避已知障碍，可用路径集预先给定且有限，同时在线测量含噪声；因此需要在严格约束下自适应地选择路径，以尽快、可靠地发现所有感兴趣区域。

Method: 将主动路径选择建模为多臂老虎机问题，利用已采集数据计算置信界进行自适应路径分配，形成迭代搜索算法；同时提出基于图的方法，在团队约束下构造低基数路径覆盖。

Result: 建立了算法的有限时间与任意时刻概率正确性保证，推导出检测所有感兴趣区域与标注所有节点所需时间的非渐近上界，并证明在广泛问题实例类上这些上界与算法无关下界在常数因子内匹配；数值仿真展示了算法行为以及路径冗余与团队规模对搜索性能的影响。

Conclusion: 所提方法在满足能量、充电返回与障碍规避等约束的前提下，实现了带理论保证的高效自适应区域检测，路径覆盖构造与冗余、团队规模的权衡对实际部署具有指导意义。

Abstract: We study a constrained sequential decision problem for detecting regions of interest from noisy online measurements collected by a team of energy-constrained mobile sensors. The sensor motion is restricted to a precomputed set of feasible paths that satisfy mobility constraints, limited available energy by requiring return to charging stations, and avoidance of known obstacles. We cast the resulting active path selection as a multi-armed bandit problem and propose an iterative search algorithm that adaptively assigns paths using confidence bounds computed from the collected data. We also propose a graph-based method to construct low-cardinality path covers under the team's constraints. We establish finite-time and anytime probabilistic correctness guarantees for the proposed algorithm, derive non-asymptotic upper bounds on the times required to detect all regions of interest and to label all nodes, and show that these bounds match algorithm-independent lower bounds up to constant factors for a broad class of problem instances. Using numerical simulations, we illustrate the behavior of the proposed approach and the effect of path redundancy and team size on search performance.

</details>


### [101] [A Two-Wheeled Quadrotor with All-Clockwise Tilted Rotors for Level-Attitude Surface Locomotnion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40502)
*Takumi Ito,Satoshi Nakano*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种四旋翼全部同向（顺时针）倾斜旋翼的轮式四旋翼，利用表面接触约束在不倾斜机身的情况下产生切向推力，实现空中与地面接触运动的统一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统轮式四旋翼必须倾斜机体才能产生横向力，导致侧向运动时难以保持期望姿态。作者希望在不增加额外旋翼倾转执行器或机构的前提下，仅依靠接触面约束与旋翼布局设计获得沿接触面方向的推力，同时保持机身水平。

Method: 分析表面接触条件下的可行力集合与系统可控性；针对不同旋翼自旋配置对机体设计进行优化，比较全部顺时针旋转与常规交替旋转配置所能产生的切向力大小；并通过仿真验证自由空间悬停与地面水平运动。

Result: 优化结果表明，四旋翼全部顺时针旋转的配置比常规交替旋转配置能产生更大的切向力；仿真验证了自由空间悬停以及在地面上保持水平姿态的水平移动，且无需额外旋翼倾转执行器或机构。

Conclusion: 通过全同向倾斜旋翼与接触约束的结合，可在不倾斜机身的情况下实现地面切向运动，兼顾空中与接触式两种运动模式，为轮式四旋翼设计提供了新思路。

Abstract: Conventional wheel-equipped quadrotor UAVs must tilt their bodies to generate lateral forces, limiting their ability to maintain a desired attitude during lateral motion. This paper proposes a wheeled quadrotor with all-clockwise tilted rotors that uses surface contact as a constraint to generate propulsion along the contact surface without tilting the vehicle. We analyze feasible forces and system controllability under surface contact and optimize the vehicle design considering different rotor-spin configurations. The optimization results show that a configuration in which all four rotors spin clockwise can generate a larger tangential force than the conventional alternating-spin configuration. This approach enables both aerial and contact-based locomotion without additional rotor-tilting actuators or mechanisms. Simulation results demonstrate both hovering in free space and horizontal locomotion on a floor while maintaining a level attitude.

</details>


### [102] [BURGER: Benchmarking and Understanding Robotic Generalization via Embodied Drawing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40532)
*Xiangyu Xu,Bangshuai Peng,Ziyi Liu,Mengjie Zhao,Chenhao Lu,Zhengyang Fan,Huazhe Xu,Kaizhe Hu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 BURGER，一个语言条件机器人绘画基准与可扩展的提示驱动轨迹生成流水线，用 28,200 条仿真演示（282 个绘画任务）系统评测五个代表性 VLA 模型在数据规模、语言接地与多模态行为上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究"VLA 模型能否随训练数据规模有效学习多样的语言条件行为"需要大规模多样化的机器人数据集，而在真实机器人上采集成本高昂，因此需要一个可扩展、可低成本生成大规模数据的评测基准。

Method: 构建 BURGER 基准：以绘画为任务形式，智能体从同一张空白画布出发，控制机械臂按语言指令绘制指定物体或空间构图，每条指令允许多种有效画法与笔画顺序；采用提示驱动的流水线自动生成连续绘画轨迹，覆盖仿真与真实世界；在 28,200 条仿真演示、282 个绘画任务上评测五个代表性 VLA 模型，并分析数据规模、语言接地与多模态行为；最后在 Franka Panda 机械臂上部署训练策略。

Result: BURGER 支持对语言接地、空间构图与动作生成的细粒度分析，揭示不同 VLA 模型各具特点的失败模式；实验表明部分 VLA 模型可从更大的任务多样性与演示规模中获益，能够捕获多模态绘画行为，并能泛化到熟悉物体的未见组合；部署后的策略可在人类干预下进行反应式绘画。

Conclusion: 绘画任务为研究 VLA 的语言接地、多模态性与数据缩放提供了一种低成本、可扩展且开放式答案的评测载体；BURGER 可作为诊断 VLA 失败模式的基准，并支持从仿真到真实机器人的迁移验证。

Abstract: How effectively can vision-language-action (VLA) models learn diverse language-conditioned behaviors as training data scales? Investigating this question requires large and varied robot datasets, whose collection on physical robots remains costly. We introduce BURGER, a language-conditioned robotic drawing benchmark with a scalable, prompt-driven pipeline for generating continuous drawing trajectories, both in simulation and the real world. In BURGER, an agent starts from the same blank canvas and controls a robot arm to draw the object or spatial composition specified by language, while each instruction allows an open-ended variety of valid drawings and stroke orders. Using 28,200 simulated demonstrations spanning 282 drawing tasks, we evaluate five representative VLA models across data scales, language grounding, and multimodal behavior. BURGER supports fine-grained analysis of language grounding, spatial composition, and action generation, revealing distinct failure patterns across VLA models. Our experiments suggest that some VLA models can benefit from greater task diversity and demonstration scale, capture multimodal drawing behaviors, and generalize to unseen compositions of familiar objects. Finally, we deploy a trained policy on a Franka Panda arm, demonstrating the ability to draw reactively under human intervention. Project homepage and code can be found at https://siyingzhao.github.io/BURGER/.

</details>


### [103] [DAWN: Diffusion-based Alignment and unfastening Workflow Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40537)
*Hemant Kumar,Piyush Goenka,Nikhil Chopra*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出DAWN分层机器人框架，用扩散策略与多模态分类器完成真实世界的螺钉拧卸（unfastening）任务，成功率接近熟练人工操作员。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主机器人拧卸螺钉对回收拆解、安全与降本意义重大，但因螺丝刀精确对准、力控执行和环境变化鲁棒性难，目前基本靠人工，且长时程、接触密集型操作学习困难。

Method: 将任务分解为两个顺序阶段：(1) 对准与插入，(2) 拧卸。框架含三部分：AlignNet（扩散策略，保证刀尖精确啮合）、LatchNet（多模态分类器，检测阶段切换成功）、UnfastNet（实现稳定力控的螺钉拆除）。在Franka Emika机器人上验证，并做消融研究分析关键输入模态与训练策略。

Result: 对准与插入成功率与训练有素的人工操作员相当；阶段间切换稳健；在多种未见场景下拧卸成功率很高。消融表明部分输入模态与训练策略至关重要。

Conclusion: DAWN通过分层分解与多模态感知集成，实现了可扩展、鲁棒且数据高效的真实世界拧卸操作，具备良好应用前景。

Abstract: We present DAWN (Diffusion-based Alignment and unfastening Workflow Network), a hierarchical robotic framework designed to perform real-world, unfastening tasks through multi-modal sensory integration. Autonomous robotic unfastening is crucial for improving recycling processes, enhancing safety, and reducing operational costs; yet, it remains predominantly manual due to inherent challenges in precise screwdriver engagement with fasteners, detailed force execution, and robustness to environmental variability. To address the complexity of learning long-horizon, contact-rich manipulation tasks, we subdivide the unfastening task procedure into two sequential stages: (1) alignment & insertion, and (2) unfastening. DAWN consists of three key components: AlignNet, a diffusion-based policy ensuring precise screwdriver tip engagement; LatchNet, a multi-modal classifier reliably detecting successful stage transitions; and UnfastNet, which achieves stable, force-controlled screw removal. Experimental validation on a Franka Emika robot demonstrates that DAWN achieves alignment & insertion success rates comparable to trained human operators, robustly transitions between stages, and attains an impressive success rate in unfastening across various unseen scenarios. Ablation studies further elucidate critical input modalities and effective training strategies, highlighting DAWN's scalability, robustness, and data efficiency.

</details>


### [104] [PhysVLMM: Interaction-Conditioned Physical Belief Maps for Articulated Objects in Robot Scene Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40539)
*Dibyendu Ghosh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出PhysVLMM，为视觉-语言-运动地图中每个可运动部件维护物理信念（关节方向的后验分布），并提供地图更新与查询两个算法，从而回答规划器对"拉动某部件会如何响应"的问题。实验表明其关节类型识别率达86%，拉力响应预测误差比可微仿真低41%，速度快四个数量级，且给定风险阈值下误判"安全"的情况不超出该阈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动机器人需要开门、开抽屉，但现有场景地图只记录物体是什么、是否移动过，不记录它对拉力的动态响应；同时，仅凭观察部件运动在理论上无法辨识其动力学，单次测量拉力也只能确定被拉部件、且仅到一个方向，因此需要一种能表达不确定性的物理信念建模方法。

Method: 方法核心是对每个可运动部件的物理信念进行维护：（1）不将拉动方向作为点估计，而是保留为后验分布；（2）利用闭式解将每次拉动所获证据传递到地图中未被触碰但外观相似的部件；（3）支持以给定风险回答"该动作是否会成功"或选择弃答。算法包括地图更新与查询两部分。

Result: 在18个真实家庭的真实门与抽屉上：关节类型识别率达86%（单视图学习方法为48%）；拉力响应预测误差比可微仿真低41%（CRPS 0.323 对 0.544），速度快四个数量级（每例13 ms 对 230 s）；后验使最终位置误差从0.460降至0.270；证据共享使未触碰部件的力误差从56%降至42%；在0.02至0.30的各个风险水平下，误判为安全的回答均在阈值内；把同一家庭所有已测部件合并，效果与相似部件分组相当；更简单的变体在预测被拉部件停止位置上至少同样好。

Conclusion: 以概率后验形式维护物理信念并结合相似部件间的证据共享，能在实时性约束下显著优于可微仿真，并提供可信的风险可控查询；但证据共享与复杂建模带来的增益有限，简单变体在部分任务上已足够，后续可进一步权衡模型复杂度与收益。

Abstract: Mobile robots must open doors and drawers, yet their scene maps record what each object is and whether it has moved, not how it will respond to a pull. This paper proposes PhysVLMM, a method that maintains a physical belief for every articulated part of a Vision-Language-Motion Map and answers a planner's queries about it, with two algorithms: a map update and a query. Watching a part move provably cannot identify its dynamics, and a measured pull identifies only the pulled part, up to one direction. PhysVLMM therefore (i) keeps that direction as a posterior instead of a point estimate, (ii) passes the evidence of each pull in closed form to the untouched look-alike parts of the map, and (iii) answers whether an action will succeed at a stated risk, or abstains. On real doors and drawers in 18 homes, it identifies the joint type in 86% of held-out pulls (48% for a learned single-view method) and predicts the response to a pull with 41% lower error than differentiable simulation (CRPS 0.323 vs. 0.544), four orders of magnitude faster (13 ms vs. 230 s per case). The posterior lowers the final-position error from 0.460 to 0.270, sharing lowers the force error of untouched parts from 56% to 42%, and at every stated risk from 0.02 to 0.30 the false-safe answers stay within it. Pooling all measured parts of a home does as well as the look-alike groups, and simpler variants predict where a pulled part stops at least as well.

</details>


### [105] [A Tutorial on Shape Servoing of Deformable Objects-Part I: Fundamentals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40559)
*Ignacio Cuiral-Zueco,Alessio Caporali,Miguel Aranda,Gianluca Palli,Gonzalo López-Nicolás,Youcef Mezouar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 这是一篇关于可变形物体数据驱动雅可比形状控制的两部分教程的第一部分，系统介绍形状控制问题建模、形状表示、变形雅可比估计与在线更新及标准控制律，并给出闭环局部稳定性条件与不可达目标形状的原因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 变形雅可比依赖物体的力学性质、几何形状与边界条件，这些因素往往难以精确建模，因此需要一种无需精细力学模型、仅从观测到的形状变化中局部估计雅可比的数据驱动方法。同时，该领域缺少系统性的入门教程。

Method: 采用数据驱动、雅可比（形状伺服）框架：先给出形状控制问题的形式化表述与形状表示，再利用机器人运动引起的形状变化在线估计与更新变形雅可比，并结合文献中的标准控制律组装成完整的形状伺服闭环；随后推导该闭环的局部稳定性条件。

Result: 构建了完整的形状伺服控制回路，并推导出其局部稳定性条件；同时说明了某些目标形状无法到达的原因。

Conclusion: 数据驱动雅可比形状控制可在不依赖精确力学模型的情况下实现可变形物体的形状调节；本文作为两部分教程的第一部分奠定基础，第二部分将讨论更进阶的主题，全文以双臂操作弹性杆并由相机观测的平面仿真示例贯穿说明。

Abstract: In robotics, shape control (or shape servoing) refers to the automatic manipulation of a deformable object to achieve a desired shape. It involves a feedback loop that exploits the relation between robot motion and changes in the measured shape. This relation, known as the interaction Jacobian or, in this context, the deformation Jacobian, depends on the object's mechanical properties, geometry, and boundary conditions (e.g., where the object is grasped). Because these factors are often difficult to model accurately, the deformation Jacobian can be estimated locally from observed shape changes produced by commanded robot motions, without requiring a fine-tuned mechanical model. This article is the first of a two-part tutorial on data-driven Jacobian-based shape control of deformable objects. It introduces the central elements of the approach: the formulation of the shape-control problem, shape representation, estimation and online update of the deformation Jacobian, and a standard control law from the literature. It then assembles these elements into a complete shape-servoing loop, derives a local stability condition for this loop, and illustrates why some target shapes cannot be reached. Part II covers more advanced topics. Throughout both parts, a simulated planar example in which two robot arms manipulate an elastic rod observed by a camera illustrates the concepts.

</details>


### [106] [Gym-Anything for Robotics: Scaling physical simulations with digital agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40565)
*Pranjal Aggarwal,Sean Welleck*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 Gym-Anything for Robotics 框架，让编码智能体自动创建逼真的机器人仿真环境，并基于此构建包含 25 个仿真、25 个测试与 175 个训练 RL 环境的 RoboWorld 基准，同时提出多种提升前沿模型表现的学习范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逼真且快速的机器人仿真有望支撑具身智能模型的大规模训练并迁移到真实世界，但现有仿真仅覆盖狭窄的任务与物理现象。核心瓶颈在于构建更好的仿真需要大量专家投入，难以规模化。作者的关键洞察是：仿真创建本质上是一个编码智能体任务，因此编码智能体能力的提升可直接转化为更丰富、更可信的仿真。

Method: 构建 Gym-Anything for Robotics 框架，由编码智能体自动生成机器人仿真。依据美国 GDP 数据挑选仿真主题以覆盖有经济价值的实体工作；明确定义"好仿真"与"好任务"的标准；设计对抗式的"创建—审计"循环，对每个仿真与任务按这些定义进行校验。基于该流程构建 RoboWorld（25 个仿真，25 个测试环境与 175 个难度各异的训练 RL 环境）。在此基础上引入多种改进方式：多次尝试的上下文学习、读取仿真器状态的"特权教师"在上下文中教导学生、模型从自身特权经验中自辅助学习，以及在不同场景生成演示以训练 VLA 模型（如 π0.5）的代码策略。

Result: 即使最强的前沿模型在所有 RoboWorld 测试任务上均失败，平均奖励仅 0.06。作者提出的四类改进方法（重复尝试的上下文学习、特权教师、自辅助学习、代码策略生成演示训练 VLA）均带来显著性能提升。

Conclusion: 将仿真创建视为编码智能体任务可有效突破专家投入的规模化瓶颈。RoboWorld 揭示了当前前沿模型在多样化真实物理任务上的严重不足，而所提出的多种学习范式为改进提供了可行方向。作者开源了流水线、RL 环境与基准以促进后续研究。

Abstract: Realistic, fast robotics simulations could enable large-scale training of embodied AI models that transfer to the real world. However, despite decades of progress, available simulations cover only a narrow range of real-world tasks and physical phenomena. The core bottleneck is that creating better simulations requires significant expert effort and therefore does not scale. To address this, we introduce Gym-Anything for Robotics, a framework in which coding agents create realistic robotics simulations.
