Our core insight is that simulation creation is a coding agent task, so improvements in coding agents translate directly into richer and more faithful simulations. We select simulations based on U.S. GDP data to cover economically valuable physical work, define what makes a good simulation and a good task, and develop an adversarial creation-audit loop that checks each simulation and task against these definitions. Using this pipeline, we build RoboWorld, a set of 25 simulations with 25 test and 175 training RL environments of varying difficulty. Even the best frontier models fail on every RoboWorld test task, achieving an average reward of only 0.06. Building on RoboWorld, we introduce several new ways for frontier models to improve, including in-context learning across repeated attempts, a privileged teacher that reads the simulator state and teaches a student in context, self-assisted learning from the model's own privileged experience, and code policies that generate demonstrations across varied scenes to train vision-language-action models (VLAs) such as $π_{0.5}$. Each of these substantially improves performance. We release our pipeline, RL environments, and benchmark to facilitate future research. Code and data are available at https://gym-anything.github.io/robotics

</details>


### [107] [A Conveyor Belt Mechanism for Repositionable Magnetic Coupling Between Continuum Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40600)
*Ella Walsh,Eric Diller,Jessica Burgner-Kahrs*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种可沿连续体机器人本体连续主动重定位功能元件的"传送带"机构，并将其用于磁耦合多机器人系统的刚度主动调节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续体机器人的功能元件（如传感器、磁体）通常固定在机身特定位置，无法随任务需求变化而调整位置；而多机器人耦合系统的有效刚度也依赖于耦合点位置与数量，需要一种能够在机器人运行过程中主动改变功能元件空间分布的硬件手段。

Method: 设计可在腱驱动连续体机器人本体上循环运动的传送带机构，在直径9 mm的机器人内集成最多三条传送带；测量并对比传送带驱动力与机器人腱驱动力；以永磁体为案例，在两条传送带上安装磁体，实现机器人间耦合点位置与数量的在线可调，并测试耦合系统有效刚度与耦合位置、耦合点数量的关系。

Result: 传送带驱动所需的力与腱驱动力相当或更低；耦合点位置可在运行中改变，有效刚度随耦合位置呈可预测的反立方关系，并随耦合点数量增加而增大。

Conclusion: 传送带机构能够在运行过程中主动重定位功能元件以适配变化的任务需求，磁耦合多机器人系统仅为其概念验证之一；该方法为连续体机器人提供了可重构的功能分布能力。

Abstract: This work proposes a novel conveyor belt mechanism enabling continuous, active repositioning of functional elements along the body of a tendon-driven continuum robot. Up to three conveyor belt mechanisms are integrated within a 9 mm diameter continuum robot. We demonstrate equivalent or reduced conveyor belt actuation forces compared to robot tendon actuation forces. As a case study, we mount permanent magnets along two conveyor belts, and demonstrate actively reconfigurable inter-robot coupling. Coupling point location and quantity can be adjusted during robot operation to actively tune the effective stiffness of the coupled multi-robot system. We show that effective stiffness follows a predictable inverse-cubic relationship with coupling location, and increases with the number of coupling points. These findings demonstrate that the conveyor belt mechanism enables active functional element repositioning for changing tasks requirements, with magnetic inter-robot coupling as one proof-of-concept.

</details>


### [108] [SymAnt: Grounding Long-Horizon Action Anticipation with Adaptable Symbolic Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40632)
*Fnu Aryan,Yaqi Xie,Joseph Campbell,Woojun Kim,Kwonjoon Lee,Hossein Nourkhiz Mahjoub,Ehsan Moradi-Pari,Katia Sycara,Simon Stepputtis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SymAnt是一种神经-符号方法，结合场景图与知识图的联合搜索来构建符号上下文，从而在仅有短观测的情况下预测长时程动作序列，并支持用户直接编辑知识以调整预测而无需再训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人与智能系统需要仅凭短暂观测就预测长序列未来动作。纯神经网络模型通常依赖大量观测、难以解释且不易调整；符号先验则提供了结构化的替代方案。

Method: SymAnt将神经网络的表达力与场景图、知识图的结构化推理统一起来。给定短视頻，它在两个图上进行联合搜索以构建符号上下文，统一推理空间关系、物体属性、可供性和相关动作；该符号上下文作为先验，驱动一个迭代精化过程，逐步生成并改进未来动作序列。

Result: 在Breakfast、50 Salads、EPIC-KITCHENS、EGTEA Gaze+和Assembly101五个数据集上验证了有效性，尤其在仅有短观测上下文时表现突出；方法参数高效。

Conclusion: 通过将预测建立在符号上下文之上，SymAnt能以参数高效的方式从短观测窗口预测长时程动作，并允许用户直接编辑底层知识来调整预测而无需额外训练。

Abstract: As robots and intelligent systems increasingly interact with humans, they must anticipate long sequences of future actions from only brief observations. While purely neural models typically require extensive observations to predict future actions and remain difficult to interpret and adjust, symbolic priors offer a structured alternative. To this end, we introduce SymAnt, a novel neuro-symbolic approach that unifies the expressiveness of neural networks with the structured reasoning of scene and knowledge graphs. Given a short video, SymAnt performs a joint-search over both graphs to construct a symbolic context, enabling unified reasoning over spatial relationships, object attributes, affordances, and associated actions. This symbolic context serves as a prior for an iterative refinement process that progressively generates and improves the sequence of future actions. By grounding predictions in symbolic context, SymAnt anticipates long-horizon actions from short observation windows in a parameter-efficient manner, while enabling users to directly edit the underlying knowledge to adjust predictions without further training. Through our experiments, we demonstrate the effectiveness of SymAnt, particularly when only short observation contexts are available, across five diverse datasets: Breakfast, 50 Salads, EPIC-KITCHENS, EGTEA Gaze+, and Assembly101.

</details>


### [109] [SwapBot: Tool-Centric Robot Manipulation via Interchangeable End-Effectors and Tactile Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40646)
*Yunchu Zhang,Ray Song,Junhan Jiao,Liyiming Ke,Yuchen Yang,Ruihan Gao,Emma Romig,Siddhartha Srinivasa,Yiyue Luo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SwapBot 是一种模块化、以工具为中心的机器人末端执行器系统，通过低成本适配器将家用工具快速转换为机器人末端执行器，并结合触觉传感与遥操作示范采集实现接触丰富的工具操作任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 日常环境中的机器人操作大量涉及工具使用，如煎豆腐、刷酱、精准放置等任务需要在接触丰富的交互下用力切割、轻巧刷涂并精准摆放，且往往需要切换不同工具。因此，自主更换并使用任务适配的工具对工具中心式操作至关重要。作者不追求单一通用夹爪，而希望以统一设计原理覆盖多样化（含铰接式）工具并兼容多机器人平台。

Method: SwapBot 采用“机器人侧固定耦合接口 + 工具侧低成本适配器”的统一设计原则；每个适配器集成紧凑型触觉传感器，支持快速自主更换，并与直观遥操作系统对接以便高效采集示范。作者开展用户研究评估遥操作体验，并用采集的示范训练模仿学习策略；通过消融实验对比触觉增强示范与仅 RGB 策略的表现。

Result: 用户研究表明，在遥操作中 SwapBot 降低了任务完成时间与操作者工作负荷；基于示教训练的模仿学习策略成功完成煎豆腐、拌沙拉、翻肉等工具中心式任务；消融实验显示，触觉增强示范在接触丰富技能上比仅 RGB 策略提升 15 至 25 个百分点的成功率。

Conclusion: 模块化、配备触觉的工具是一种实用且可扩展的路径，有助于实现稳健且能力更强的机器人操作。

Abstract: Robot manipulation in everyday environments inherently involves substantial tool usage. Seemingly simple household tasks, such as cooking pan-fried tofu, require forceful cutting, delicate brushing, and precise placement using different tools under contact-rich interaction. Thus, the ability to autonomously swap and use task-appropriate tools is important for tool-centric manipulation. We introduce SwapBot, a modular, tool-centric end effector system featuring low-cost adapters that quickly convert common household tools into robot end effectors. Rather than pursuing a single universal gripper, SwapBot adopts a unified design principle that combines a fixed robot-side coupling interface with low-cost, tool-side adapters, enabling compatibility with diverse tools, including articulated ones, and multiple robot platforms. Each adapter integrates compact tactile sensors, supports fast autonomous swapping, and interfaces with an intuitive teleoperation system for efficient demonstration collection. User studies show that, during teleoperation, SwapBot reduces task completion time and operator workload. Using collected demonstrations on the robot, we train imitation learning policies to successfully perform tool-centric tasks like cooking tofu, stirring salad, flipping meat. Our ablation study shows that using tactile-augmented demonstrations improves policy performance across contact-rich skills, increasing success rates by 15 to 25 percentage points over RGB-only policies. These results highlight modular, tactile-equipped tools as a practical and extensible pathway toward robust and capable robot manipulation. Project website https://personalrobotics.github.io/SwapBot.

</details>
